Text:

<1>
bhūtādisūtrasya paunaruktyamuktam| taccintyameva| tāvānasya iti mantrasya brahmaparatāṃ siddhivatkṛtya tatparāmarśakayacchabdasvārasyena jyotirvākyasya brahmāparatāyā ādyasūtraprameyatvāt| tādṛśavākyasya brahmaparataivāsiddheti śaṅkāyāṃ bhutapādatvādibhirgāyatrī vākyasyāpi brahmaparatvāmityasya bhūtādisūtraprameyatayāpaunaruktyaleśasyāpyanavakāśāt| śrīmadbhāṣyaphakkikānāmuktārthatāyā atispaṣṭatayā, evamapi spaṣṭe’pi prameyabhedena paunaruktyodbhāvanaṃ vyāmohamātranibandhanaṃ dveṣanibandhanaṃ veti bhāṣyadarśinā spaṣṭameva|
kiṃ ca catuṣpadetyādyapyasat| vāgāderanyārthamuktatvena bhūtādivadamukhyatvāt| tathāhi -- bhūtamiti vākyasya hetuvannigadārthavādanyāyena gānatrāṇakartṛtvādinā stutyā sarvātmakatvopapādanāya asyā hīdaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ pratiṣṭhatam iti bhinnakakṣārūḍhavākyavattādṛśavākyabhedo gāyatryāmuktaḥ| na tu prādhānyena| ata eva yā vai sā gāyatrīyaṃ vāva sā yeyaṃ pṛthivītivat yā vai sā gāyatrīyaṃ vāva sā yeyaṃ vāk iti nauktam| anyathāgrimaparyāyasāmyāya tathaivāvakṣyat| prāṇasya tu gāyatryātmakatvaṃ viśiṣya noktamiti| na kiñcidetat|
ānandatīrthīyāstu -- catuḥ sūtryā ekādhikaraṇatvaṃ svīkṛtyaiva vi me karṇā patayato cakṣurvīdaṃ jyotirhṛdaya āhitaṃ yat ityādyāgrisūktasthaṃ vākyam| yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate iti cchāndogyasthāvākyaṃ vodāharaṇam, adhikaraṇabhedaṃ svīkṛtya jyotiścaraṇabhidhānāt iti sūtre pūrvoktamagrisūktasthaṃ vākyamudāharaṇamuttarātrisūtryāstūktacchāndogyavākyamudāharaṇamiti vā pratipādayanti| tayā guhānihitatvasyānyaniṣṭhatvaśaṅkayā nandamavādhikaraṇākṣepeṇa ca saṅgatiṃ vadanti| tathā hi -- lokato’nyatra prasiddhajyotirnāmno’tra brahmaṇi samanvayapratipādanācchastra saṅgatiḥ| yo veda nihitaṃ guhāyām ityānandamayasya hṛdayaguhānihitatvamuktam| tadguhānihitatvam vi me karṇā patayato vi cakṣurvīdam iti kasyacijjyotiṣaḥ pratīyate| tatra vākye’yamarthaḥ -- me karṇai jyotiṣā viruddhaṃ patataḥ| cakṣuśca viruddhaṃ patati| yadidaṃ hṛdaya āhitaṃ jyotistadapyatyantaṃ karṇādiviruddhaṃ vartate| me manaścātīva viraddhaṃ carati| ato’vyāptabuddhirahaṃ kiṃ nu vakṣyāmi| kiṃ vedānīṃ cintayāmīti | etacca yadi viṣṇoranyattarhyānandamayatvaṃ ca tasyaiva prasajjata iti nirṇeyaṃ bhavatīti|
tatredaṃ cintyam -- saṅgatistāvadayuktā| guhānihitatvahṛdayāhitatvayorekarūpatvābhāvenāpratyābhijñānācchāndogyavākye sutarāṃ tadabhāvācca| pratyuta yadidamantaḥ puruṣe jyotiḥ iti jāṭharajyotiṣoraikyopadeśena tadabhāvasyaiva jñāpanācca|yo’yamantarhṛdaye iti gāyatryāḥ śravaṇe’pi pūrvapakṣe gāyatrījyotiṣorbhinnatvena jyotirdāvārā tadākṣepāyogācca| catuḥ sūtryā ekādhikaraṇatvaṃ svīkṛtyoktāgrisūktasthavākyodāharaṇatvamapyayuktam| tatra cchandobhidhānabhūtādipādavyapadeśopadeśabhedānāmabhāvena cchandobhidhānabhūtādisūtrāsaṅgatyaprasaṅgāt|
yattu candrikāyām -- uktākṣepe jyotirhṛdaya āhitaṃ yat ityāgrisūktasthasyaiva jyotiṣaśchāndogye’pi jyotiḥ śrutyā yo’yamantarhṛdaye iti hṛdayanihitatvadharmeṇa ca śākhāntarādhikaraṇanyāyena pratyabhijñāyamāna venaikyānannoktasūtrāsaṅgatiriti samādhānamuktam| tadasat| divaḥ paratra viśvottīrṇapradeśavṛttitvena cchāndogyavākye śrutasya jyotiṣo hṛjayanihitatvenāgrisūktoktajyotiṣo bhinnatvena jyotirdvayasyaikalpanāyogāt| ata eva na śākhāntaranyāyaviṣayatvam| agrisūkte karṇādividūratvaśravaṇena cchāndogye tadetaddṛṣṭaṃ ca śrutaṃ ca iti śravaṇena māsāgrihotranaiyamikāgrihotrayorivātrāpi jyotiṣorbhinnayornāmaikyasya vastvaikyāprayojakatvāt|
yaccoktākṣepa ākārabhedema tayoravirodhopapattiriti candrikāyāṃ samādhānamuktam| tadapyasat| niścite hi dvayorjyotiṣoraikya ākārabhedenādhirodhopapattikalpanā tatkalpanayā caikyasiddhirityanyonyāśrayatvaprasaṅgāt|
yacca candrikāyām -- na ca pūrvapakṣe’grisūktasya samākhyābalādagriparatvācchāndogyavākyasya ca cchandaḥ paratvāttayorekāviṣayatvalābhāvānna pratyabhijñāsambhava iti śaṅkyamityākṣipya tejo vai brahmavarcasaṃ gāyatrī iti gāyatryāṃ tejorūpatāśravaṇāttejaḥ -- śabdāgriśabdāyoranatibhinnārthatvādagrau gāyatrīśabdasya gāyatryāṃ vāgriśabdasya sambhavenāgrisaktavacchāndogyasyāpyagriparatāyāśchāndogyavadagrisūktasyāpi gāyatrīparatāyā vā sambhavena dvayorekārthatvānna pratyābhijñāsambhava iti samādhānam -- tadupahāsyameva | brahmarvasatejauhetau gāyatryām -- āyurghṛtam iti vadgauṇavyapadeśe’pi tasya tejaso’grirūpatvābhāvena tāvanmātreṇa vākyābhedāsiddhyā ca tayoraikyāsiddheḥ| anyathā tejaḥ śabdagāyatrīśabdayoḥ sāmānādhikaraṇyadarśavat yajamānaḥ prastaraḥ iti nirdeśādyajamānaprastarayorapyaikyāpattyā sūktavākena yajamānasyaiva praharaṇaṃ syāt|
catuḥ sūtryā aikādikaraṇye chāndogyavākyasudāharaṇamityapi na yuktam| chāndogyavākye vicaraṇābhidhānābhāvāt| agri sūktacchāndogyasthajyotiṣoruktarītyaikyasthāyuktatvāt| svavākyagatāsādhāraṇānekaśrutiliṅgatyāgenānyavākyagataliṅgena nirṇaye bījābhāvācca|
yacca candrikāyām -- yadyapi cchāndogyoktasya brahmatve yadvaitabrahma ityādiśrutiliṅgāni svavākyasthāni santītyākṣipya tathāpi tattatsūktasthaniravakāśaviṣṇuliṃṅgai sāvakāśaśrutisūktabādhena sarvavedāntānāṃ viṣṇau samanvayaṃ darśayituṃ sūtrakāreṇagrisūktasthaṃ liṅgamuktāmiti samādhānaṃ tathā ca cchāndogyasthaṃ jyotirviṣṇuḥ| asyaiva jyotiṣo’grisūktevicaraṇābhidhānāt iti sūtrārthena bādhakamiti| tadasat|
niravakāśaliṅgavirodhena śrutibādhasyāntarādhikaraṇākāśādhikaraṇanyāyasiddhatayānanyathāsiddicaraṇādividvaratvaliṅgenaiva tatsamākhyāśrutidvayabhaṅgenāgrisūktasyāntarādityanyāyasājātyena brahmaparatvasiddhayā tannirṇayasyānapekṣitatayā tanmukhena tadabhidhānavaiphalyācca| niravakāśaliṅgavalāttasya jyotiṣaḥ paramātmatve’pyasya jyotiṣo niravakāśajāṭharāganyaikyasacivaśaśrutibalādanyaparatvaśaṅkāyāstenāparihārācca| kiñcaitanmate chandobhidhānāt iti sūtraṃ na vicaraṇābhidhānarūpahetvākṣepakam| tatpratipādakāgrisūktagāyatrīvidyayoraikye hetvabhāvāt| kathañcittadupapādane vā jyotiḥ -- śabdasyeva gāyatrīśabdāsyāpi niravakāśāttadvirodhenānyaparatvānupapādakatvāt| śruterniravakāsaliṅgabhañjakatve ca jyotiḥ -- śabdasyaiva tadbhāñjākatvāpatte| anyathāsiddhisahatvasyobhayostulyatvāt| ata eva na sādhyākṣepakatvamapi | gāyatrīśabdāsamabhivyāhārādatra jyotiṣo brahmānyatve’pyagrisūktasthajyotiṣo brahmānyatvāsiddheḥ| dvayoraikyāsambhavastūkta eveti dik|
bhinnādhikaraṇapakṣastu sutarāṃ na sambhavati| vikāraśabdānneti ceto’ ityādāviva dhandobhidhānānneti cet ityukteraikādhikaraṇyasūcakatvāt| chandobhidhānādityādhikaraṇasya cchāndogyasthagāyatrīśabdanirṇayārthatvoktirapi na svarasā| tathā sati gāyatrī talliṅgāt ityeva vaktavyatvāt| hṛdayanihitaṃ jyotirviṣṇurityābhihitam| tacca cchāndogyaśrutau śrūyate -- yadataḥ paro divo jyotiḥ iti| tacca gāyatrītvena paṭhyate| gāyatrīśabdaśca cchandasyeva rūḍha iti hṛdayanihitaṃ jyotirna viṣṇuḥ ityuktākṣaipapūrvakagāyatrīśabdanirṇayārthatvāyoktavinyāsa ityapi na svarasam| gāyatrīśabdasamabhivyāhārācchāndogyasthajyotiṣo’brahmātve’pyagrisūktasthasya brahmatvānākṣepāt| ubhayorekyāsambhavasyoktatvāt| agrisūkte’nuktasya gāyatrīśabdasya tadanākṣepakatvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- aikādhikaraṇyamatadvaye’pi vākyadvayamapyudāharaṇamiti kalpanam, tathā yathā hyagrisūktasthavākye prakaraṇānugṛhītaśrutyā pūrvapakṣastathā chāndogye’pi gāyatrī iti sarvam ityupakramāt saiṣā catuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā gāyatrī iti copasaṃhārāttasya gāyatrīprakaraṇatvena tadanugṛhītajyotiḥ -- śrutyā pūrvapakṣa iti pūrvapakṣayuktisādhāraṇyāttathāgrisūkte viratiśayavaibhavalakṣaṇacaraṇena siddhāntavacchāndogye’pi etāmeva nātiśīyante iti niratiśayavaibhavarūpeṇa caraṇena siddhānta iti siddhāntayuktisādhāraṇyāccaikādhiraṇatā| yathāghārāgrihotrādyādhikaraṇeṣu ādhāramāghārayati agrihotraṃ juhoti ityudāharaṇabhede’pi dravyadevatālakṣaṇayāgarūpābhāvānna yāgāvidhitvamiti pūrvapakṣayuktisādhāraṇyānmantravarṇādinā devatādilābhānna rūpādyabhāva iti siddhāntayuktisādhāraṇyāccaikādhikaraṇatā tadvat iti vākyadvayodāharaṇatvopapādanaṃ ca| tadapi na sat| chāndogyasthajyotiṣo vicaraṇābhidhānābhāvena pūrvapakṣasiddhāntayuktisādhāraṇyasyādikaraṇaikye’pi vākyadvayodāharaṇatāyāṃ sādhakatvenopanyastasyāsiddheḥ|
yadapi candrikāyām -- agrisūkta iva etāmeva nātiśīyante iti niratiśayavaibhavarūpaṃ chāndogye’pyasyaiva vicaraṇābhidhānamastītyuktam| tadapi na| vi me karṇā patayato vi cakṣuḥ ityāderagrisūktoktasya karṇāderviruddhapatanarūpajyotiratikramaṇarūpavicaraṇasya etāmeva nātiśīyante iti cchādogyoktagāyatryanātikramaṇarūpasya vicaraṇasya parasparaṃ bhinnasvalarūpasyekaśabdenābhidhānāyogāt| tābhyāṃ gamyamānaniratiśayavaibhavaparatvaṃ tu nābhidhānarūpaṃ sambhavatyāṃ gatau kliṣṭaṃ kalpayituṃ yuktam| kiṃ ca yadyubhayorvākyayoratra viṣayatā tadā chandobhidhānāt iti vat agnyabhidhānāt iti vaktavye tadabhidhānopekṣāyāṃ bījābhāvaḥ| chandaḥ śabdo’grerapyupalakṣakaḥ iti candrikoktaṃ tu na sat| upadeśabhedabhūtādavyapadeśayostadāpyagate | upadeśabhedāditi pūrvapakṣayuktirbhūtādipādetisiddhāntayuktiścacchāndogyavākyamātrāsādhāraṇī iti cadrikoktaṃ tu chandrobhidhānāt iti sūtre tathaiva vaktuṃ śakyatayā tatropalakṣaṇatvāṅgīkārasya niṣphalatvāpattyānupādeyameva|
āghārāgrihotrādhikaraṇadṛṣṭānto’pyayukta eva| tadadhikaraṇe -- agrihotraṃ juhoti āghāramaghārayati ityādivākyaṃ na karmavidhāyakam| tatra dravyadevatayoraśrutatvāddravyadevatālakṣaṇayāgarūpasyābhāvāt| kintvagrihotravākyaṃ payasā juhoti dadhnā juhoti ityādivākyavihitasya karmasamudāyasya, āghāravākyam ṛjumāghārayati ūrdhvamāghārayati ityādi vākyavihitakarmasamudāyasyānuvāda iti pūrvapakṣe -- dadhyādivākyena na dadhyādiguṇamātravidhānaṃ sambhavati| tvanmate’grihotra vākyasya karmavidhāyakatvābhāvena guṇinaḥ kasyācidasiddheḥ| nāpi dadhyādiguṇaviśiṣṭakarmavidhānaṃ sambhavati| vidhigauravāpatte | tasmādagrihotrādivākyameva karmavidhāyakam| tatra dadhyādivākyairdravyalābhaḥ| devatā ca homakaraṇamantravarṇāt iti siddhānte cobhayagocaragrāhakasya sādhāraṇasyeva prakṛte pūrvapakṣāsiddhāntayuktisādhāraṇasyāsiddheruktatvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- upadeśabhedāt iti pūrvapakṣayuktiḥ bhūtādipāde iti siddhāntayuktiśca cchāndogyavākyamātrāsādhāraṇītyatrārthavādanaye bhaṭṭamata eko vicāro’rthavādamantrādisādhāraṇaḥ| anyastvarthavādamātrāsādhāraṇa iti dviparvavicāradṛṣṭāntakathanam| tadapi na| arthavādanaye hi vāyurvai kṣepiṣṭhā devatā ityarthavādagatāḥ śabdāstathā uru prathasva iti mantragatāśca śabdāḥ vāyavyaṃ śvetamālabheta bhūtikāmaḥ iti vidhigatavāyavyādiśabdānāṃ tathā puroḍāyāṃ prathayati iti vidhigataśabdānāṃ ca nairapekṣyeṇaiva bhūtārthamanvācakṣate | vidhivākyagatavāyavyādiśabdā arthavādagataśabdanairapekṣyeṇaiva viśiṣṭamarthaṃ vidadhatīti mantrārthavādebhyo bhāratarāmāyaṇādivatkaścidvṛttāntaḥ pratīyate, na tvanuṣṭheyaṃ kiñcidityekavākyatvābhāvānnārthavādamantrāṇāṃ dharmai prāmāṇyamiti pūrvapakṣe -- padaikavākyatvābhāve’pi vidhivākyasya puruṣaṃ prerayituṃ vidheyārthaprāśastyadevatāprakāśādyapekṣatvādarthavādamantrāṇāṃ ca phalavadarthāvabodhaparyavasitādhyayanavidhiparigṛhītatvena pūruṣārthāpekṣatvātprāśastyaṃ lakṣaṇayā samarpayadyataḥ kṣipragāmī(mi) svabhāvatayā śīghraphalaprado vāyurasya parśordevatā tataḥ praśastamimaṃ vāyavyaṃ paśumālabhetoti vākyayoranvayādarthavādānāṃ vidhivākyekavākyatayā dharme prāmāṇyam| evaṃ mantrāṇāmapi vidhyaikavākyatayaiva prāmāṇyamityeko vicāro mantrārthavādasādhāraṇaḥ| yato vāyuḥ kṣiprameva phalaprado’to vāyavyaṃ paśumālabhetatyevamarthavādoktaṃ hetumapekṣyaiva vidhiḥ puruṣaṃ niyuṅkte| tataḥ sāpekṣatvādvirvarna prāmāṇyamanapekṣyatvarūpamiti pūrvapakṣe -- sāpekṣatvaṃ hi pramāṇantarādigatārthabodhakatvam| arthavāde vimataṃ karmānuṣṭheyaṃ phalavadadevatopotatvādrājasevāvat ityanumānaṃ yadi vivākṣitaṃ syāttadā virdharnānāntarasāpekṣatvalakṣaṇamapramāṇyaṃ syāt| na tvevamasti| kintu phalapadadevatātīṣakatvopanyāsena karmaprāśastyamuvalakṣyate| tathā yati praśastaṃ karmānuṣṭheyamityasminnarye sārvavādavidheḥ| paryavasānādekavākyatā labhyata iti kutaḥ| pramāṇāntarasāpekṣatvamiti vidhiḥ pramāṇameveti siddhānta ityanyo vicāro’rthavādamātrasādhāraṇaḥ| tatra dvitīyavicāreṇa kṛtasya mānāntarānapekṣatvarūpaprāmāṇyaprakāraviśeṣasthāpanasya prathamavicārakṛtasāmānyena prāmāṇyasiddhyadhīnasiddhikatvādvicāradvayasya parasparapekṣatvavadiha vicāradvayasya parasparāpekṣatvābhāvāt, tadvadiha vicāradvayasya sāmānyaviśeṣarūpahetudvayābhāvācca vaiṣamyasya tadvidāṃ spaṣṭatvāt|
yadapyānandatīrthīyānāṃ prathamādhikaraṇaśarīram -- jyotiridamāgrirviṣṇurveti sandehaḥ| asya sūktasyāgrisūktatvāttadgatajyotiḥ śabdo’griparaḥ| na ca hṛdaya āhitaṃ yat iti guhānihitatvaliṅgena bādhaḥ śaṅkanīyaḥ| prakaraṇasanāthāyāḥ śruterliṅgena durbalena bādhāyogāt| na ca niravakāśatvaṃ śruteriva liṅgasyāpi| jāṭhare jātavedasi liṅgasya sāvakāśatvāt| ataḥ sāvakāśaliṅgātparabalābhyāṃ prakaraṇaśrutibhyāmuktajyotiṣo yuktamagritvamiti pūrvapakṣe -- niravakāśena karṇādividuratvena prakararaṇabādhācchruterviṣṇāveva mahāyogavidarvadvadrūḍhibhyāmupapatterviṣṇurevedaṃ jyotiriti siddhāntaḥ| spaṣṭaṃ cedaṃ candrikādau tadīyagranthe|
tatredaṃ cintyam -- niravakāśaliṅgena śrutibādhasya pūrvādhikaraṇadvayasiddhatvena punarihānyaparatvaśaṅkānutthānāt| prakāraṇasya liṅgāddurbalatvena na tatsahāyenoktaśaṅkotthānam| niravakāśatayā prabalena liṅgena prakaraṇabādhakasya kaimutyanyāyasiddhatvāt| etena tvamagre ityagriśabdasannidhānādagriparatvaśaṅketi candrikoktakalpanaṃ nirastam| sannidhānasya prakaraṇādapi durbalatvena tadvādhasya daṇhāpūpikānyāyasiddhatvāt| kiñca jyotiḥ śabdāgriśabdayordevatāparatve’ntaradhikaraṇanyāyela , bhūtaparatva ākāśādikaraṇanyāyena gatārthatvaṃ durvāram| na ca prakaraṇānugrahādadhikā śaṅketi yaktam| śruterviṣaṇuparatve prakaraṇasyāpi viṣṇuparatvasambhavenānyaparatvāyogāt| na cāgrisūktatvādivyavahāreṇa prakaraṇasyānyaparatvaśaṅketi kalpanaṃ yuktam| akṣividyādityavidyetyādivyavahārasyākṣiśabdādityādiśabdavattāmātreṇeva gārhapatyaparāyā api ṛca aindrīti vyavahārasyendraparavattāmātreṇeva viṣṇuparasyāpi sūktasyāgripadavattāmātreṇāgrisūktatvavyavahārasiddhe|
yadapi -- uktarītyākṣipya jāṭhare’grau sāvakāśasya liṅgasyāgrau sāvakāśaśrutyā bādhopapatteriti pūrvapakṣasamarthanaṃ candrikāyām| tadapyasat| jāṭhare’grau hṛdayanihitatvasyāprasiddherliṅgasya niravakāśatvāt| jāṭharatvaprasiddhivirodhena tatsāvakāśatvakalpanānupapatteśca| karṇādividūratvasyāgrāvasambhavācca| tadācchādanena pūrvapakṣastvasmadrītyā pūrvapakṣasiddhāntābhiprāyeṇādikaraṇayojanāsambhavenāyukta eva|
yadapi candrikāyām -- idamagrisūktaṃ na viṣṇusūktamityadhyāpakaprasiddhāgrisūktatva -- viṣṇusūktatvābhāvavyavahārasacivvānniravakāśamapi liṅgaṃ śruti -- pratibadhnātīti niravakāśatvamātrapayuktaliṅgataprābalyasya niravakāśoktavyavahārasācivyaśrutitvobhayaprayuktaśrutigataprabalyāddurbalatayā liṅgagatabahutvaprābalyasya śrutitvarūpasvabhāvaprayuktaprābalyāddurbalatayā yuktameva sūktasyāgriparatvamiti
pūrvapakṣopapādanam| tadapi na vicārakṣamam| prāṇavidyākṣividyādityavidyetyādivyavahāravadaindrīmantraprasiddhivaccoktarītyānyathāsiddhavyavahārāderarthaviśeṣanirṇāyakatvāyogāt| ata evaikaviṣṇuviṣayakatvāviśeṣe’pi tattatpadaśravaṇāśravaṇarūpaparaviṣeṣaśravaṇaprayuktaḥ puruṣasūktamidaṃ nārāyasūktamidaṃ nedaṃ pūruṣasūktaṃ nedaṃ nārāyaṇasūktamidaṃ viṣṇusūktaṃ nedaṃ viṣṇusūktam ityādivyavahāraḥ saṅgacchate| tattatpadāśravaṇena tattatsūktatvābhāvavyavahāre’pi tasya tatparatvābhāvānirṇāyakatvāttatsācivyācchruteḥ prābalyāyogāt| liṅgasya tu niravakāśatvaprayuktaṃ prābalyaṃ nirapavādameveti sati bādhake nātrāgrisūktādisamākhyā nirṇāyikā prabhavati| niravakāśatvabahutvaprayuktaprabalyānna śrutitvasūktasācivyapayuktamapi prābalyam| śrutitvapayuktaprabalyasya prāgeva nirastatvācca| chāndogyavākye ca noktaprakārādhikā śaṅkā pravartate|
tamovirodhitvaṃ jyotiṣpadapravṛttinimittamāśritya brahmaṇi tamasā saha vartamāne jyotiḥ sabdāsambhavena pūrvapakṣotthānamityapi paroktaṃ na sat| prajananaṃ jyotiḥ, virāṭchandasāṃ jyotiḥ, trivṛtpañcadaśasaptadaśa ekaviṃśa etāni vā jyotīṣiṃ ityādi bahutaraprayogavirodhena tamovirodhitvasya jyotiṣpadapravṛttinimittatvābhāvāt| ādityāditejomaye brahmāṇi tamasā sahāvasthānasya kenāpi pramāṇenāsampratipatteḥ prakāśakatvamātrasyaiva tatpravṛttinimittattavaucityāt| astu vā yathākathañcittamovirodhitvaṃ jyotiṣpadapravṛttinimittamityāśaṅkā tathāpi tasyā dityādisādhāraṇatayā grimātrapūrvapakṣopaṣṭambhakatvavarṇanamasaṅgatameveti noktapūrvapakṣo yuktaḥ| pūrvapakṣasyāyuktatvenaiva tannirākaraṇasiddhānto’pyayukta eva| kiñca -- 
karṇādīnāṃ vidūratvaṃ viṣṇoranyatra naiva hi|
agrestu śrutisūktādi sāvakāśaṃ parātmani||
ityādinā karṇādividūratvaliṅagena jyotiḥ śruterviṣṇuparatvanirṇayasiddhānto na yuktaḥ| talliṅgasya śivasādhāraṇyāt| tathā hyatharvaśikhāyām -- atha haina paippalādo’ṅgirāḥ sanatkumāraścātharvāṇamuvāca, bhagavankimādau prayuktaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyāyitavyam, ki taddhyānaṃ ko vā dhyātā kaśca dhyeyaḥ ityanena dhyānadhyātṛdhyeyaviṣayapraśnamavatārya sa ebhyo’tharvā uvācomityetadakṣaramādau prayuktaṃ dhyānaṃ dhyāyitavyamityedakṣaraṃ paraṃ brahmā iti praṇavānugataṃ brahmā dhyeyatvenokatvā asya pādāścatvāro vedāḥ ityādinā praṇavasya trimātrābhistasya vyāptimuktvā etasmāddhyānādau prayujyate sarvakaraṇopasaṃhāratvāddhāryaṃ dhāraṇātturīyaṃ brahamā ityanenāvasānasthitacaturthamātrātmakapraṇavākhyaturīyaparabrahmāṇaḥ sarvakaraṇyavyavahāratītatvasya dhyeyatve hetutvamupanyasya dhyāyīteśānaṃ pradhyāyitavyam ityupasaṃhāre śiva eva dhyeyasvarūpamiti nirṇayātsarvakaraṇavidurasya śivasya karṇādivadūratvākṣateḥ| uktapraśnavākye’yamarthaḥ -- prathamato vidhātreta śeṣaḥ| dhyānam -- dhyāyata iti dhyānaṃ dhyānaviṣayaḥ, dhyāyitavyam -- śiṣyaputrādīnāmupadeśayogyam| athavā dhyānayogyam| ayamatra vivakṣito’rthaḥ -- dhyeyatvena yogyaṃ prayuktaṃ prathamotpanno vighātā kiṃ dhyātavānbhavadādibhyaḥ putrebhyaśca kimuktavāṃśceti| śeṣa spaṣṭāryam| mantropaniṣadi ca -- yadā tamastanna divā na rātrirna sanna cāsacchiva eva kevalaḥ iti sivaṃ prastutya nainamūrdhvaṃ na tiryañcaṃ na madhye parijagrabhṛt ityanena ko’pyenaṃ kutrāpi na gṛhītavānityabhidhāya na sanddaśe tiṣṭheti rupamasya ityukatvā taddhetutvena na cakṣuṣā paśyati kaścadenam iti śrutam| tatra cakṣuṣetyupalakṣaṇam| anyairapīndriyairenaṃ na kaścijjānāti| tato na saṃdṛśe’sya rupaṃ savarupaṃ tiṣṭhati saṃdṛśe samyagdarśana ityarthaḥ| tathā ca cakṣuḥ karṇādividūratvasya śivasādhāraṇyaṃ śrutisiddhaṃ nirvivādameva|
karṇādividvaratvaṃ ca iyat itiparicchedānarhavaibhavatvameveti candrikoktamasādhveva| vi me karṇā patayato vi cakṣuḥ ityanena karṇādīnāṃ jyotiratikramaṇasyaivokterniratiśayavaibhavatvasya tena gamyamānatve tu caraṇābhidhānāt ityatrābhidānapadāsvārasyamityāmihitameva pūrvapakṣānirākaraṇe | tasyāpi śivasādhāraṇyaṃ viṣṇvodyaiśvarvasyāpi śivādhīnatvapratipādanena vyavasyāpitamantastaddharmādikaraṇe| vidvadraḍhyādinā jyotiḥ śruterviṣṇāveva mukhyātvīktirapi na yuktā| nārāyaṇapase jyotiḥ itivanmantropaniṣadi śivaparāyāḥ iśāno jyotiravyayaḥ itīśānapadavācye śive’pi tadraḍherdarśanāt|
idaṃ na viṣṇusuktamityadhyāpakaprasiddhistu dhānyamāsa dhinuhi iti mantrasya māṃsahaviṣkayāge niviṣṭasya māsamasi ityūhe kartavye mṛgo’si ityahaprasiddhivadajñānamūlaiva| viṣṇvādiśabdābhāvādeva tatprasiddheriti candrikokta tu na sādhu| bahujanasaṃvādaviṣyaprasiddherajñānāviṣayatvakalpanasyāyuktatvāt| dṛṣṭāntoktistu svakīyabhramapamūlaiva| dhānyasabdasya satuṣadravyavācinaḥ puroḍāśarūpahaviṣprakṛtidravyatvenaiva nirdiṣṭasya māṃse haviṣi tatprakṛtidravyavācino mṛgaśabdasyaivohanīyatvauciysya nyāyavitsaṃmatatvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- agrernāparicchedyavaibhavatvam| agrirvaigadevānāmavamaḥ ityavamatayā bhīṣāsmādagristapati iti sabhayatayā parihyena mriyante vidyudṣṭaṭiśca candramā ādityo’griḥ iti maraṇādimattayā ca tasya śrutatvāt| agriśrutyādi tu agrināgriḥ samidhyate taddevā jyotiṣo jyotiḥ nārāyaṇaparojyotiḥ viṣṇureva jyotiḥ ahamagrirahaṃ hutam jyotiṣāmapi tajjayotiḥ ityādiśrutismṛtibhirviṣṇau sāvakāśāmityukatvā, evaṃ sūktāntareṣvapi draṣṭavyam| tathā hi -- aindre na tvā vām̐ itdra kaścana jāto na janiṣyate iti , tathā īśānamasya jagataḥ svardṛśamīśānamindra tasthuṣaḥ iti , tathā bārhaspatye viśveṣāmijjanitā brahamaṇāmasi iti , tathā saure, sūrva ātmā jagatastasthuṣaḥ iti , tathā viśvakarmasūkte viśvataścakṣuḥ  yo naḥ pitā janitā ityādīni santi brahmāliṅgāni| kicaṃ saure yenā pāvaka cakṣuṣā bhuraṇyaṃ taṃ janām̐ anu| tvaṃ varuṇa paśyasi iti sūryasya pāvakavaruṇādeśabdaiḥ saṃbodhanam| tathā vāruṇe athā vayamāditye iti varuṇasyā dityādiśabdeḥ, saṃbodhanam, tathā greye, ṭa tvamagra indro vṛṣabhaḥ satāmapi tvaṃ viṣṇururugāyo namasyaḥ ityagriṃ saṃbodhya tatrendraviṣṇvādiśabdāprayoga uktasūktānāṃ sravaśabdavācyabrahmāparatve yuktonānyatheti sarvasūktagatanāmasamanvayaparatvamasyoktam| tatpakṣahetvorādiśabdābhāvādupalakṣaṇatvāśrayaṇasya kliṣṭatvāddheyam| kiṃcāgrisūktasthajyotiṣaiva nirṇaye jyotirdarśanāt ityasya gatārthatāpattiśca|
yadapi candrikāyām -- jyotirdarśanāt ityatra tvagrisūktasthasyāpi jyotiṣo mriyamāṇatvādiliṅgairjīvatve śaṅkitāni liṅgāni brahmaparopakramopasaṃhārasthatvādantarbhāvitaṇyantānīti vakṣyata iti paunarukatyāpattinivāraṇam | tadapi mriyamāṇatvādiliṅgānāṃ brahmaṇi sandaṃśanyāyenānyārthaparatāyā atraiva nirūpāyituṃ yuktatvāddheyam|
yadapi -- chandobhidhānāt ityāditrisūtryā adhikaraṇāntaratvavarṇanam| tathā hi -- atra lokato’nyatraprasiddhagāyatrīnāmasamanvayaḥ kriyate | chāndogye gāyatrī vā idam̐ sarvam ityārabhya atha yadataḥ paro divo jyotirdīpyate iti śrūyate| tatra gāyatrī kiṃ chandoviśeṣa uta viṣṇuriti saṃśaye, gāyatrīśrutiprasiddhyā vāgvaiṣā iti vākyatvasaṅkīrtanācca cchandoviśeṣe eva| na ca jyotiḥ śrutyā bādhaḥ| śrutyā śrutibādhāyogāt| jyotiḥ paryāyatejaḥ śabdasya tejo vai gāyatrī iti śravaṇena gāyatryāmeva tadupapatteśca| gāyatrīśabdaśca na viṣṇo rūḍhaḥ| tatra mānābhāvāt| liṅgasya rūḍhyā bādhāt| na cātra tadadhīnatvanyāyaḥ pravartate| vedarūpāyāstasyā nityatvāt| tathā cedaṃ jyotirapi tadrūpameveti pūrvapakṣe, siddhāntaḥ -- gāyatrī viṣṇureva| tathā hi -- darśamaṃ śrutirityarthaḥ| etadgāyatryā gānatrāṇakartṛtvaśravaṇāt| tasya cchandasyabhāvāt| ato jyotirapi viṣṇureva| tadadhīnatvācca mukhyatā| na ca nityatvavirodhaḥ|
svabhāvajīvakarmāṇi dravyaṃ kālaḥ śrutiḥ kriyā|
yatprasādādime santi na santi tadupekṣayā||
iti nityānāmapi tādadhīnyaśruteḥ| bhūtādipādavyapadeśādapīdaṃ nirṇīyate| saiṣācatuṣpadā ṣaḍvidhā gāyatrī iti gāyatryāḥ ṣaḍvidhatvamanūdya catuṣpadatvaṃ vyapadiśya tatraiva puruṣasūktamantraḥ| saṃvādāyodāhriyate -- tadetadṛcābhyuktaṃ tāvānasya iti| asya puruṣasya tāvānpūrvokto mahimā| na kaivalametāvānkintu puruṣastataḥ pūrvoktādapi mahimno jyāyān| katham| sarvāṇi bhūtānyasyaikaḥ pādaḥ, asyāmṛtaṃ svarūpabhūtaṃ pādatrayaṃ divi tiṣṭhatīti|
yaduktaṃ gāyatrī jyotiśca viṣṇuriti| tadbhavedyadi gāyartrājyotiḥ prasaṅgayorekaṃ vastūcyeta| na caivaṃ tripādasyāmṛtaṃdivi iti gāyatryā dyusthitatvoktyā paro divaḥ iti jyotiṣo divaḥ paratvokatyā virodha iti śaṅkanīyam| trilokasaptalokādyabhiprāyeṇopadeśabhedopapatteḥ| tathā hi -- bhurbhuvaḥ svariti lokatrayābhiprāyeṇa lakṣayojanocchritāntarikṣoparitanaśvetadvīpānantāsanavaikuṣṭhānāṃ dyutvāttadgatarūpatrayaṃ divītyucyate| saptalokāpekṣayendrasadanasya dyutvādvaikuṣṭhasya divaḥ paratvaṃ merorapi dyutvāttatparānantāsanasya divaḥ paratvaṃ sūryamaṇḍalasyāpi dyatvena taduttaraśvetadvīpasya divaḥ paratvaṃ tatrasthamūrtittayasya divaḥ paratvaṃ ca yujyate| ata ubhayathopadeśe’pi virodhābhāvādubhayatrāpyekameva vipṇvākhyaṃ vastūcyata iti gāyatrī viṣṇureva| sa eva jyotirānandamayaśceti siddhānta iti|
tatredaṃ cinttyate -- yadyatra gāyatrīnāma samanvetavyaṃ tarhi bhāṣye jyotirvākyopanyāsaḥ kimarthaḥ| na tāvatpūrvādikaraṇākṣepārthaḥ| jyotirvākyasyobhayādhikaraṇaviṣayatāmate tadākṣepe’pi jyotiḥ sūtre’grisūktasthamuktavākyameva viṣaya iti pakṣe tadanākṣepāt| anākṣepe ca cintāvaiphalyāt| gāyatrīśabdasya prāgbrahmāviṣayatvānuktyā chandobhidhānānneti cenna iti śaṅkānutthānācca| yadi caitaddoṣaparijihīrṣayā jyotirnāmaiva cchāndogyasthaṃ samanvetavyamiṣyate tarhi samanvetavyanāmāgrahaṇānyūnatā| svapakṣasādhanaktvaiva cchandobhidhānādityākṣepo’pi nocitaḥ|
yattu -- gāyatrīvākye viṣaye jyotirvākyopādānaṃ pūrvapakṣe jyotiradhikaraṇākṣepadarśanārtham| jyotirvākye viṣaye gāyatrīvākyopādānaṃ gāyatrīśabdarūpapūrvapakṣabījapradarśanārthāmiti candrikāyāmuktam| tadasat| gāyatrī vai ityupakramācchāndogyasthajyotiṣacchandaratve’pyagrisūktasthajyotiṣaḥ karṇādividūratvena sādhitasya brahmātvasyānākṣepāccintāvaiphalyāt| nanu dvayoraikyāśrayaṇeneyaṃ śaṅketi parairuktameveti cet| karṇādividūratvasya draṣṭṛtvaśrutatvādeścātyantāviruddhatayā pratyabhijñābādhasyoktatvāt|
yattu candrikāyām -- uktamākṣipya jyotiṣo gāyatrītve iyaṃ vāva sā yeyaṃ pṛthivī iti gāyatryabhinnatvenoktāyāṃ pṛthivyām asyaṃ hīdaṃ sarvaṃ bhūtaṃ pratiṣṭhitam ityuktasthitihetutvāderapi taddharmatvāpattyā janmādisūtrākṣepasambhavāccintāsāphalyaṃ varṇitam| tanmandam| idaṃ sarvam iti pratyakṣāvagatakiñcidāśrayatvamātrokatyā janmādisūtrābhihitacetanācitanātmakakṛtsnajagajjanmasthitalayahetutvādirūpabhavadabhimatāṣṭakartṛtvānākṣepāt| tāvatāpi jyotirvākyaprayojanālābhācca| etena yo’yamantarthṛdaye iti cchāndogyasthe jyotiṣyānandamayadharmabhūtahṛdayanihitatvaśravaṇena jyotiradhikaraṇamanākṣipyaivānandamayādhikaraṇākṣepakatvavarṇanamapi candrikoktamapāstam| guhānihitatvahṛdayanihitatvayorbhedana pratyabhijñānābhāvādityuvatyānandamayādhikaraṇākṣepakatvābhāvasya pūrvamuktatvācca| kiñca gāyatryāśchāndastve’pi tadupakramamātrānna jyotiṣaśchadastvam| prakaraṇavicchedakāthaśabdaśravaṇena athaiṣa jyotiḥ ityādāvetacchabdasyeva ata yadataḥ paro divo jyotiḥ ityatrāpi yacchabdasyāpyuktaparāmarśitvāsambhavāt| asmanmate tu chandobhidhānāt ityatraiva prakaraṇavicchedamākṣipya tannirākaraṇena yadataḥ para ityatra yacchabdasya pūrvaprakṛtavācitvameva , na athaiṣa jyotiḥ ityatreva prastoṣyamāṇaparārśitvamiti vyavasthāpyata iti na doṣaḥ| ata eva gāyatrīpūrvapakṣo’pi na yuktaḥ| kiñca niravakāśakarṇādividūratvaliṅgamātreṇa sāvakāśajyotiḥ śrutibādhasya pūrvādhikaraṇa uktatvādatra yadvai tadbrahma iti niravakāśaśrutisahitena niravakāśabhūtādipādatvaliṅgena gāyatrīśrutibādhasya kaimutikanyāyasiddhatvānnoktapūrvapakṣodayaḥ|
yattu candrikāvām -- viṣṇau prasiddheṣvanyeṣu bhagavannāmasu satsvanyatra prasiddhahagāyatryādiśabdaprayogavaiyarthyānna gāyatrīśabdo bhagavatpara ityādhikaśaṅkāyā pūrvapakṣotthānavarṇanam| tadasat| ākāśādipadasāmānādhikaraṇye’syāḥ śaṅkāyā viśiṣyākīrtanena tatparihārāyogāt| sarvatra tatparihārastu na sūtrārūḍhaḥ| sūtre chandaādyabhidhānāt iti vinyāsāpatteḥ| etena -- ākāśādiśaśabdasādhāraṇīyam| gāyatrīsabde’nupapattyantarasadbhāvādadhika śaṅkodaya udāhṛta iti candrikoktamanādaraṇīyam|
yadapi candrikāyām -- yuktamagrisūktasthajyotiṣo viṣṇutvam| upadeśabhedābhāvāt| iha tu divaḥ iti pañcamyupadeśavākyasthasya jyotiṣo jyotiḥ śrutyā viṣṇutve’pi divi iti tadviruddhasaptamyupadeśavadvākyasthāyā gāyatryā na viṣṇutvamityasādhāraṇī tvanyetyadhikaśaṅkāpradarśanam| tadapyasat| pratyabhijñānaprayuktasyaiva viṣṇuparatvasyopadeśabedapratibadhyatayā nimittāntaraprayojyasya tasya tadapratibadhyatayā tatra tadabhāvahetutvābhidhānasyāyuktatvāt| chāndogyavākyagatadraṣṭṛtvādiviruddhakarṇādividūratvopadeśasya tatrāpi sattvānna pratyabhijñānamityasyoktatvāt| ata eva pañcamīnirdeśetyādyapi na yuktam| pañcamīnirdeśasya viṣṇuparatāyā mahetutvāt| jyotiṣaścaraṇābhidhānādihetvantareṇa viṣṇuparatvasyeva sarvātmatvādihetvantareṇa viṣṇuparatvasya gāyatryāmapi sambhavena tatropadeśabhedasyāpratibandhakatvāt| yadi hi jyotirupakramādgāyatrī syāttasyāstadabhedacca tasyā viṣṇuparatvamuktaṃ syāt| tathopadeśabhedānna tadabheda iti śaṅkā syāt| iha tu gāyatryupakramājjyotiṣaśchandastve śaṅkite tadrūpabhedābhidhānaṃ chandastvābhāve’nukūlameva syāt|
nanu gāyatryā viṣṇutvājjyotiṣo’pi viṣṇutvamatra pratipādyaṃ taccopadośadbhajyata iti| maivam| gāyatrīnāmasamanvaye pradhāne siddhe jyotirnāmasamanvayābhāve’pyadoṣāt| na hyatra jyotiṣo’nyatve pūrvādhikaraṇasthajyotiṣo’pyanyatvaṃ hetubhedādityuktam| abhadhāne cārthikena codyaparihāro yukto netyuktārthapratikṣepāsvārasyādubhayorapratipādyatvenaikadeśākṣepo yuktaḥ| tathā satyākṣepyanāmagrahaṇakartavyatāpatteḥ|
yadapi candrikāyām -- agrisūktasthajyotirniṣṭhakarṇādividūratvaviruddhadṛ,ṣṭaśrutatvādyupadeśabhedānnedaṃ jyotirviṣṇuḥ, tataśca gāyatryāpi na viṣṇurityaparādhikaśaṅkāpradarśanam| tadapi pūrvoktaśaṅkānirākaraṇayuktyaiva na sat| jyotiṣīnyatve’pi gāyatryā viṣṇutvāvirodhāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- ekasyā vahniśruteranyatraprasiddhibādhe’pi gāyatrīvākpṛthivyādyanekaśabdānāmanyatraprasiddhibādho na yukta ityadhikaśaṅkāpradarśanam| tadapyasadeva| antaradikaraṇe śrutibāhulye’pi liṅgena bādhadarśanātprakṛta ekaviṣayānekaśrutyabhāvena śrutibāhulyāsiddheśca| anyathātiprasaṅgāt| vāggāyatrīpadayoraparyāyatvātmāṇamātariśvādipadavalliṅgabādhyatvopapatteśca|
yacca candrikāyām -- antaradhikaraṇa indrādibahuśrutīnāmanyatra prasiddhibādhastvanayatrāprasiddhāntaḥ syatvamukhena yuktaḥ| na ceha tathā mukhamastītyuktam| tanna| prakṛte’pi tathābhūtasarvabhūtātmakatvādimukasadbhāvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- etacca pramāṇāśritā tu -- yuktamākāśādyakaraṇoktaṃ janyabhūtākāśasvabhāvasya vivaratvāderjyotiḥ śabdapravṛttinimittasya ceśādhīnatvam| dharmiṇā saha janyatvāt| gāyatrīrūpavedasya tvanāditvena tatsvabhāvasya gānatrāṇakartṛtvādeḥ pravṛttinimittasya neśādhīnatvamityādhikāśaṅkāpradarśanam| tadapi na sat| na tāvadvaraṇānā padānā vā nityatvaṃ vedanityatvam| kavikāvyāderapi nityatvaprasaṅgāt| api tvānupūrvīnityatvam| taccoccāraṇaprayojyamiti kathaṃ tasya neśvarīdhīnatā| tasyā anāditvarūpanityatvasya sāditvarūpajanyatvavirodhitve’pīśvaradhīnatvāvirodhitvācceti|
pūrvapakṣaścāpi na yuktaḥ | pūrvādhikaraṇe brahmaparatvena niṇartiyā jyotiḥ śrutyā gāyatrīśrutibādhasaṃbhavāt| ata eva śrutyā śrutibādhāyoga iti tattvaprakāśikāyāṃ jayatīrthoyoktirapi na yuktā| nirṇītatvānirṇītatvābhyāṃ velakṣaṇyāt| na copakramasthatvādgāyatrīśrutatuprabalyamityabhiprāyakalpanamityapi sādhu| brahmātarkādupakramāpekṣayomapakramaprābalyamavagacchatā tvatpūrvapakṣiṇā tathā pūrvapakṣitumayogyatvāt|
nanu gāyatryā aviṣṇutve atha yadataḥ iti yadataḥ paro jyotiḥ iti nirdi,ṭapratinirdeśaḥ [ataḥ] yacchabdasamānādhikaraṇo jyotiḥśabdo’pi tatparaḥ syāditi jyotirapyaviṣṇuḥ syāditi cet| na | athaiṣa jyotiḥ ityatraitacchabdasyaiva prakṛte’pyathaśabdena vibhinnaprakaraṇasya yacchabdasya prakṛtaparāmarśitvāyogena prakariṣyamāṇaparatvaucityāt| anekārthasya tasyedamādivatpūrvaparāmarśitvābhāvācca| tasya gāyatrīparatve yā jyotiḥ iti strīliṅgataucityacca| brahmavarcasatejohetau gāyatryām āyarghṛtam itivat tejo vai gāyatrī ityanyathāsiddhamiti pūrvamevoktm| ato jyotirgāyatrīvākyayorekaviṣayatāgrāhakayamāṇābhāvadbhāyatrīśabdasya cchandaḥparatve’pi jyotiḥ śabdasya tatparatvāsiddheḥ| kathaṃcidekavākyatākalpane ca nirṇītopasaṃhārasyajyotiṣpadānurodhena gāyatrīparasyaiva brahamāparatāpattyā na cchandaḥ parau jyotirgāyatrīśabdāviti yuktaṃ kalpayitumiti| evaṃ siddhānte sūtrākṣarārthastāvadayuktaḥ| tathā cetorpaṇao ityādeḥ pañcamyantasya hetusamarpakatve tathā hi darśanamityasya dṛṣṭāntasamarpakatvaṃ svataḥ svarasam| tatparityajya tathā hi ityāderhetusamarpakatvakalpanasya cetorpaṇao’ityādeḥ prayojanasamarpakatvakalpanasya cāyuktatvāta| kiṃca tathāśabdaḥ sādṛśyavācī prasiddhaḥ | tena sādṛśyānusaṃdhānameva svarasataḥ pratīyate| tathāhi ityakhaṇḍo nipāta uktārthāpapādanapratijñāyāṃ svarasaḥ| tvaduktārthe tu na svārasyam| uttarasūtradvayānanvayastukta eva| tādadhīnyāsaṃbhavasya pūrvapakṣopanyastasya na kiṃcidupapādakaṃ sautraṃ padam| utsūtrasiddhāntastvayuktaḥ| ata eva prāṇaḥ ityādivadatideśādeva tadvāraṇasūcanaṃ tvatideśābhāvādeva na yuktam|
yattu candrikāyām -- 
anādīnāṃ guṇādīnāṃ yathā guṇyāditantratā|
tathānādiśruterastu kṣemāyeśvaratantratā||
yathā vaiśeṣikamate’nādinityānāmapi gaganādiparimāṇādīnāṃ svāśrayagaganādyadhīnatā, yathādvautimate vānāderātmājñā nasaṃbandasya jīvabrahmāvibhāgādeśca saṃbandhyajñānādyadhīnatā, yathā vā prābhākaramate’nāderduḥkhaprāgabhāvasya mokṣasya jñānādhīnatā, yathā vā matāntare duritaprāgabhāvasyānāderapi niṣiddhākaraṇādhīnatā, tathā gāyatryāstatpadapravṛttinimittasya cotpattyabhāve’pi viṣṇvadhīnatā sthitaiveti pūrvapakṣaparihārapradarthanam| tanna samyak| gāyatryāśritasya dharmasya gānatrāṇakartṛtvasya kathaṃ tadanāśrayabrahmādhīnatvāmiti śaṅkāyāṃ guṇānāṃ svāśrayādhīnatvopapādanenāparihārāt| nahi nityāyā gāyatryāstatsvabhāvasya cānyādhīnatve gaganādipariṇādidṛṣṭānto ghaṭate| tadvadaha guṇaguṇibhāvābhāvāt| svaniṣṭhāni hi dravyāṇi nityāni gaganādīnyanadhīnānyupalabhyante| atastatsādhāraṇyaṃ na tādadhīnyaprayojakam| anityeṣvapi notpādakatvamāśritatvaṃ vā tādadhīnyaniyāmakam| anutpādakapoṣakādyadhīnatvasyaiva bahulamupalambhāt| śrutiprāmāṇyāttadaṅgīkāre tu tadeva tarhi vaktavyam| na tu dṛṣṭāntabhāvāt| duḥ khaprāgabhāve na jñānādhyadhīnatā | api tu prāgabhāvanāśakapratiyogyutpattipratibandhakatvamātram| tanmātreṇa tādardhānyavyavahāramātram| evamadvaitimate’pyanādyajñānasaṃbandhasya jīvabrahmāvibhāgādeścāvidyānāśādhīnanāśapratiyogitvenāvidyādhīnatvavyavahāramātrameva| duritaprāgabhāvasyāpi niṣiddhākaraṇādhīnatvaṃ duritotpattikāraṇaniṣiddhakaraṇābhāvādeva| naivaṃ paramātmano gāyatrīsvarūpe gānatrāṇakartṛtve vā karaṇatvamiti dṛṣṭānto’pyayukta eva| kaṃcāstvanāderapi gāyatryākhyavedasyeśvarādhīnatvaṃ māstu vā kiṃ tena syāt| na ca gāyatryāstādadhīnyāttatpadavācyatā brahmaṇaḥ| kiṃtu tatpadapravṛttinimittasya tādadhīnyāt| taccedānīmapi sidhyati| bhūtyasya rājādhīnatve’pi tadgatasvabhāvaviśeṣāṇāṃ tādadhīnyādarśanena vastuta(na) stādadhīnyasya tatsvabhāvaviśeṣatādadhīnyaṃ pratyaprayojakatvāt| dīpagataprakāśakatvādestatpadapravṛttinimittasya tailādyadhīnatvadarśane’pi tailāderdīpādipadavācyatvādarśanena tatpadapravṛttinimittasya tādadhīnye’pi tasya tatpadaprayogāprayojakatvacca| tasmāt -- catuḥ sūtryā aikādhikaraṇyam| tatra ca cchāndogyavākyameva viṣayaḥ| tatrasthajyotiṣpadasyaiva paramātmaparatvaṃ sādhyam| tatra mantravarṇāktacaraṇābhidhānameva hetuḥ| tatra cchandobhidhānāt ityasiddhiśaṅkā| tasyāṃ chandasi bhūtacaraṇatvāsaṃbhavasūcanena cchandobhidhānasyānyathāsiddhisūcanena tatrāpi bhūtādipādavyapadeśenopaṣṭambhanam| punarupadeśabhedaprayuktāsiddhiśaṅkānivāraṇamityevāsmadbhāṣyakāropadarśitaḥ krama eva yukta iti|| 27||
śramacchaṃkarapādābjabhūṣaṇe paradūṣaṇe|
raghunāthakṛtirbhūyājjyotirnayavivecane||
iti jyotiradhikaraṇaṃ samāptam||

atha dvitīyādhyāyasya prathamaḥ pādaḥ|
atha smṛtyadhikaraṇam|
manvādyadvaitavādipratibhaṭakapilādyāgamasyaiva bādho nyāyyastvevaṃ gurūṇāṃ suvacavivaraṇākṣepiṇāṃ dūṣaṇāya|
vlṛptaḥ ko’pi prabandhaḥ saralamatimatā rāghavācāryasūricchātreṇāyaṃ prayatnātprabhavatu viduṣā saṃsadi protsavāya||
smṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṅga iti cennānyasmṛtyanavakāśadoṣaprasaṅgāt||9||
pūrvādhyāye jagadupādānatvakartṛtvādyupalakṣitaṃ pratyagabhinnādvitīyaṃ brahamā vedāntaiḥ pratipādyata iti vedāntānāṃ tādṛśabrahmaparatvaṃ saprapañcaṃ nirūpitam| tadiha smṛtyādivirodhodbhāvanenākṣipyāsminnadhyāye samarthyata ityanayoradhyāyorviṣayaviṣayibhāvaḥ saṃgatiḥ| ata evāsya prathamādhyāyānantaryaṃ phalavattvaṃ ca| viṣayaparijñānaṃ vināsā(sa) ṅgatyanākṣepasya tatparihārasya vā kartumaśakyatvāt| atra sākṣātparamparayā vā bādhakodbhāvanena samanvayākṣepaḥ pūrvavādyabhimataḥ| tatra sākṣādbādhakamapekṣya paramparayā bādhakasya vilambitapravṛttikatvādativirodhinaḥ svapakṣe smṛtinyāyavirodhasya sākṣādvādhakasyaiva prathamapāde nirāsaḥ| tataśca itareṣā cānupalabdhe iti sūtre mahattattvādisādhakapramāṇābhāvena tadviṣayakasmṛteramūlakatvātkāryasmṛteriva rūpeṇa tatsadharmatayā kāraṇasmṛteraprāmāṇyaṃ prasādhya smṛtyanavakāśaprasaṅgasyeṣṭāpattiparāhatatvenābādhakatve samarthite vakṣyamāṇānumānādyaduṣṭapramāṇamūlakatvādamūlatvāsiddhyā neṣṭāpattiḥ saṃbhavatīti punaḥ smṛtyanavakāśa āpādita uktaparamparayā svapakṣavirodhināṃ tādṛśānumānādīnāṃ duṣṭatvopapādanena dvitīyo nirāsaḥ| etena -- tṛtīyacaturthayorapyādyavatsvapakṣabādhakoddhārarūpatvādyanantaryaṃ syāt| dvitīyasya tu parapakṣadūṣaṇasarūpatvādantyatā syāditi candrikākāroktaṃ dūṣaṇaṃ nirastam| kāraṇasmṛteranumānādimūlakatvasmṛtyanavakāśaprasaṅgeṣṭāpattyayogaśaṅkāyāṃ tanmūlatvābhimatānumānādīnāṃ duṣṭatvopapādanamantareṇopakrāntasmṛtyavirodhasyāparyavasānāddvitīya eva tadupapādanasyāvaśyakatvāt| athaivaṃ parābhimatamūlapramāṇaduṣṭatvopapādanam na vilakṣaṇatvāt ityataḥ prāgeva kartavyaṃ syāditi cet| atra tātparyavidaḥ -- smṛ(śru) tiverodhe sati smṛtyaprāmāṇyasya virodhe tvanapekṣam iti nyāyena sarvaireva vedaprāmāṇyavādibhirupagantavyatayā vilakṣaṇatvāditarkaramupajīvya vedāntānāṃ vedāntyabhimatārthaparatānirāse kṛta eva tantrasya prāmāṇyāsaṃbhavāttadupajīvyavedāntānāṃ smṛtivipratiṣedhena brahmāparatvābhāvaḥ śaṅkituṃ śakyo nānyathā, iti prathamasūtroktabādhakopanīvyabrahmāvādasākṣādvādhakavilakṣaṇatvāditarkanirāsasyaiva prathamaṃ kartumucitatvāditi| tṛtīyaturīyayorbhūtabhautikādiviṣayaśrutīnāṃ parasparavipratiṣedhādviruddhavākyadvayānyataratvamahājanaparigṛhītatvādeḥ prāmāṇyaprayojakatvā saṃbhavena tadanyasya ca prāmāṇyaprayojakasya vede durvacatayā prāmāṇyaprayojakadharmaśūnyānāṃ vedāntānāṃ noktabrahmāparatvalakṣaṇasamanvayaḥ saṃbhavatīti rītyā paramparayā brahmāvādavirodhinaḥ śruti vipratiṣedhasya nirāsa iti pādopādhibhedaḥ| ete pādopādhibhedāḥ śrīmadbhāṣye bhagavatpādaiḥ saṃkṣepeṇa darśitāḥ idānīṃ svapakṣe smṛtinyāyāvirodhaparihāraḥ pradhānādivādānāṃ nyāyābhāsopabṛṃhitatvaṃ prati vedāntaṃ sṛṣṭyādiprakriyāyā avigītatvamityasyārthasya pratipādanāya dvitīyo’dhyāya ārabhyata iti| tṛtīyacaturthapādayorapyupādhibhedastairevopadarśitaḥ -- viyadādiviṣayavṛttivipratiṣedhastṛtīyena parihṛtaścaturthenedānī prāṇādiviṣayaḥ parihriyata iti|
yattu candrikāyām -- anye tu -- ādyapāde svapakṣe sāṃkhyādismṛtibhistadunnītatarkaiśca virodhaparihāraḥ| dvitiye sākhyādimatānāṃ duṣṭatvoktaḥ| tṛtīye pūrvabhāge pañcamahābhūtaśrutīnāmuttarabhāge tu jīvaśrutīnāṃ caturthapāde liṅgaśarīraśrutīnāṃ parasparavirodhaparihāra iti pādārthānāhuriti| niruktapādopādhibhedamanūdya tanna ādye tṛtīye ca pāda ekopādhyabhāvāt| ādye’pi itareṣā cānupalabdheḥ etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ svapakṣadoṣācca etena śiṣṭā aparigrahā api vyākhyātāḥ ityādau paramatānāṃ duṣṭatvoktestvayāpi svīkṛtatvācca| nanvādye teṣāṃ duṣṭatvoktiḥ svapakṣasya tairvirodhanirāsārtheti cet| na| dvitīye’pi taduktestadarthatvāt| anyathā dvitīyesyāvirodhādhyāsāsaṃgatiḥ syāt| kiṃ ca yathādyapādasthe dṛśyate tu ityatra vilakṣaṇayorapi brahmāprapañcayorvṛścikagomayayoriva kāryakāraṇabhāva ityetadarthapratipādake svapakṣe tarkavirodhaparihāraḥ| tathā dvitīye’pi madaddīrghavat iti sūtre yathā vā dvyaṇukādaṇorhrasvācca sato mahaddīrghaṃ ca tryaṇukaṃ jāyate nārṇu nohrasvam, evaṃ cetanādbrahmaṇo’cetanaṃ jagajjaniṣyate iti tvayā bhāṣitatvena tatrāpi tarkavirodhaparihāro’styeva| tṛtīyasya pūrvabhāge’pi antarā vijñānamanasī ityatra tvanmate etasmājjāyate prāṇomanaḥ sarvendriyāṇi ca ityādiprāṇamanaādiviṣayaśrutyavirodhasyoktatvena na bhūtaśrutyavirodha eveti niyamaḥ| nanu annamayaṃ hi saumya manaḥ, āpomayaḥ prāṇaḥ, tejomayī vāk iti śrutermanaādīni bhūtānyeveti tadvicāro bhūtavicāra eveti cet| tarhi caturthe’pi tathā prāṇaḥ saptagaterviśeṣitatvācca saptagaterviśeṣitatvācca aṇavaśca ityādivahvadhikaraṇaiḥ kṛta indriyavicāro’pi tṛtīyasya pūrvabhāge syāt| caturthe’pi saṃjñāmūrtikalṛptiḥ ityatra anena jīvena iti vākye jīvasya kartṛtā nocyate kituṃ brahmāṇa iti tvayā siddhāntitatvena na liṅgaśarīrasyaiva vicāra iti niyamaḥ| kiṃca tṛtīyacaturthayorapyādyavatsvapakṣavirodhaparihārarūpatvādādhānāntaryaṃ syāt| dvitīyasya tu parapakṣadūṣaṇarūpatvādantyatā syāt iti dūṣaṇamuktam| tatrocyate -- yaduktam -- ādye tṛtīye ca pāda ekopādhyabhāvāditi| tadasat| svapakṣasākṣādbādhakoddhāraḥ prathamapādopādhiranugata eva | svapakṣe smṛtinyāyavirodhaparihāraḥ iti prathamapādopādhikathanaparabhāṣye smṛtinyāyayordvairāśyena kathaṃna tu tayoḥ svapakṣasākṣādvirodhitvaparihāre’pi yuktimapekṣya sarvajñapraṇītatvamahājanaparigṛhītatvādinā vedatulyāyāḥ smṛterabhyārhitatvasūcanadvārā tadvirodhanirasanaprāthamyabījoddhāṭanāya| abhyarhitatvalābhaśca dvaṃdve prathamaṃ nirdeśāt| abhyarhitaṃ pūrvam ityanuśāsanāt| evaṃ tṛtīyapāde pūrvottarabhāgayormahābhūtadīvaviṣayakaśrutervirodhaparihāre’pi bhautiketaraviṣayaśrutiparahārarūpastatpādopādhignugata eva| tvadīyairjayatīrthai(rthīyai)rapyadyātmetaraviṣayakatvaghaṭitasyaiva tṛtīyapādopādherupadarśitatvācca|
yadapi -- itareṣāṃ tānupalabdheḥ ityādi| tadasmadanabhipretārthadūṣaṇatvāccintyam| itareṣāṃ cānupalabdheḥ ityatra kāryasmṛteriva kāraṇasmṛterapi nirmūlatvopavarṇanasyaivābhipretatvena smṛtimūlatvābhimatānāmanumānādīnā duṣṭatvopavarṇanasyānabhipretatvāt| ata eva prathamapādānte idānīṃ parapakṣaniṣedhapradhānaṃ prakaraṇaṃ prāripsamāṇaḥ svapakṣaparigrahapradhānaṃ prakaraṇamupasaṃhārati bhagavatpādairabhihitam| tathā yogādhikaraṇe’pi yogasmṛtivirodharūpaṃ svapakṣabādhakamuddhṛtam| na tu tanmatābhimatānumānādiduṣṭatvamupapāditam| svapakṣadoṣācca ityatrāpi paropanyastabrahmāvādavirodhidūṣaṇānāṃ parapakṣasādhāraṇatayā paraisteṣāṃ dūṣaṇatvaṃ nāstītyevāṅgīkāryamityevābhiprāyo bhāṣye varṇitaḥ| na tu paramatānāṃ duṣṭatvam| etena śiṣṭāparigrahāḥ ityatrāpi vaiśeṣikādyuktānāṃ brahmāvādasākṣādvirodhitarkāṇāṃ nirāsa eva kṛto na tu tadīyasmṛtimūlatvābhimatānumānaduṣṭatvamuvarṇitāmiti naiteṣā sūtrāṇāṃ paramparayā bādhakanirasanapare dvitīyapāde saṃgatiḥ|
yathādyapādetyādikamapi na sat| kāraṇaguṇāḥ kāryeṣu svajātīyaguṇamārabhanta ityabhyupagamavādinā jagataścetanabrahmakāryatvābhyupagame jagataścetanatvaṃ syādityāpādite yadi kāraṇaguṇānāṃ sarveṣāmeva niyamena svajātīyaguṇārambhakatvaṃ tavānumataṃ tadā svapakṣavipratiṣedhaḥ, hrasvaparimaṇḍalābhyāṃ mahaddīrghayorutpattestvayopagamena pārimāṇḍalyādeḥ kāraṇaguṇasya svajātīyakāryaguṇarambhakatvāditi parapakṣadūṣaṇamukhenaiva svapakṣabādhakoddhārasya mahaddīrghavadvā iti mutre’bhipretatayā tatsūtrasya svapakṣabādhakoddhāraparapakṣadūṣaṇobhayarupatayā parapakṣadūṣaṇaparadvitīyapāde’pi niveśopapatteḥ| na caivaṃ tatsūtrasyobhayarūpatve prathamopasthitasvapakṣabādhakoddhāraparaprathamapādaniveśaparityāge bījābhāva iti śaṅkyam| svātantryeṇa vaiśaṣikābhimatasvapakṣabādhakodvārasya prathamapāde kṛtatvena dvitīye ca svatantryeṇa tanmatanirāsasya prastutatvācca tatraiva tanniveśopapatteḥ| tvanmate’pi bhoktrāpatteḥ iti sūtre nanu mukteśvarayorbheda samarthanaṃ phalādhyāsaṃgatamato naiva tatsūtrārthaḥ ityādinā phalatve’pi yuktivirodhe’ntarbhāvādatroktam iti bhāṣya mavatāryaitatsūtrasya phalādhyāyasaṃgatatve’pyatra nāsaṃgatiḥ| yenānyarthakalpanam| uktarītyā yuktivirodhe’pyantarbhāvāt| anyathobhayāntarbhūtasyaikatrāpyanuktiprasaṅgādityubhayāntarbhūtasyaikatra niveśasvīkāraccaitaddūṣaṇasya svaśirastāḍanarūpatvāt|
tṛtīyasya pūrvabhāge’pītyādikamapi bhāṣyādarśināṃ pratāraṇamātratvādasadhveva | antarā vijñānamasīo’ ityetatsūtra ātmano bhūtānāmutpattipralayāvanulomapratilomakramābhyāṃ bhavata iti nirūpite ātharvaṇe bhūtānāmātmanaścantarāle karaṇānyanukamyante -- etāsmājjāyate prāṇaḥ ityādinā, teṣāṃ cotpattilayakramāntarānveṣaṇa uktakramavirodhaḥ iti antarā vijñānamanasī krameṇa talliṅgāditi cet ityanenākṣipte yadi tāvadbhautikāni karaṇāni tato bhūtotpattilayābhyāmeva teṣāmutpattilayau bhavata iti naitayoḥ kramāntaraṃ mṛgyam iti aviśeṣāt iti sūtrabhāgasya bhāṣye vyākhyānāttatsūtrasya bhūtaviṣayaśrutivirodhaparihārarūpatvasyātispaṣṭatvāt| tathā ca tasmātparvoktotpattipralayaktamabhaṅgo bhūtānām iti pūrvapakṣopasaṃhāre’ tasmānnāsti bhūtotpattiktamabhaṅga iti siddhāntopasaṃhāre[ca] bhāṣyakṛduktamiti|
caturthe’pītyapi na sat| bhautiketaraviṣayaśrutivirodhaparihārātmake tṛtīye pāde bhautikaprāṇādiviṣayaśrutivirodhaparihāraparāṇām tathā prāṇāḥ ityādīnāṃ niveśāyogāt| utpattiviṣayaśrutivipratiṣedhaḥ prāṇānāṃ parihṛtaḥ, saṃkhyāyāviṣaya idānī parihriyate iti saptagaterviśeṣitatvācca iti sūtrāvataraṇabhāṣyaparyālocanayā tatrendriyavicārasya katatve’pi pādadvaya indriyavicārasya viṣayabhedo bālenāpi subodha eva|
caturthe’pītyādyapi na yuktam| saṃjñāmūrtio’ ityatra anena jīvenātmanānupraviśya ityādivacanānusāreṇa jīvasyaiva bhautikanāmarūpasraṣṭṛtva mavagamyate| taddhedaṃ tarhmavyākṛmāsīttannanāmarūpābhyāmeva vyākriyate ityatra tu parameśvarasyeti bhautikanāmarūpaviṣayaśrutivirodhasyaiva nirākṛtatvena caturthapādodhiniyamātyāgasyātispaṣṭatvāt|
tṛtīyacaturthayorapyādyavatsvapakṣavirodhaparihārarūpatvādādyānantaryaṃ syāt| dvitīyasya tu parapakṣadūṣaṇarūpatvādantyatā syāditi dūṣaṇaṃ tu prāṅ nirastameveti dik|
ānandatīrthīyāstu -- prathame yuktyavirodho dvitīye samayāvirodhastṛtīye kevalaśrutyavirodhaścaturthe sayuktikaśrutyavirodha ityāhuḥ| tadasat| alaukikārthagocarabahujanaparigṛhītavākyasaṃdarbharūpasamayatvasya tvadīyacandrikākāroktasmṛtisādhāraṇatayā smṛtīnāṃ samayarūpatābhiprāyeṇaiva smṛtyadhikaraṇe samayavigānaṃ saṃdehabījamiti tattavaprakāśikāyāṃ jayatīrthoktyā ca smṛtīnāṃ samaya bahirbhāvāyogena smṛtyavirodhasya samayapada eva saṃgateḥ| smṛtīnāṃ yuktyādirūpatveneti jayatīrthokteḥ smṛtīnāmiva samayānāmapi yuktirūpatvāviśeṣātsamayavirodhasyāpi smṛtivirodhavadādyapādenirasanaucityāt| samayānāmapi smṛtiviśeṣarūpatayā smṛtivirodhaparihāreṇa samayāvirodhasyāpyuktānupādhyā| itareṣā ca ityatra kṛtatvena tadartha dvitīyapādānapekṣaṇācca | na ca tatra sāmānyato nirākṛtānāmatra viśeṣato nirākaraṇāditi jayatīrthoktirapi sādhīyasī| viśeṣatī nirākaraṇasya samayeṣviva smṛtiṣvapi tulyatayā dvitīyapāde evopavarṇita ityasyāpi vaktuṃ yuktatvāt|
yattu candrikāyām -- smṛtyavirodhaparādhikaraṇasya yuktipādeniveśānupapatti māśaṅkya pāśupatādismṛtīnāṃ samayarūpatvena svoktārtha saṃmatitvena śrutiyuktikathanena ca yatuḥ svarūpatvāttadavirodhasyaikaikavirodhapratipādake pādaviśeṣenantarbhāve’pyavirodhādhyāyāntarbhāveṇa kvacinniveśe kartavye prathamaṃ tadatikrame hetvābhāvaccaturātmakatayā prabalatayā ca prathamaṃ nirasanīyatvāccādhyāyādāveva niveśa iti samāhitam| tadasat| ato vaidika smṛtīnāmapramāṇatvānna śrutyuktajagatkāraṇatvasya tadvirodhaḥ iti jayatīrthokteḥ kāṇādabauddhārhatādismṛtyavalambipūrvapakṣāṇāṃ smṛtyadhikaraṇa eva candrikāyāṃ pradarśitatvāccavaidikasmṛtimātravirodhaparihārasya tvadabhipretatvena sāṃkhyāpāśupatādicatūrūpāṇāmeva samayāna mādye nirāsa iti nirbandhasya vaktumaśakyatvāt|
kiṃcābhimānyadhikaraṇe mṛdabravīt atha ha cetanāścācetanāśca tejobannānyākāśa iti tānyacetanāni vāyurvāva cetanaḥ iciśrutyorvirodhasyāsadadhikaraṇe asadevedamagra āsīt sadeva saumyedamagra āsīt iti śrutyorvigānasya , etena śiṣṭāparigrahāḥ ityatra akasmāddhīdamanāvirāsīdasakasmāttiṣṭhati iti etasmājjāyate prāṇaḥ ityādiśrutīnām bhoktrāpatteḥ ityatra pare’vyaye sarva ekī bhavanti ityaikyaśrutibhedaśrutyoḥ itaravyapadeśāt ityatra jīvakartṛtābodhakaśruteḥ eko ha vai nārāyaṇaḥ ityādiśrutīnāṃ ca parasparavirodhanirāsakatvasaṃbhave pravalāyā śruterupasarjanatvaṃ parikalpya -- mṛnna vadati jaḍatvāt, kāraṇaṃ pralaye’sadbhavitumarhati vastutvātkāryavat, vipratipannaṃ niṣkāraṇamacetanakāraṇaṃ jīvakāraṇaṃ vā kāryatvātkūlapatanadadhyādivat, viṣṇurna jagatkartā jīvābhinnatvādityādiyuktivirodhaparihāratākalpanenādyapādāntarbhāvoktiranucitaiva|
evaṃ kevalaśrutivirodhaparihāraparatṛtīyapāde -- viyadadhikaraṇe ātmana ākāśaḥ saṃbhūtaḥ anādirvāsamākāśaḥ iti śrutyorvirodhaparihārapare ākāśasyotpattimattvāṅgīkāre’nubhūtiyuktibahuvākyavirodhāt ityādinā viyadanutpattimadvibhutvāt iti yukterjayatīrthāpadarśitāyāḥ śruterviyadanāditva sādhikāyāḥ, etena mātariśvā vyākhyātaḥ iti dvitīyādhikaraṇe tāsāṃ vāyurvatsaḥ sā vā eṣā devatānādiryo’yaṃ pavate iti śrutyorvirodhanirāsapare sarvadevatopāsyatā ca notpattimattve ghaṭata ityādinopadārśitāyāḥ sarvadevatopāsyatvātiśayita cetanatvayuktestadanāditvasādhikāyāḥ asaṃbhavastu sato’nupapatteḥ ityādhikaraṇe asataḥ sadajāyata sadeva saumyedamagra āsīt iti śrutivirodhanirāsake brahmāṇaḥ sāditvasādhikāyā asaṃbhavadutpattikanabhonabhasvatsāmyarūpāyā asaṃbhavadudbhāvayornabhonabhasvatorityādinā janmaśruteḥ sayuktikatvena prābalyādityantena tvaduktāyā yukteḥ, āpaḥ ityadhikaraṇe agrerāpaḥ tadapo’sṛjata iti śrutivirodhaparihārapare’pāṃ tejojanisādhikāyā vimatā āpastejaso jātā aptvātsvedādivadityādinātvaduktāyā yukteḥ, tadabhivyānādeva tu talliṅgātsaḥ ityadhikaraṇe yatprayantyabhisaṃviśanti rudro mā viśāntakaḥ iti śrutyorvirodhaparihārapare viṣṇoḥ saṃhārakartṛtvābhāvasādhikāyā viṣṇorjagadutpādakatvālloke pituranyasyaiva mārakatvadarśanāditi tvaduktāyā utpādakayukteḥ, antarā vijñānamanasīo’ ityādhikaraṇe akṣarātparamādeva [iti] śruteḥ yacchedvāṅmanasī prājñastadyacchejjñāna ātmani iti pūrvāvasthitamanastattvasya pūrvameva laya iti liṅgadarśanasya cotpattikrameṇa vyutkrameṇa ca layaviṣaye virodhaparihārapare na sarveṣā vyutkrameṇaiva layaḥ| kiṃtu keṣācitkramātkeṣāṃcidvyutkrameṇa| tathā hi -- vijñāna tattvaṃ tāvanmanastattvādutpannaṃ śrūyate -- manasaścaṃ vijñānam iti śrutau| tasya ca paścādutpannasya vijñānatattvasya paścādeva layaḥ pūrvasthitasya manastattvasya pūrvameva layaḥ iti pūrvapakṣopapādikāyā tasyāḥ sayuktikaliṅgadarśanao’
ityādinā tvaduktāyā, jño’ta evaṃ ityadhikaraṇe nityo nityānām sarva ete cidātmano vyuccaranti iti śrutyorvirodhaparihāre jīvānutpattisādhikāyāḥ na jīva utpattimānanāditvādīśvaravat|| na ca sādhyāviśiṣṭatā hetoḥ| prāgabhāvaśūnyatāyā vivakṣitatvāt| na ca tatra vivādaḥ| utpatterevābhāvaprasaṅgāt| kāraṇaśūnyatāṃ vānāditvaṃ brūmaḥ| na ca tatrāpi vivādaḥ| kāraṇānupalabdheḥ ityādinā tvaduktāyāḥ kāraṇarāhityayukteḥ, utkrāntigatyadhikaraṇe vyāptā hyātmānaścetanā nirgaṇāśca sarvātmānaḥ sarvarūpā anantāḥ, iti kāṣāyaṇaśruteḥ aṇurhyeṣa ātmāyaṃ vā eti sinītaḥ puṇyaṃ cāpuṇyaṃ ca iti gaupavanaśruteśca jīvasya vyāptatvāṇutvalāviṣaye virodhanirāsakejīvavyāptitvasādhikāyāḥ tāvaduṇurātmā sarvadehagatasparśajñānāt ityādinā tvaduktāyā dehavyāpisukhādyupalabdhirūpayukteḥ, vyatireko gandhavattathā ca darśayati ityadhikaraṇe sa nityo niravayavaḥ puṇyayukpāpayugimaṃ lokamamuṃ cāvartate sa vimucyate sa ekadhā mana saptadhā na śatadhā iti gaupavanaśrutiḥ sa pañcadā sa saptadhā sa daśadhā bhavati| sa śatadhā ca sahastradhā sa gacchati sa mucyate iti pārāśaryā [ya] ṇaśruteśca jīvasyaikatvabahutvaviṣaye virodhaparihārapare jīvasyaikarūpatvasādhikāyāḥ, yujyate khaluvibhāvātmani yogaprabhāvena śarīrabahutayā vā ityādinā tvaduktāyā anekaśarīragitvarūpayukteḥ, yāvadātmabhāvitvacca na doṣastaddarśanāt ityadhikaraṇe layamabhyupaiti sa cānantyāya kalpyate ityanityatvanityatvayoḥ śrativirodhanirāsake jīvotpattisādhikāyā upādherutpattimattve pratibimbasyotpattiniyamādityanena tvaduktāyāutpattimadupādhipratibimvitatvarūpatāyā yukteḥ, pustvādivattvasya sato’bhivyaktiyogāt ityadhikaraṇe vijñānātmā saha deveśca sarvaiḥ sa ānanda sa balaḥ sa ojaḥ [iti] śruteḥ sa duḥ khādvimukte ānandī bhavati| so’jñānādvimukto jñānī bhavati ityādipauṅgiśruteśca parasparavirodhanirāsake jīvasyānāndatvāsādhikāyāḥ sarvadā tadanubhavaprasaṅgarūpayuktestvaduktāyā, kartā śāstrarthavattvāt ityadhikaraṇa īsvarasyaiva kartṛtvabodhakaśruteḥ phalasaṃpattyā jīvakartṛtvābodhikāyāḥ sayatkarma kurute tadabhisaṃpadyate iti śruteśca virodhanirāsake jīvasya kartṛtvābhāvasādhikāyā lāghavarūpayukteḥ samarthitaṃ hīśvarasyaivānanyayogena kartṛtvam itaravyapadeśāt ityādau| ata eva dvayoḥ kartṛtvamapi pratyuktam| īśvarasyaiva kartṛtvāṅgīkāre kalpanālāghavaṃ ca syāt iti tvaduktāyāḥ , ābhāsa eva ca ityadhikaraṇe rūpa rūpam iti śruteḥ prakāraṇavaśādanumitapratibimbatvābhāvabodhakaśruteśca virodhanirāsaka ekeśvarapratibimvatvābhāvasādhikāyāḥ pratibimbānāmeka jātīyatvābhāvarūpayuktestvaduktāyāḥ sattvenaiṣāmadhikaraṇānāṃ yuktivirodhanirāsake prathamapāda eva niveśa ucitaḥ|
atra ca pramāṇavākyasya svavirodhyarthaviṣayakajñānakaraṇatvavyāpakāpramāṇ‌vaka(ra) tvaniyamādvedāntaniṣṭhasyoktapramāśaktatvasya tadviṣayakaviruddhaviṣayabodhakasāṃkhyāsmṛtyaprāmāṇyaṃ vinānupapadyamānatayā tādṛśa(śā) prāmāṇyāpādakatvamapratyūham| tādṛśaprāmāṇyāpādakāpatteśca brahmāpramāśaktatvajñānavirodhitayā vedāntā yadyuktabrahmaparāḥ syustadā svavirodhyarthakajñānakaraṇatvavyāpakāprāmāṇyavantaḥ syu iti tarkaḥ svapakṣasākṣādvirodhīti tannirāsakatvādasyādhikaraṇasyādhyāyapādasaṃgatiḥ| tatra vedāntānāmuktabrahmaparatvamayuktamuta yuktamiti tadartha sāṃkhyāsmṛtiprāmāṇye sādhaka mastyuta nāstīti tadartha ca sāṃkhyāsmṛtiḥ kimaduṣṭakaraṇajanyajñānāmūlā,, uta neti saṃśaye -- vedāntā noktabrahmāparāḥ| tathā sati sāṃkhyāsmṛteraprāmāṇyaprasaṅgāt| na ceṣṭāpattiḥ| sarvajñapraṇītāyā mahājanaparigṛhītāyāśca vedavadeva prāmāṇyasya nirapavādatvāt| āptirhi prāmāṇyabojam| sā ca veda iva tantre’pi tulyā| tatpraṇeturiva tantrapraṇeturapi| ṛrṣiprasūtaṃ kapilaṃ yastamagre jñānairbibharti jāyamānaṃ ca paśyet iti śrutisiddhasarvajñatākatvāt| na ceśvarajñānasya nityatayāsaṃbhāvitadoṣakaraṇatvena tanmūlakānāṃ vedānā prāmāṇya niścetu śakyate kapilādisiddhajñānānāṃ ca janyatayā saṃbhāvitadoṣakaraṇatvānna prāmāṇyaniscayasaṃbhava iti vācyam| bhagavadanugrahāvirbhūtāvijñānānāṃ kapilādīnāṃ jñāne doṣamūlakatvaśaṅkāyā ayuktatvāt| nahi teṣāṃ pratāraṇādidoṣakalpane kiṃcidvījamasti| na ca virodhe tvanapekṣa syādasati hyanumānam iti nyāyasyātrāvakāśo’sti| nirṇītatātparyakaśrutivirodha eva tatprasarāt| na ca tatrāpyupakramādibhistātparyanirṇayaḥ śakyaśaṅkaḥ| balavattakrāvaṣṭambhena sāṃkhyācāryaiḥ svābhimatārtha eva tasya tātparyasya varṇitatvāt| nanve(cai) vaṃ sati manvādismṛtyaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgastatrāpi mahājanaparigṛhītatvasya prāmāṇyabījasya tulyatayeṣṭāpattyayogāditi vācyam| tāsāmanuṣṭheyārthe sāvakāśatayā sāṃkh‌yasmṛtiviruddhārthe tātparyākalpanāt| sākhyāsmṛtīnāṃ tvadhyātmadarśanamadhikṛtyaiva pravṛttānāṃ niravakāśatvāt| ata eva vedatantrayoḥ samabalatve nirṇayo vācyo na tu tantrānurodhena nirṇaya iti parāstam| vedasyāpi dharmādau svakāśatvāt| na copaniṣadāṃ niravakāśatā| tāsāmapi sāṃkhyāyarītyā svakāśatāyā upapādanīyatvāt| tathā hi -- na tāvatkāraṇavākyānāṃ brahmaṇi jagadupādānatābodhakatvaṃ tadganakartatābodhakatvaṃ vā| vakṣyamāṇatarkavirodhāt| nāpi pratyagabhinnaparamātmabodhakavākyānāṃ yathāśrutārthakatvam| hitākaraṇabandhamuktivyasthābhāvaprasaṅgāt| itthamevādvitīyatvādipakṣe doṣā unneyāḥ| evaṃ ca sāṃkhyāpravacanoktarītyā sṛṣṭivākyeṣu prakṛtitābodhakavākyānāṃ jagatprakṛtibhūtapradhānasvāmitāmātreṇaupacārikatvam| kartṛtābodhakavākyānāṃ keṣāṃcidavāntareśvarabodhakatvaṃ keṣāṃcidaupacārikatvamam| tattvamasyādivākyānāṃ cinmaṇḍalāntaḥ pātitvabodhakatvāmityevāgatyānusā(sa) raṇīyam| tathā ca sāṃkhyāsūtrāṇi -- tatsaṃnidhānādādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ maṇivat mūktātmanaḥ praśaṃsā, upāsā siddhasya vā nādvaitaśrutivirodho jātiparatvāt evamādīni| tatra bhāṣyam -- nanu tathāpi prakṛtyādyakhilādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ śruyamāṇaṃ nopapadyate| loke saṃkalpādinā pariṇamamānasyaivādhiṣṭhātṛtvavya vahārāditi | tatrāha -- taditi| yadi saṃkalpena sraṣṭatvamadhiṣṭhātṛtvamucyate tadāyaṃ doṣaḥ syāt | asmābhistu pūruṣasya saṃnidhānādevādhiṣṭhātṛtvaṃ sraṣṭṭatvādirūpamiṣyate maṇivat| yathāyaskāntamaṇeḥ sāṃnidhyamātreṇa śalyaniṣkarṣakatvaṃ na saṃkalpādinā tathaivā dipuruṣasya saṃyogamātreṇa prakṛtermahattatvādirūpeṇa pariṇamanam| idameva sopadhikasraṣṭṛtvamityarthaḥ|
nanvevamīśvarapratipādakaśrutīnāṃ kā gatistatrāha -- mukteti| yathāyoga kācicchutirmukrātmanāṃ kevalātmasāmānyasya jñeyatāvidhānāya saṃnidhimātreśvaryeṇa stutirūpā prarocanārthā| kācicca saṃkalpapūrvakasraṣṭṛtvādipratipādikā śrutiḥ siddhasya brahmāviṣṇuharādevarevānityeśvarasyābhimānādimato’pi gauṇānityatvādimattvānnityatvādyupāsanāparetyarthaḥ| nanveva pūruṣanānātve sati -- 
eka eva hu bhūtātmā bhūte bhūte vyavasthitaḥ|
ekadhā bahudhā caiva dṛśyate jalacandravat||
nityaḥ sarvagato hmatmā kūṭastho doṣavarjitaḥ|
ekaḥ sa bhidyate śaktyā māyayā na svabhāvataḥ||
ityādya śrutismṛtaya ātmaikyapratipādikā nopapadyanta iti| tatrāha -- nādvetaśrutīti| ātmaikyaśrutīnāṃ virodhastu nāsti| tāsāṃ jātiparatvāt| jātiḥ sāmānyamekarūpatvam tatraivādvai taśrutīnāṃ tātparyāt| nanvakhaṇḍatve| tatra prayodanābhāvādityarthaḥ| 
nanvayaṃ pūrvapakṣo na yuktaḥ| uktadoṣāṇaṃ samanvayādhyāya eva tatra tatroddhatatvāt| tathāhi -- īkṣatyādhikaraṇe pūrvapakṣabhāṣye pradhānasyānekātmakasya pariṇāmasaṃbhavātkāraṇatvopapattirmṛdādivat nāsaṃhatasyaikātmakasya brahmāṇaḥ ityupādānatvasya brahmaṇi pratikṣiptatvāttatsādhanaṃ vinā na siddhāntotthitiriti kṛtsnaprasaktiḥ iti nyāyābhāso’pi tatra nirasanīyatayā vivakṣitaḥ| evaṃ kvacidīkṣaṇasyaupacārikatvamityapi tatraiva nirastam| evamadṛśyatvādhikaraṇe -- 
cidātmanā tu sārūpyaṃ jaḍānāṃ nopapadyate|
jaṅa pradhānamevātau jagadyoniḥ pratīyatām||
iti nibandhe pūrvapakṣotthāpanāt|
vivartastu prapañco’yaṃ brahmāṇo’pariṇāminaḥ|
anādivāsanodbhūto na sārūpyamapekṣyate||
iti siddhantapraṇayanācca na vilakṣaṇatvāt ityādyadhikaraṇārtha evokta | jijñāsasūtra eva ca sarvajñatvalasarvakartṛtvatadabhāvādiviruddhadharmādhyāsājjīvabrahmā bheda māśaṅkya tanmithyātvasādhane viṣayaḥ| samarthita iti tadananyatvādadhikaraṇārtho’pi spaṣṭīkṛtaḥ| prakṛtiśca ityatrāpi kāryaṃ cedaṃ jagatsāvayavamacetanamaśuddhaṃ ca dṛśyata ityādinā na vilakṣaṇatvāt ityādi sūtrārtha eva darśita iti tadanuguṇena siddhāntena bhavitavyameveti cet| atra vadanti -- īkṣatyādhikaraṇe nityacaitanyarūpasya paramātmano mukhyasarvajñātvāsaṃbhavāttānnirvāhāya janyajñānopagame saśarīratvāpatteścasiddhāntahetorasvārasyamupapādya pūrvapakṣopodvalanāyaitatpādīyanirasanīyasya niravayavatvādiprayuktopādānatvāsaṃbhavasyopadarśitatve’piḥ tannirāsakanyāya etatpādīyasiddhānyasyaiva samāśrayaṇāt| yathoktaṃ tatraṃ siddhāntabhāṣye -- yathā tu tarkeṇāpi brahmaṇa eva kāraṇatvaṃ nirvoḍhuṃ śakyate na pradhānadīnāṃ tathā prapañcayiṣyate na vilakṣaṇatvāt ityevamādineti| etenoktanibandhaḥ prakṛtyadhikaraṇabhāṣya cāvirodhena vyākhyātam| evamanyatrāpi draṣyavyam|
yattu candrikāyām -- yattu vedāntānāṃ bahumukhatayā paratantraprajñānāṃ puṃsā taiḥ svato’rthaniścayāyogena smṛtyā tadarthe niścetavye sati manvādismṛtīnāmanuṣṭheyaparatvena sāvakāśatvātkāpilasmṛtestu siddhārthaniṣṭhāyāṃ mokṣasādhanasamyagdarśanamadhikṛtya pravṛttāyā anyaparatvāyogānnāravakāśatvarūpadoṣaprasaṅgāttadanusāreṇa vedāntā acetanakāraṇaparatayā vyākhyeyā iti prāpte manvādismṛtiṣupurāṇādeṣu ca bahunāṃ b‌rahmakāraṇatvavādivākyānāṃ sattvātteṣāmanavakāśatvarūpadoṣaprasaṅgāttadanurodhena cetanakāraṇatvaparatayā vedāntā vyākhyeyā iti siddhānta ityasmadrītyā pūrvapakṣasiddhāntāvanūdya tanna, vedāntānāṃ cetanakāraṇatāyāmaikamukhyasya gatisāmānyāt ityatroktatvena samanvayādhyāya upapāditatvena ca bahumukhaśaṅkānudayāditi pūrvapakṣe dūṣaṇamuktaṃ tadata eva nirastam| prasiddhānāṃ vedāntānāṃ yādṛśaka( kā)raṇaparatvamanyeṣāmapi na tadviruddhatvamiti prāguktatve’pyuktarītyā sarveṣāmeva vedanāntānāṃ sāṃkhyācāryoktarītyā na brahmāparatvamiti śaṅkāyāmanupapattyabhāvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- sparśanaśrutivirodhena sarvaveṣṭanādismṛtyaprāmāṇyasya pūrvatantra evoktatveneha smṛtivirodhena śrutyasvārasyaśaṅkānudayācceti pūrvapakṣe dvitīyaṃ dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi na sat| nirṇītatātparyaśrutivirodha eva virodhe tvanapekṣa syāt ithi pūrvatantrīyanyāyapravṛtteḥ pūrvapakṣa evopapāditatvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- asyādikaraṇasya sautrasmṛtiśabdā saṃkocāyāvaidikasmṛtimamātraviṣayatvasaṃbhave tatsaṃkocena kāpilasmṛtimātraviṣayatvoktyayogācceti pūrvapakṣe tṛtīyaṃ dūṣaṇamuktam| tatparihāraśca -- satīṣvadhyātmaviṣayāsu bahuṣu smṛtiṣu sāṃkhyayogasmṛtyoreva nirākaraṇe yatnaḥ kṛtaḥ| sāṃkhyāyogau hi parama puruṣārthasādhakatvena loke prakhyātau| śiṣṭaiśca parigṛhītau| liṅgena ca śrautenopabṛṃhitau| tatkāraṇa sākhyayogābhipanna jñātvādeva mucyate sarvapāśaiḥ iti sāṃkhyasmṛtimātra nirāsanirbandhabījakathanaparaśrīmadbhāṣyadarśināṃ spaṣṭa eva|
yadapi candrikāyām -- mahaddīrghavadvā ityādau kāṇādimataṃ duṣyate , tathā racanānupapatteśca nānumānam ityatra sāṃkhyamatamapīti caturthadūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi na | nirdoṣaśrutimūlakasmṛtuvirodhānnirmūlatvānmahattattvādikāryasmṛteriva tatsadharmatayā kāraṇasmṛteraprāmāṇyasyaitadadhikaraṇaprameyatvena vakṣyamāṇānumānādyaduṣṭapramāṇamūlakatvādamūlatvāsiddhiśaṅkāyāṃ tatsādhyakānumanaduṣṭatvasya racanānupapatteśca ityādhikaraṇaprameyatvena cādoṣāt| ayamatra siddhāntaniṣkarṣaḥ -- tulyabalayorvirodhe niravakāśena sāvakāśasya saṃkoco vaktavyaḥ| na caiva prakṛte’sti| vedānusārismṛtīnāṃ klṛptamūlatayā niścitaprāmāṇyakatvāt| vedaviruddhānāṃ ca sāṃkhyasmṛtīnāṃ mūlaṃ yāvadanumīyate tāvatpratyakṣaśrutivirodhenāprāmāṇyaṃmeva bhramapratāraṇādiklṛptamūla niścīyata iti na mūlaśrutikalpanāvakāśaḥ| tathā ca nāsaṃ prāmāṇyaniścayaḥ saṃbhavatītyuktatarkasyeṣṭapattiśaṅkāparāhatatvānnāsmatpakṣavirodhitā| tadetadanyasmṛtīnāmaprāmāṇyamāpādayatā sūtrakāreṇaiva sūcitam| anyasmṛtīnāṃ prāmāṇyānabhyupagame tāsāmaprāmāṇyāpādanāyogāt| sāṃkhyasmṛteḥ pramāṇaviriddhārthakatvasyāprāmāṇyahetorlābhāt| nanuktaṃ samabalatvaṃ śrutismṛtyoḥ| duruktaṃ tat | śruteḥ prāmāṇāntaraprāpti nirapekṣāyā eva pramāṇatvāt| smṛteśca tatsāpekṣatvāt| kapilādisiddhāḥ pramāṇāntareṇārthamupalabhya tadbuvodhayiṣayā smṛtīḥ prāvācena | anyathā smṛtīnāṃ kathamāptimūlakatvam| kathaṃ vā tadabhāve prāmāṇyam| svataḥ siddhajñāptimūlakatā ca nānapekṣatvabhañjīkā | yato jñapterutpadyamānāyā eva doṣajanyatvaśaṅkāyā aprāmāṇśaṅkāyāḥ prasarāt| nanvevaṃ tārkikavadvedapauruṣeyatvābhyupagamāpattiriti cet| na | yatastārkikā vedaprāmāṇyamāptapraṇītatvānna svata iti manyante| tathā hi sūtram -- tadvācanādvāmnāyaprāmāṇyam iti| idaṃ ca sarvajñeśvarasiddho taduktatayā vedaprāmāṇyasiddhistasyāṃ ca satyāṃ pramāṇavedaprasiddhasarvajñeśvarasiddhirityanyonyāśrayaduṣṭam| aupaniṣadāstu -- utpattau pramāṇyasya svatastvābhyupagamānna tatheti viśeṣāt| na ca nityajñāne’pi bhramatvaṃ śakyaśaṅkam| asati bādhake jñānānāmutyargataḥ prāmāṇyāt| nanvetatsiddhāntaviruddham‌, dṛśyasāmānyasya mithyātvenesvarajñānasyāpi mithyāviṣayatvalakṣaṇabhramatvānapavādatvaditi cet| nahi mithyāviṣayakatvalamātraṃ bhramatvam| idaṃ rajatamanirvācyamasadvā iti jñānasyāpyaprāmāṇyaprasāṅgāt| kituṃ tadviti tadanavagāhitvam| na cānirvācyamanirvācyatvādinā viṣayīkurvato ṅagavajjñānasya tatsaṃbhavaḥ| na ca kapilādijñānasya nityatāyāṃ kiṃcitpramāṇamasti| tasmānnirastasamastadoṣaśaṅkāyā śrutyā tanmūlayā smṛtyā vā na samabalatvaṃ kāpilasmṛteriti na sāvakāśatvaniravakāśatvābhyāṃ vyavasthā yuktā| kiṃca vedaviruddhasākhyāpravaktuḥ kapilasya śrutipratipādyatvaṃ prasiddham| kapilamiti sāmānyaśruteranyasya ca kapilasya sagaraputrāṇāṃ pratapyurvāsudevāmnaḥ sparaṇāt| na ca tāvatāpi kapilaśabdārthasaṃdeha eveti vācyam| ṛṣiṃ prabhūtam iti vacanaṃ śvetāśvatarahagatam| taccadvitīyabrahmāpratipādana eva svarasamiti camasādhikaraṇe nirṇītam| tena cādvaitānuyāyī vāyudevāṃśaḥ kapila eva pratipādyata iti nirdhārayituṃ śakyatvāt| kiṃca yadvai kiṃcana ityākāraka eva pāṭha idānītanavaidikānām|) manuravādattad‌bheṣajam iti manūktimātrasya prāmāṇbodhakaśruterjīgarūkatvena taduktisāvakāśatvakathanamasasaṃgatam| anyathā kiṃcanaṃ itipadavairthyāpatteḥ| manunā tu --
sarvabhūteṣu cātmānaṃ sarvabhūtāni cātmani |
samaṃ paśyannātmayājī svārājyamadhigacchati ||
iti sārvātmyaṃ praśaṃsatā kāpilaṃ mata ninditamiti gamyate|
kapilo sahi na sārvātmyaṃ manyate| ātmābhedānupagamāt| śrutyanurodhinyaḥ smṛtayaśca -- yattatsūkṣmamavijñem iti paraṃ brahma prakṛtya sa hyantarātmā bhūtānāṃ kṣetrajñaśceti kathyate iti cok‌tvā tasmādavyaktamutpannaṃ triguṇaṃ dvijasattam ityāhuḥ| tathānyatrāpi avyaktaṃ puruṣe brahmānniṣkriye saṃpralīyate ityāhuḥ|
ataśca saṃkṣepamimaṃ śṛṇudhvaṃ nārāyaṇaḥ sarvamidaṃ purāṇaḥ|
sa sargakāle ca karoti sargaṃ saṃhārakāle ca tadatti bhūyaḥ||
iti purāṇe | bhagavadgītāsu ca -- 
ahaṃ sarvasya jagataḥ prabhavaḥ pralayastathā || iti|
paramātmānameva ca prakṛtyāpastambaḥ paṭhati -- tasmātkāyāḥ prabhavanti sarve mūlaṃ śāsvatikaḥ sa nityaḥ itīśvarakāraṇatvavādinyaḥ| mahābhārate -- 
bahavaḥ puruṣā rājannutāho eva eva tu | iti vicārya -- 
bahavaḥ puruṣā rājansāṃkhyayogāvicāriṇām||
iti parapakṣamupanyasya taduvyudāsena -- 
bahūnāṃ puruṣāṇāṃ hi yathaikā yonirucyate|
tathā taṃ puruṣaṃ viśvamākhyāsyāmi guṇādhikam|| ityupakramya -- 
mamāntarātmā tava ca ye cānye dehasaṃsthitāḥ|
sarveṣā sākṣibhūto’sau na grāhmaḥ kenacitkvacit||
viśvamūrdā viśvabhujo viśvapādākṣināsikaḥ|
ekaścarati bhūteṣu svairacārī yathāsukham|.
iti sarvātmatāyāṃ ca bhāṣyoktāḥ|| 1||
amulatvācca sāṃkhyāsmṛteraprāmāṇyamāha -- 
itareṣā cānupalabdheḥ|| 2|| iti|
itareṣāṃ pradhānāditareṣāṃ mahadāditattvānāṃ lokavedayoranupalabdheḥ| tathā ca svatantrakapradhānavādinī kāpilasmṛtirapramāṇaṃ mūlaśūnyatvānmahattatvādibodhakakāpilasmṛtivat| mahattattvādayo yathā na śrutyādisiddhāstathopapāditam na saṃkhyopasaṃgrahāto’ ityatra bhāṣyavyākhyānayoḥ| atra nirdoṣaśrutimūlakasmṛtivirodhātsāṃkhyāsmṛteraprāmāṇyaṃ prathamasūtra uktam| dvitīye ca nirmūlatvādaprāmāṇyam| nirmalatvaṃ ca svasamānaviṣayakāduṣṭakaraṇajanyajñānamūlakatvarūpasamūlatvābhāvo prāmāṇye hetuḥ vede vyabhicāravāraṇāya smṛtitvapanyadvā hetuviśeṣaṇamavaseyam|
yattu candrikāyām -- caccoktaṃ paraiḥ pradhānāditareṣāṃ mahadādīnāmanupalabdheśca sākhyamatamayuktamiti itareṣām itisūtrārthamanūdya tanna, na saṃkhyopasaṃgrahāt ityatraiva śrutyādiṣu mahadādīnāmupalabdhe rdarśitatvāditi duṣaṇamuktam| tadapi na sat| nirmūlamahattattvādikāryasmṛtisadharmatayā kāraṇasmṛteraprāmāṇyasyaitatsūtrārthasya dūṣaṇāsparśitvāt| kāryasmṛteraprāmāṇyātkāraṇasmṛteraprāmāṇyaṃ yuktamityābhiprāyake śrīmadbhāṣye niruktasūtrārthasyātispaṣṭatvena darśitatvādanyasminnarthe śaṅkānudayācca|| 2||
etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ|| 3||
atra brahmāvādasākṣādvirodhiyogasmṛtivorodhādadhyāyapādasaṃgatiḥ| saṃśayapūrvapakṣāvāha -- 
yogasmṛtyāsti saṃkoco na vā samanvaye|
dharme vedaḥ sāvakāśaḥ saṃkoco’navakāśayā||
iti saṃśayapūrvapakṣādikaṃ prāguktadiśāvaseyam|
yattu candrikāyām -- yaccoktam vidyāmetāṃ yogāvidhiṃ ca kṛtsnam ityādiśrutyā yogasya parigṛhītatvena na sāṃkhyatulyateyadhikāśaṅkāyām eteneti| tanna| tatkāraṇaṃ sāṃkhyāyogādhigamyaṃ jñātvā devaṃ mucyate sarvapāśai ityādiṣu śrutiṣu sākhyāsyāpi parigṛhītatvenādikāśaṅkābhāvāt| tatra vaidikaṃ jñānameva sāṃkhyāśabdārthaścedihāpi jñānopāya eva yogaśabdārthe’stviti dūṣaṇamuktam| cacchrīmadbhāṣyahṛdayāparīkṣānibandhanam| tathā hi -- yoga viṣaye tāṃ yogam iti vidyāmetāṃ yogavidhiṃ ca ityādinā vaidikāni liṅgānyuktvā yogaśāstre’pi atha tattvadarśanopāyo yogaḥ iti samyagdarśanārthatvenaiva yogo’ṅgī kriyate| ataḥ saṃpratipannārthekadeśatvādaṣṭakādismṛtivadyogasmṛtirapyanapavadanīyā bhaviṣyatīti| iyamabhyadhikāśaṅkātideśena nivartyate iti
| adhikāśaṅkopapādane atha tattvadarśanopāyo yogaḥ 
iti hiraṇyagarbhapraṇītayogaśāstrīyasūtrasya bhāṣye pradarśitatvāddhairaṇyagarbhasmṛtyavaṣṭambhenāyaṃ pūrvapakṣa iti gamyate| tatra ca saṃbhavatyevābhyadhikāśaṅkā| sā cettham -- ṛṣiṃ prasūtaṃ kapilaṃ yastamagre ityādiśrutirna vāsudevāvatārakapilaparā| tasyājānāsiddhyā dhaureyasyetarānugrāhmatvāyogāt| līlāparatvaṃ ca saṃbhavātyāṃ gatāvanucitam| nāpi tantrapraṇetṛkapilaparā| agrepadāsaṃgateḥ| tasya hiraṇyagarbhaputra(para) tvenaiva prasiddheḥ| kiṃtu hiraṇyagarbhaḥ samavartatāgre hiraṇyagarbhaṃ paśyata jāyamānaṃ yo brahmāṇaṃ vidadhāti pūrvam ityādibahuśrutisiddhaprathamajatvādiliṅgapratyabhijñānurodhenaikā kapilaśrutiratiprasiddhamapi tantrapraṇetāraṃ parityajyaṃ prasiddhamapahāyaikakapilavarṇahiraṇyagarbhaparatayā neyā| na ca nirapekṣavedavirodhāduktasmṛteprāmāṇyam| yadvai kicanaṃ manuravadattadbhejam iti śrutyā hiraṇyagarbhavacanamātrasya prāmāṇyoktyanurodhena vedāntānāmeva tadanurodhena vyākhyeyatvāt| anyathā kiṃcanetipadavaiyarthyāpatteḥ| na coktaśrutiḥ prasiddhamanuktīnāmeva prāmāṇyaṃ bodhayati, na hiraṇyagarbhoktīnāmiti kathametāditi śaṅkyam| tasyāṃ manuśabdasya samaṣṭimanobhimānihiraṇyagarbhaparatvāt| tathā hi -- taittirīśākhāstha[sya] mānavī ṛcau dhāyye kuryāt iti vidherhyayamarthavādaḥ -- yadvai kiṃcana manuravadat iti | tadvidhividheyayo[ṛ]coḥ devānāṃ ya inmano yajamāna iyakṣati iti śravaṇādeva manobhimānihiraṇyagarbhaprakāśakatāvagamyate| tathā ca vidhigatasya mānavī itipadasya manuprakāśike ityarthaparatvāt| manupadasya smṛtipraṇetṛmanuparatve tadasaṃbhavāt| vidhipada(gata)sya manugata(pada) sya hiraṇyagarbhaparatve tadaikārthyāya vidhivākyopāttaṛgviśeṣastadānuguṇyena vyākhyeyaḥ| tadarthavādagatamanuśabdasyāpi hiraṇyagarbhaparatvameva yuktam| īśvarapratipādakatvācca vedāntapakṣānusāritve’pyasya hairaṇyagarbhayogaśāstrasya sphuṭam na ca itareṣā cānupalabdheḥ iti nyāyena tannirāsasya ( tannirārāsaḥ) yadvai kiṃcana iti hiraṇyagarbhoktimātraprāmāṇyamabhidadhānāyāḥ śrutereva kāryasmṛtyādeḥ samūlavyavasthāpakatvāt| anyathā kiṃcana itipadavaiyarthyasyoktatvātṭha ityuktābhyadhikā śaṅkā ca parihṛtā bhāṣye -- arthaikadeśasaṃpratipattāvapyarthaideśāvipratipatteriti| siddhāntamāha -- 
prāptāpi yogatātparyādatātparyānna sā pramā|
avaidikapradhānādāvasaṃkocastathāpyataḥ|| iti|
ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- astu tādṛśarcorhiraṇyagarbhaprakāśakatā tathāpi vidhigatasya taddhitapratyayasya na prakāraśyaprakāśakasaṃbandhaparatvam| api tu yadvai kiṃca iti vākyaśeṣānusāreṇa tāsāṃ manunā dṛṣṭamānavītyartha evāśrayaṇīyaḥ | tathā ca hiraṇyagarbhaprakāśakayorapi manusūktāntargatyā manunā dṛṣṭatvasaṃbhavānna klesena manuśabdasya prasiddhamanutyāgena hiraṇyagarbhaparatvaṃ yuktamiti| tathā ca pradhānādyaṃśe pratyakṣavirodhena tadaṃśe yogaśāstrasya tātparyakalpane’saṅgamanirguṇapuruṣam(pra)vrajyādyupadeśena nivṛttiniṣṭhatvādirūpabhāṣyoktāṃśāntaprāmāṇyasya rakṣitumaśakyatayā tadaṃśe yathāśrutārthe tātparyamemava nāstīti kalpyate| tātparyābhāve’pi tatpratipādanaṃ paramātmano’tisūkṣmatayā tasminmahasā cittasya yojayitumaśakyatayā prathama [ta] stadupayogitayā sthūlaviṣayaṃ yogaṃ vaktam| tat --
pūrvapakṣāsamutthānamādye hetorasiddhatā|
dvitīye’bhyadhikā śaṅkānupapattistṛtīyake||
iti candrikākāroktaṃ sarvaṃ parāstamiti dik|
ānandatīrthāyāstu -- smṛtyanavakāśao ityādisūtratrayamekamadhikaraṇaṃ smṛtivirodhamanirāsakam| tatra ghūrṇā(ṇā)kṣaratulyānāṃ vedānāmāptimūlakatvābhāvātsarvajñapraṇītānāṃ ca pāśupatādismṛtīnāṃ ca rudrādipratyakṣamūlakatvena śrutito balavattvāttadanusāreṇaiva vedārtho varṇanīya iti na viṣṇorjagatkāraṇatvaṃ yuktam| pāśupatādismṛtiṣu rudrādereva jagatkāraṇatvokteriti prāpte -- viṣṇavādipratyakṣamūlakapāñcarātrādismṛtīnāṃ samabalatve pratirodhidviṣṇvādyuktasya śrutiṣu saṃvādadarśanena rudrādyapekṣayā tattadviṣṇvādipraṇītatvādadhikabalatve bādhāt| tādṛśasmṛtisacivayā śrutyātitarāṃ bādhācca na pāśupatādismṛtīnāṃ prāmāṇyam| ato noktavedaprāmāṇyena viṣṇorjagatkāraṇatvasamanvayānupapattiḥ| atra ca viśeṣeṇāvaidikanikhilasmṛtipratyekāvalambanena pūrvapakṣā bahavaḥ candrikādyuktāḥ| tathā hi candrikāyām -- atra ca yeṣā pāśupatasakhyādīnāṃ mate vedasyāpauruṣeyatvaprāmāṇye tanmate smṛtyanusāreṇa śrutirneyeti śrutiprāmāṇyena pūrvapakṣaḥ| yeṣāṃ tu kaṇādādīnāṃ mate vedasya pauruṣeyatvaprāmāṇye teṣāṃ kaṇādādīnāṃ smṛteḥ spaṣṭārthatvaṃ sayuktikatvaṃ ceti tatra bījamiti te’pi pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ| bauddhādimate tvāptapraṇītabauddhādyāgamavirodhādāptapraṇīto vedo|pramāṇamityeva pūrvapakṣaḥ| ata eva sudhāyām pāśupatasāṃkhyābauddhārhatādi iti baudhdhādiśabda evamādi | saṃkoce pramāṇābhāvādavaidikatvena saṃkocastu nyāyaprāptatvātsahya ityāhu | tadasat | rudrapraṇītānāmapi parasparaviruddhānāṃ bahunāmāgamānāmupalambhena tatpraṇītānāmapi parasparaviruddhānāṃ bahūnāmāgamānāmupalambhena tatpraṇītāgamāntaraviruddhāyāḥ pāśupatasmṛterāptimūlakatvamāśrityāpauruṣeyaśrutisvārasyabhaṅgasya śaṅkitumaśakyatvāt| kaṇādādisamṛtyavalambī pūrvapakṣo’pi na yuktaḥ| teṣāmapi rudropadeśādeva tādṛśāstrāpaṇetṛtvalasmaraṇāt| kapilakamalāsanayorāptau tu śrutirupadarśitā| nanvevaṃ sāṃkhyāyogayoḥ paraspavirodhādaprāmāṇyaprasaktyā tvanmate’pi kathaṃ pūrvapakṣasiddhiriti cet| āśayānavabodhāt| tathā hi -- ādyādhikaraṇe yogādhikaraṇasiddhāntanyāyena yogasmṛterīśvalarāṃśe nyāye tātparyam| itarāṃśe tu na virodhaḥ| tayorupāyabhūtayogapratipādakatayā manvādismṛtivatsāvakāśateti vā dvitīye’pyādyasiddhantanyāyena prayokturanāptatvāmāśritya vā dvitīye’pyādyasiddhāntanyāyena prayoktaranāptatvāmāśritya pūrvapakṣotthiterapratyūhatvāt| paśupatipraṇītānāṃ ca smṛtīnāṃ parasparaviruddhatvādekataraprāmāṇyanirdhāraṇe kāraṇābhāvāt| tatprāmāṇyavalambanena pūrvapakṣo na yukta ityuktam| pāñcarātrādismṛtiviruddhabauddhādisamṛtyavalambī pūrvapakṣo’pyanayaiva saraṇyā nirastaḥ| bhagavatpraṇītapāñcārātrādismṛtiviruddhāyāstatpraṇītaśākyādismṛterāptimūlatāyā durniśceyatvāditi| rudrādyuktasya rudrāderjagatkāraṇatvādeḥ śrutiṣu yathāsaṃvādastathopapāditaṃ prāgiti jagatkāraṇatvāṃśe pāśupatasmṛtyapekṣayā pāñcārātrāderadhikabalatvāsiddheśca| yaśca itareṣā cānupalabdheḥ iti sūtrasyetareṣāṃ pāśupatasmṛtiṣūktānāmatīndriyebhya itareṣā phalānuṣṭhānāttaduktakarmāniṣṭhānaparityāgayoḥ kṛtayorapi pratyakṣato’nupalabdhestadaṃśe pāśupatādismṛtīmānaprāmāṇyamityartha iti| tanna| vaiguṇyādapi phalānupalambhopapatteḥ| na vilakṣaṇatvāt ityatra kārīryādisthale tvayā tathaiva phalānupalambhasya samarthitatvācca| śrutau prāmāṇyaniścayena tannirvāhāya vaiguṇyaṃ tatra kalpitaṃ mayeti cet| atrāpyaprāmāṇyasyāniścayenotsargataḥ prāptaprāmāṇyarakṣaṇāya tathā kalpayitu yuktatvāt| atrāprāmāṇyaṃ pramāṇāntareṇa niścitamiti cet| tarhi sūtravaiyarthyāt| niścite’pyarthe hetvantarābhidhānaṃ tadaivocitaṃ bhavati yadi prācīnaṃ hetumanupajīvyasya svasādhyasādhānakṣamatvam| tacca na saṃbhavatīti| etena yogaḥ pratyuktaḥ ityatra yogasmṛtau dṛṣṭārthāvisaṃvādātpramāṇyamāśaṅkya prathamaṃ svadenaṃ dvitīyaṃ gātrabhañjanamiti taduktakramānupalambhānna tatprāmāṇyamityarthavarṇanamapi na yuktam| itareṣām ityatra yogyānāṃ phalānāmiva kramasyāpi vivakṣāsaṃbhavena tṛtīyasūtrasyāpi tvanmate vyarthatvāt||
mīmāṃsānyāyapañcāsyarāghavācāryasūriṇām|
śiṣyasyaiṣā kṛtirbhūyādraghunāthasya sanmude||
iti smṛtyadhikaraṇaṃ samāptam|
<3>
atha na vilakṣaṇādhikaraṇm|
na vilakṣaṇatvādasya tathātvaṃ ca śabdāt|| 9||
atra brahmaṇo jagadupādanatvasākṣādvirodhinyayanirāsādadhyāyādisaṃgatiḥ| yaduktaṃ śrutyanurodhismṛtyaprāmāṇyaprasaṅgātkāpilasmṛteraprāmāṇya iṣṭāpattiriti| tadayuktam| kāpilasmṛreva śrutyanurodhitvāt| nahi brahmaṇo viyadādiprakṛtitāvādiśrutivirodhātsaṃkhyāsmṛteraprāmāṇyaṃ saṃbhavati| vilakṣaṇatvāditarkavirodhena śrutibhirbrahmaṇa viyadādiprakṛtitāyā eva bodhayitumaśakyatvāt| smṛtyadhikaraṇapūrvapakṣopaṣṭambhakasaṃkhyācāryoktarītyā brahmaṇi viyadādiprakṛtitābodhakaśrutīnāṃ tātparyasya samarthanīyatvāditi pratyavasthānadanantarasaṃgāti| yadvāmūlānāṃ smṛtīnāṃ samanvayāvirodhitve’pyavyāhatavyāptipakṣadharmatākānāṃ nyāyānāṃ samanvayavirodhitā nirapavādaiveti sā bodhyā| atroktasamanvalayo na yukta uta yukta iti, tadarthaṃ brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvābhāvasādhane vilakṣaṇatvādihetavo duṣṭā utāduṣṭā iti saṃśaye -- pradhānameva sukhaduḥkha mohātmakatvāttādṛśajagadupādānaṃ na tu brahmā| | atādṛśatvāt| sajātīyayoreva prakṛtivikārabhāvātmakopādānopādeyabhāvadarśanāt| atra viśeṣavyāptāvidyā dṛṣṭāntaḥ| yadyadvilakṣaṇaṃ tanna tatprakṛtikamiti sāmānyavyāptau yathā hiraṇyavilakṣaṇo ghaṭo na hiraṇyaprakṛtika iti dṛṣṭānta iti pūrvapakṣastadidamuktam -- -- vilakṣaṇatvāt iti| asya jagato’cetanatvādinā vailakṣaṇyātsādharmyābhāvānna brahmopādānam| yadvāsya brahmaṇo na tatātvaṃ na jagatprakṛtitvam| vilakṣaṇatvāt| tathātvaṃ ca vailakṣaṇyaṃ ca śabdacchruteriti sūtrārthaḥ| śrutiśca vijñānaṃ cāvijñānaṃ cābhavat iti cetanācetanavibhāgena brahmajagatorvailakṣaṇyaṃ bodhayantī bhāṣyodāhṛtā| yattu candrikāyām -- asmadrītyā svīyapramāṇavirodhena parakīyapramāṇāprāmāṇye pūrvatrokte’tra svīyapramāṇāprāmāṇyanirāsaeva yukto na svapakṣe nyāyavirodhaparihāra iti uktarītyā pūrvapakṣe duṣaṇamuktam| tadasat| sāṃkhyektanyāyānāṃ śrutyarthaniṇar‌ya(rṇāya) kānāṃ balavattvasya pūrvaṃ śaṅkitatayā teṣā durbalatvānupapādane sāṃkhsmṛtivirodhanirāsāparyavasānena tannirvāhāya tādṛsanyāyānāṃ daurbalyasyāpyavaśyavaktavyatveoktapūrvapakṣāvaśyakatvāt| anyathā prakrāntasmṛtyavirodhaparīkṣāmaparisamāpya kevalalayuktivirodhena samanvayākṣepacintāyā asaṃgatatvāpatteḥ| svīyapramāṇāprāmāṇyaparihārastvatra na yuktaḥ| vedaprāmāṇyavādibhiḥ kapilakamalāsanādibhistadaprāmāṇyasya śaṅkitumaśakyatvāt| bauddhārhatādīnāṃ pūrvapakṣitve ca tadanabhyupagatena vedaprāmāṇyādīnasiddhikena vedanityatvādinā tatprāmāṇyasādhanasyāśakyatvāt| varṇanityatāyāṃ ca bauddhādyagamānāmapi pramāṇyāpattiḥ| ānupūrvīnityatā ca na vedapramāṇyamantarā setsyati| yuktimātrasya boddhādyamagasādhāraṇyāt| prāmāṇyasya svatastvādinā tatsādhane ca nityatvalarūpavailakṣaṇaṣānusaraṇaṃ tasya śrutisiddhatvakathanaṃ ca vyartham| śrutipramāṇyāyāvaśyānusaraṇīyena svataḥ prāmāṇyenaiva kārīryādiśrutiprāmāṇyasyāpi siddheḥ| na ca tatrāpyasti viṣayabādhaḥ| yena tatrāpavādakasattvamaśaṅkyeta| svarūpayogyatālakṣaṇasya śrutibodhitakāraṇatvasya kkacitkāryānutpattyā virodhābhāvāt| pratyuta vākyanityatāyāmevāsti pratyakṣavisaṃvādaḥ| tasyānyathāsiddhikalpanaṃ ca vedaprāmāṇyamantarā na sukalpanam|
yadapi candrikāyām -- velakṣaṇyādanupādānatvaśaṅkāyāstvanmate dṛśyatvādhikaraṇa eva nirāsāttenāsya punaruktateti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapyasat| tatratyapūrvapakṣasiddhāntopodbalanāya nibandhādau prakṛtapūrvapakṣasiddhāntanyāyopadarśane’pi tasya tadadhikaraṇaprameyatvābhāvāt|
yacca candrikāyām -- kevalopādānasya kāryasālakṣaṇyaniyame’pi tvanmate tantūtpratyūrṇanābhyāderiva jagatprati brahmāvailakṣaṇye’pi siddhāntyabhimatasya nimittatve satyupādānatvasya saṃbhavena pūrvapakṣaḥ siddhāntāviruddha iti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapyasat| ūrṇanābhisthalepyacetanāśasyaṃ tadīyalālāderevopādānātvaṃ buddhereva ca kartṛtvam| tadasaṃsargargāhamātreṇātmanaḥ kartṛtvavyavahāra iti vadataḥ pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ sāṃkhyāsya mate cetanasyācetanopādānatāyāmasaṃpratipatteḥ| madabhyupagamaśca na sāṃkhyārītyā pūrvapakṣānutthitiprayojakaḥ|
yacca candrikāyām -- kiṃca tvanmate’vidyādestadviśiṣṭasya vopādānatvāttasya ca jagatsālakṣaṇyācchuddhasya vilakṣaṇatve’pi jagaddhamādiṣṭhānatvādātmani cānātmādibhramasya sāṃkhyādibhirapi svīkārātkathaṃ pūrvapakṣodaya iti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapyetena parāstam| upādhibhede’pyupādhiviśiṣṭasya śuddhānatiriktatvāttasyopādānatā na yukteti sāṃkhyarītyā pūrvapakṣodayasya bālenāpi subodhatvāt| kāryābhinnakāraṇatārūpamupādanatvaṃ śuddhasyāpi| paraṃ tadvivartopādānatārūpamiti dyotanāya śaddhasya bhramādhiṣṭhānatvamucyate| tādṛśaṃ copādānatvaṃ na sāṃkhyānāmanutam| tairanyathākhyāteravānirvacanīyakhyāterapi niṣedhāt| tathā ca sāṃkhyāsūtrāṇi -- janmādivyavasthātaḥ puruṣabahutvam upādhirbhidyate na tu tadvān upādhirbhidyate na tu tadvān nāvidyāto’pyavastunā bandhāyogātvastutve siddhāntahāniḥ viruddhobhayarūpā cet na tādṛkapadārthapratīteḥ | tatra bhāṣyam -- nanu caitanyaikye’pi tattadrupādhiviśiṣṭasyātiriktatāmabhyupapagamya vyavasthopapādanīyā| tatrāha -- upādhiriti| upādhireva nānā na tu tadvānupādhiviśiṣṭo’pi nānābhyupeyaḥ| viśiṣṭasyātiriktatve nānātmatā sattāyā eva śāstrantare’pyabhyupagamāpatterityarthaḥ| viruddhobhayam -- sadvilakṣaṇamasaddhilakṣamaṇaṃ ceti| kiṃcā nirvacanīyakhyātipakṣe’pi kumbhakācādau sarvatra rajatādhyāsādarśanenādhiṣṭhānāropyayo sālakṣaṇyasyāvaśyakatayā vivartopādānatāpi na brahmaṇaḥ saṃbhavatīti pūrvapakṣiṇaḥ parasya śaṅkātisulabhaiva|
yadapi candrikāyām -- kiṃca mama mate tathātvaśabdoktasyāvaidikasmṛtisālakṣaṇyasya niṣedhyatā| tava tu tadanuktasya brahmākāraṇakatvasya tathā mama vilakṣaṇatvādityatra smṛtyanavakāśeti prakṛtasya parapakṣapramāṇasya pratiyogitā| tava tu tadaprakṛtasya brahmāṇa iti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi śithilam| mama mate’pi pūrvādhikaraṇayorvedāntā jagatkāraṇatvena brahmaparā iti sādhyaghaṭakatvenopasthitasya jagadupādānatvasya tathātvaśabdenopasthitasyaiva niṣedhyatvāt| yadvetyādyuktadvītīyasūtrārthe sādhyaghaṭakakartṛtvopādānatvanirūpakatayā prakṛtasya jagato vailakṣaṇyasya pratiyogitvācca| na coktadvitīyayojanā na bhāṣyakārānumatā| yaduktaṃ cetanaṃ brahma jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ prakṛtiriti tannopapadyate kasmādvilakṣaṇatvādasya vikārasya iti tatra yojanādarśanāditi vācyam| ata āgamavaśenāgamānusāritarkavaśena ca cetanaṃ brahma jagataḥ kāraṇaṃ prakṛtiśca ityetadadhikaraṇopasaṃhārabhāṣyānurodhena tādṛśayojanāyā eva bhāṣyasaṃmatatvāt| jagato na brahma prakṛtikatvamiti prathamopadarśitopakramabhāṣyānusāriyojanāyāmapi na kṣatiḥ| janmādyasya yataḥ iti sūtra ivātrāpīdaṃśabdena pratyakṣādisiddhajagatparāmarśasaṃbhavāt| paramaprakṛtasya brahmaṇo vailaṇyapratiyogitvasaṃbhavāt| brahmaniṣṭhopādānatānirūpakatvasya pūrvādhikaraṇayoḥ sādhyaghaṭakatvenopasthitasya tathātvaśabdena parāmarśasaṃbhavācca| tvanmate paraṃ smṛti śabdena strīliṅgenopasthitāyāḥ pāñcarātrādismṛteḥ sarvanāmnā śrutibubodhayiṣāyām nātmāśruternityatvācca tābhyaḥ ityatra strīliṅgenaiva tena nirdiśyamānāyāḥ śruteścātathābhūtenedaṃśabdena parāmarśo na svarasaḥ|
yadapi candrikāyām -- jagato brahmavailakṣaṇyasya pratyakṣasiddhatvāttatra śabdāt iti śabdoktirayukteti dūṣaṇamuktam| tatparihāraśca bhāṣyadarsināmatispaṣṭa eveti bhāṣyādarśanamūlakameva tat| tathā hi -- prasiddhaścāyaṃ cetanācetanāvibhāgo loke| tasmādvrahmavilakṣaṇatvānnedaṃ jagad brahmāprakṛtikam iti na vilakṣaṇatvāt iti sautrabhāgaṃ vyākhyāya yo’pi kaścidācakṣīta śrutvā jagataścetanaprakṛtikatāṃ tadbalenaiva samastaṃ jagaccetanamavagamiṣyāmi| prakṛtirūpasya vikāre’nvayadarśanāt| avibhāvanaṃ tu caitanyasya pariṇāmaviśeṣādbhāniṣyati| yathā spaṣṭacaitanyānāmapyātmanāṃ svāpamūrchādyavasthāsu caitanyaṃ na vibhāvyata evaṃ kāṣṭhaloṣṭādīnāmapi caitanyaṃ na vibhāvayiṣyate iti śaṅkāviṣanirākāraṇaparatayā tādṛśasautrabhāgasya anavagabhyāmānameva hīdaṃ loke samastasya vsatunascetanatvam| cetanaprakṛtikatvaśravaṇācchabdaśaraṇatayā kevalayotprekṣyate| tacca śabdenaiva virudhyate iti vyākhyātatvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- kicaṃ yadi vijñānaṃ cāvijñānaṃ ca iti śrutirvailakṣaṇye pramāṇam| kathaṃ tarhyupādānatve na pramāṇam| sacca tyaccābhavat iti pūrvavākyenopādanatvapratīteḥ| api ca vijñānāvijñānaśabdau na kāryakāraṇaparau | kiṃtu tvatpakṣe sattyadādiśabdavatkāryaparāveva| caśabdābhyāṃ pratyekaṃ bhavanānvayapratīteriti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi na sat| vijñānaṃ cetanaṃ nirdiśya tataḥ pārthakyenāvijñānanirdeśe kṛte cetanapadārthapratisaṃbandhī tato’nyo bādhakābhāvādacetanaḥ sidhyati| upādānatāyāṃ ca nirvikaraśrutirūpabādhakasadbhāvādupādānatvaśrutirgauṇī bhavatīti viśeṣasya śaṅkāyāṃ saṃbhavāt| ata evāpi cetyādyapi nirastam| cetanātiriktavastusiddhimātreṇa prakṛtapūrvapakṣasaṃgatyā vijñānā vijñānaśabdayoḥ kāryakāraṇaparatāyā asptabhirapyanāśrayaṇāt| kāraṇasya cetanatā tu brahmāvādyanumatā satyaṃ jñānam ya sarvajñaḥ sarvavit ityādiśrutisiddhā ceti|
