na ca jalasya jalāntaramelana aikyam| tādṛgeva iti bhāṣyoktasāvadhāraṇaśrutyā yato vṛddhiḥ pradṛśyate iti smṛtyā ca virodhāt‌| kiṃca -- 
mānabhedātsparśarasapūrvabhedācca nīrayoḥ|
melane’pyabhidā nāsti yathaiva kṣīranīrayeḥ||
na kevalaṃ yato vṛddhiḥ iti smṛtyuktaṃ parimāṇabhedamātram| kiṃtu śītoṣṇayoḥ sparśarasayoḥ| tattatsaritsaṃbandhinosca jalayormelane sparśādibhedo’pi dṛśyate| anyathā haṃsena vivicyamānayoḥ kṣīranīrayorapyabhedaḥ syāt| drutasuvarṇatāmrayormelanenābhede tatkāryapaṇādau kevalasvarṇakāryapaṇānmūlyādibhedasca na syāt| kṣīre siktā surāpi kṣīraṃ syāt| prasiddhasca kṛṣṇādisaptanadījalānāṃ gaṅgadisahastrajalānāṃ ca punaḥ pratyekaṃ samudrasaṃgaṃmārtha vibhaktānāṃ vilakṣaṇaruparasādimattayā bhedaḥ| kiṃca avasthiteriti kāśakṛtstraḥ iti sūtre bhāmatyāmeva -- kā tu nadyābhimatāyuṣmataḥ| kiṃ pāthaḥ paramāṇava uttaiṣāṃ saṃsthānabheda āho tadavayavārabdho’vayavī | tatra saṃsthānabhedasyāvayavino vā samudrapraveśena nāśatkathaṃ samudreṇaikatā| nadīpāthaḥ paramāṇūnāṃ tu samudrapātha paramāṇubhyaḥ pūrvasthitebhyo bheda eva nābhedaḥ| evaṃ samudrādapi teṣāṃ bheda evetyādirītyā nadīsamudrāyorabhedo nirastaḥ| evaṃ ca -- 
māṣādivacca rāśyādau sabhādau brāhmaṇādivat|
jale jalantarasyāpi saṃśleṣo na tvabhinnatā||
dvavadravyatvenānyonyamiśraṇe tu bhedāpratītiriti candrikoktaṃ sarvaṃ nirastaprāyameveti dik|| 13||
śrīmacchaṃkarapādābjabhūṣaṇe paradūṣaṇe|
vyākhyādvayāvivekena raghunāthakṛtiḥ satām||
bhoktrādhikaraṇe bhūyānmude vādijayāya ca|
mīmāṃsānyāyapārīṇarāghavāryaprasādajā||
iti bhoktrāpattyādhikaraṇaṃ samāptam||
<6>
athārambhaṇādhikaraṇam|
tadananyatvamārambhaṇaśabdadiśyaḥ|| 14||
pūrvoktabhoktṛbhogyavibhāgabhāvaprasaṅgapūrvapakṣe syāllokavat iti pariṇāmavādamāśritya samādhānamuktatvā tatraiva vivartavādena mukhyasamādhānārthamidaṃ sūtram| tathā caitatsūtravyākhyānopakrame bhāṣyam -- abhyupagamya cemaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ bhoktṛbhogyalakṣaṇaṃ vibhāgam syollokavat ithi parihāro’bhihitaḥ| na tvayaṃ vibhāgaḥ paramārthato’stītyādi| etatsūtravyākhyānopasaṃhāre’pi vakṣyati bhagavānbhāṣyakāraḥ -- sūtrakāro’pi paramārthābhiprāyeṇa tadananyatvam ityāha| vyavahārābhiprāyeṇa tu syāllokavat iti mahāsamudrasthānīyatāṃ brahmaṇaḥ kathayati| apratyākhyāyaiva kāryaprapañcaṃ pariṇāmapakriyā cāśrayati saguṇopāsaneṣūpayokṣyata iti| tatrābhyupagamyetyasya mithyātvenāvyutpādyetyarthaḥ| pariṇāmetyādeḥ pariṇāmitvapakriyā dṛṣṭāntatvenāśrayati| pariṇāmavādasādhāraṇadṛṣṭāntamamāheti tu phalito’rthaḥ| bhāṣye mahatā prabandhenāsminnadhikaraṇe prapacamithyātvopapādanaṃ ca brahmaṇaḥ pratyagabhinnatvaṃ na saṃbhavati| kartṛtvākartṛtvādiviruddhadharmayorekatra samāvesāsaṃbhavāt| viruddhadharmāṇaṃ dṛśyatvādinā mithyātvasiddhernāyaṃ doṣa iti tu na samyak| prabalapratyakṣavirodhe jāguke durbalena liṅgena mithyātvāsiddheḥ iti śaṅkānirāsāya|
kecittu -- pūrvasūtre lokavat ityukte loke vikārānṛtatvāprasiddhervikārasatyatāmabhyupetyaiva parihāro darśita iti bhramaḥ syāt| tathaupādhiko’pi jīvabrahmaṇorbheda upādhisatyatā pāramārthikaḥ syāditi bhramaḥ syāditi tadapākaraṇāyāsminsūtre vikāramithyātvapratipādanamiti vadanti|
nanvevaṃ mithyātvopapādanasaṃgatāvapyāsmitsūtre abhyupagamyacemam iti bhāṣyeṇa mithyātvātyutpādanakīrtanasya ka upayoga iti cet| atra brūmaḥ -- mithyātvopapādanaṃ vinā pūrvasūtrasiddhāntānunmeṣeṇaitadadhikaraṇasya tādṛśamithyātvopapādakasya taccheṣatvāpattyādhikaraṇāntaratvānāvaśyakatā syāditi śaṅkāyāṃ tadāvaśyakatādyotanāya mithyātvopapādanaṃ vināpi pūrvādhikaraṇasiddhāntasaṃbhavapradarśanāya tatkīrtanasyātra sāmañjasyāt| yadvā noktaṃ brahamaṇo jagadupādanatvaṃ yuktam| siddhāntyabhimatanirivikāratvabhaṅgaprasaṅgaditi śaṅkāyāṃ vikāramithyātvopapādanamatra kriyate svapakṣasākṣādbādhakanirāsasya pādārthatvācca nāsaṃgatiḥ| tathā coktākṣepasaṃgatipradarśanāya bhāṣye taduktiḥ| pūrvādhikaraṇa eva tadvayutpādane ca na tatsaṃgatiḥ saṃbhavatītyupayuktamevābhyupagamyetyanena mithyātvāvyutpādanakīrtanamiti dhyeyam| atra coktarītyā svapakṣasākṣādvirodhino virodhidharmasatyatvāderbādhakasyoddhārādadhyāyādisaṃgatiḥ| tādātmyasya bhedāviruddhatvādbhodaktrādivibhāgānupapattirupatarkasyāpratiṣṭhitatve’pi śrutyanugṛhītasya nirvīkāratvānupapattirupatarkasyaiva pratiṣṭhitatvamiti pratyavasthānādanantarasaṃgātiḥ| atra brahmaṇo jagadupādānatvaṃ nirvikāratvabhahgāpattirupapatarkaviruddha na veti saṃśayebhavatyeva tarkaviruddham| brahmaṇo nirvikāratvasya siddhāntino’pyanumatatayeṣṭāpattyanavakāśānmūlaśaithilyābhāvācceti pūrvapakṣe -- noktatarkavirodhaḥ| dharmisamānasattākavikāraśūnyatvarupāsiddhāntyabhimatanirvikāratvasya brahmāgatopādānatayāvirodhādvikārasya mithyātvādbrahmaṇa satyatvāt| svīkṛte’pyūpādānatve nirvikārasvabhaṅgābhāvāt| mithyātvaṃ cānirvācyamiti| kāryasya vikārajātasya tadananyatvaṃ kāraṇānanyatvam| mithyātvamiti yāvat| kutaḥ| ārambhaṇaśabdādibhya | ārambhaṇaśabdastāvat -- eka -- vijñānena sarvavijñāna dṛṣṭāntāpekṣāyāmucyate -- yathā saumyaikena mṛtpiṇḍena sarvaṃ mṛnmayaṃ vijñātaṃ syādvācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheya mṛttiketyeva satyam ityetrarupaḥ| ādipadāt etedātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tatsatyaṃ sa ātmā tattvamasi idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā brahmaivedaṃ sarvam ātmaivedaṃ sarvam neha nānāsti kiṃcana ityevamidiśrutijātam viśvamithyā dṛśyatvātpiracchinnatvāt ityādyanumānaṃ bhāṣyādyupadarśita saṃgāhyam| atra ca ārambhaṇaśabdadibhyaḥ iti sūtrāṃśena vācārambhaṇam ityādikaśruterhetūkaraṇāt| tasyāśca prapañcamithyātvakathana eva svarasādananyatvapadena sādhyatayā mithyātvameva pratīyata iti dhyeyam|
atha tadananyatvapadena kathaṃ mithyātvabhatītiḥ| tatra tasya śakatyādigrāhakapramāṇābāvādādhunikalakṣaṇābhyupagame ca sūtrākṣarāsvārasyam mithyātvamārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ iti vinyāsopekṣāyāṃ nirbūjatā ceti cet| atrocyate -- tadananyatvam iti sūtre tattvata iti padamadhyāhriyate| ananyatvapadaṃ cāvartanīyam| tathā ca tadananyatvapadaṃ tattavatastadananyatvaparaṃ tādātmyaparaṃ ca | tadananyatvamityatrānyapadasyātyantabhinnārthakatayā tādātmyasyāpi tato lābhasaṃbhavāt| tadaikya śūnyena tadatyantābhāvamātraṃ tādātmyam| pṛthivītadrabapayorbhedābhedavādino mate bhedavati bhedātyantābhāvāsaṃbhavāt| kiṃtu tasmādanyadeva tādātmyam| tacca tadgatadharmaviśeṣarupamatiriktaṃ vetyanyadetat| tattvatasyatadananyatvaṃ śeṣābhyānujñānārthatvādbrahyānyajagadrūpasyātāttvikatā michyātvāparaparyāyā labhyata iti noktadoṣaḥ|na ca tattvastadananyatvasādhanamātreṇa jagato mithyātvasiddhyoktapūrvapakṣanirāsasaṃbhave tādātmyasādhanaṃ vyarthamiti śaṅkyam| tucchasādhāraṇena brahmavyātiriktatāttvikarupaśūnyatvena jagato mithyātva upagate tasya tucchatvāpattyā tadupādānatvaṃ brahmaṇo durghaṭamiti śaṅkānirāsāya tādātmyasādhanaṃ vyarthamiti śaṅkyam| tucchasādhāraṇena brahmavyatiriktatāttvikarupaśūnyatvena jagato mithyātva upagate tasya tucchatvāpattyā tadupādānatvaṃ brahmaṇo durghaṭamiti śaṅkānirāsāya tādātmyasyāpi tatra vaktavyatvāt| usatkhyātimate ghaṭātiriktatāttvikarupaśūnyasyāpi śuktirajatāderghaṭajñānena jñātatāttvikarupatvasaṃpratipattyā viyadāderbrahyavijñānena vijñātatāttvikarupavattvarupasya vakṣyamāṇaikavijñānena sarvavijñānasyopapādanāyogena brahmaṇo viyadāditattvarupatābodhanāyatayostādātmyasyāvaśyaṃ vācyatvācca| ananyatvaṃ vyatirekeṇābhāvaḥ iti bhāṣye’pi niruktarupamubhayaṃ vivakṣitam| na khalvananyatvamityabhedaṃ brūmaḥ| kiṃtu bhedaṃ vyāsedhāmaḥ iti bhāmatyāmapi, abhedabhaikyaṃ bhedaṃ tadanyatāttvikarupavattā tattādātmyāsamānadhikaraṇatadbhedavattāṃ cetyartho vivakṣita iti na kācidanupapattiḥ| etena -- na tāvadrabrahmābhedastadananyatvam| anṛtasya viśvasyaṃ brahmābhedāyogāt| anaṅagīkārācca| anaṅgīkārācca | ajñānaviśiṣṭenopādānenābhedo’stīti cet| mumukṣujñeyaśuddhabrahmājñānādeva sarvavijñānārthaṃ śuddhenaivābhedasya vaktavyatvāt| anyathāvidyāpi yenāśrutaṃ śrutam itivākyoktajñeyakoṭipraviṣṭā syāt| nāpi na khalvananyatvamityabhedaṃ bramaḥ| kiṃtu bhedaṃ vyāsedhāmaḥ iti bhāmatyuktarītyā tato bhedābhāvamātraṃ tadananyatvam| bhedābhāve’bhādadhrauvyāt| bhedābhāve satyānṛtavyavasthāyogācca| abhedābhāvena vyavasthāyāṃ tenaivaikavijñānena sarvavijñānabhāvo’pi syāt| yadi caikavijñānena sarvavijñānānupapattyā bhedābhāvaḥ satyānṛtavyavasthānupapattyā cābhedābhāvaḥ kalpyate, tarhyabhayānupapattyā lāghavāya tathā idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā neti neti ityādisāmānādhikaraṇyaśrutibhedaśrutyīrmṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣṭāntasya ca svārasyāya bhedābhedāveva ki na kalpyete| brahmaṇa ekarasatvaśrutirapyanekarasātprapañcadbhedābhāve’pyabhedābhāvamātreṇa bhede’pyabhedenaivopapannāstu | yathā ca satyasyānṛtābhedo na yuktastathā tato bhedābhāve’pi| nāpi brahmavyatirekeṇāsattvaṃ tadananyatvam| viśeṣaniṣedhasya śeṣābhyanujñānārthatvādānandādivajjīvacaitanyavacca brahmātmakatvāpātāt| brahmaṇapāropitatva tu na tadananyatvaśabdārthaḥ| śaktyāropite rupye tadananyaśabdāpayogāt| prātibhāsikavyā vahārikayorvyāvahārikapāramārthikābhyāmanyatvābhāve’nirvācyatvāsiddhesca| ekavijñānena sarvavijñānāyogācca | mṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣṭāntānupapatteśca| kathaṃ hi brahmavijñānādvijñātaṃ sakalaṃ bhavet ithi candrikoktaṃ pūrvoktabhāṣyābhipretārthānavabodhavijṛmbhitamityanādaraṇīyameva| ārambhaṇaśabdādibhyaḥ iti sūtrāṃśena vācāmbhaṇam ityādiśruterhetūkāraṇāttasyāśca prapañcimithyātvakathana eva svarasāduktarītyā tādātmyaghaṭitamithyātvasyaiva tadananyatvamityanena vivakṣitārthapratīteḥ yadrajatamabhātsā śuktiḥ iti sāmānādhikaraṇyapratīteḥ sarvasaṃmatatvādāropite’pi tattvato’dhiṣṭhānānanyaśabdaprayogāvirodhātprātibhāsikavyāvahārikayorapi tattvato vyāvahārikapāramārthikābhyāmanyatvābhāvenānirvacanīyatāyā niṣpratyūhaṃ siddheścoktacandrikākāroktasya kasyāpi doṣasyānavakāśāt| atha -- vācārambhaṇam ityādiśruteḥ prapañca satyatāmupetyāpyarthavarṇanasaṃbhavātkathaṃ tasyāḥ prapañjimithyātvakayathana eva svarasaḥ| tathā hi kecidvarṇayanti -- ekavijñānena sarvavijñānīktyā kāryakāraṇayorabheda upākṣite piṇḍe dṛṣṭe’pi ghaṭādigocaraḥ saṃśayaḥ piṇḍaghaṭayorarthakriyābhedo nāmādibhedaśca kathamupapanna iti śaṅkāyām vācārampaṇam iti vākyaśeṣaḥ| vācā ghaṭena jalamāhara ityādivāk| tatpūrvako vyavahāraśca vākpadārthaḥṣa| tadartham adhyāyanena vasati ityādāviva phalasyāpi hetutvavivakṣayā tṛtīyā| vikāro ghaṭādistannāmadheyaṃ ca mattikayārambhaṇaṃ spṛśyaṃ saṃbandhanīyamiti yāvat| ālambhaḥ sparśahiṃsayoḥ iti vacanāt| sparśārthakasyālabhateḥ saṃbandhe paryavasānam| karmaṇi lyuṭ| mṛttiketyenetyatra vidyamānasyānumānayetyādyabhidhānarupavyāvahārarthaṃ tanmūlānayanādivyavahārārtha taca mṛttikayā kambugrīvādisaṃsthānaṃ ghaṭa ityādi nāmadheya ca saṃbadhyata iti pratipādite piṇḍaghaṭayorekadravyatve’pyavasthānābhedātsaṃdehādikaṃ sarvamupapadyata ityuktaṃ bhavati| nanu dravyaikyamupetyāvasthābhedātsadehādikaṃ kimityupapādyate tārkikādivatkāryakāraṇadravyayorbheda eva kuto nopeyata ityāśaṅkyoktam mṛttikettheva satyam iti| ghaṭa iti padamadhyāhṛtya ghaṭo mṛttiketyevārthaḥ| satyaṃ prāmāṇika iti yāvat| ghaṭo mṛttiketi lokasiddhapratyābhijñayābhedo’vagamyate| ato dravyaikyamādāyaiva vyavahārādikamupapādanīyamiti| naitatsvārasikam| ekamarthe varṇyamāne vāceti tṛtīyāyāścaturthyarthatvāṅgīkāro mṛttiketyanuṣaṅgastṛtīyāntatayā tasya vipariṇāmo mṛktiketyevetyatra ghaṭapadādhyāhāra śceti rītyā yojanāyā atikliṣṭhatvāt|
yadapi -- vikāro ghaṭaśarāvādiḥ mṛdo bhinnaḥ iti vācārambhaṇaṃ vāṅmātreṇa vyavahriyate na beda iti bhāvaḥ| ghaṭādi nāmadheyaṃ ghaṭādi dravyaṃ mṛktiketi kṛtvaiva satyam| sati prāmāṇike’rthe sādhu| tatra sādhuḥ iti yatapratyayaḥ| tathā ghaṭapiṇḍādināmavyavasthāvasthābhedānusāreṇaiva kartavyā na tadvaśena kāryakāraṇadravyabheda āvastheya(āstheya) ithi paryavasito’rtha iti vyākyānam| tadapi vācārambhaṇam ityatra mṛdo bhinnaḥ iti padādhyāhārānnāmadheyapadasya ghaṭadiparatvāṅgīkāreṇa saṃkocakalpanānmṛttiketyatra ghaṭādi ,dravyam iti padādhyāhārācca na svarasam|
yadapi -- vācā vāgindriyamātreṇārambhaṇaṃ yasya tannāmadheyaṃ vikāraḥ| saṃskṛtāpabhraśarupeṇa vikriyāmāṇatvāt | vyākaraṇasāpekṣaṇa vāgindriyeṇa niṣpādyaṃ mṛttiketyeva satyaṃ nityam| pravāharupeṇānidītyarthaḥ| na vadhenāsya hanyate| etatsatyaṃ brahmapuram| ityatra satyapadasya nitye prayogāt| sadāśabdasya tyabantasya satyapadatvena sadātanārthakatvācca| ekavijñānena sarvavijñānaṃ hi sādṛśyātprādhānyādvā | tatraikasya brahmaṇo jñānena sattvādinā tatsadṛśasya jñāne mṛtpiṣḍamṛnmayā dṛṣṭāntāḥ| pradhānasya tasya jñānena tadguṇībhūtasya sarvasya jñāne saṃskṛtāprabhaṃśo dṛṣṭāntaḥ| apabhraṃśajñānāddhi yatphalaṃ tattadadhikaṃ ca sārvatrikavyavahārādikaṃ saṃskṛtajñānādbhavatīti saṃskṛtaṃ pradhānam| tādṛśaguṇapradhānadṛṣṭāntatālābhārthaṃ ca vācārambhaṇam ityādāvapi yathā saumyaikaina ityādikamanuṣañjanīyamiti rītyānandatīrthīya vyākhyānam| tadapyasvarasam| tathā hi -- vācetyanena vāgindriyamātraṃ lakṣaṇīyam| anyathā vyākaraṇaniṣpādyatvālābhenāpabhraśālābhāpatteḥ| manmate vācetyasya vāṅmātramityarthe nāmadheyamityatra nāmadheyamātramityarthe lakṣaṇā tu śrutasāmānādhikaraṇyāmupapattinibandhanā lokasiddhā ceti vakṣyate| satyaśabdasyābādhye ruḍhatvānnityatvārthakatvamapi lakṣaṇayaiva | saṃskṛtasyāpi mukhyanityatvābhāvena sadāśabdamādāya yogāsaṃbhavena vyākaraṇāniṣpādyatvarupagauṇanityatve lakṣaṇāpattiśca| sadāśabdasya tyabantatvamapi vyāraṇāsiddhameva | vikāraśabdasya mukhyavikārārthakatve tvadiṣṭārthāsiddhyā saṃskṛtoccāraṇāśaktyoccāryamāṇārthakatvaṃ lakṣaṇayaiva| nāmapadasyāpi śabdārthakatvaṃ lakṣaṇayaiva| apabhraśasya saṃketavatprātipādikaruparuḍhyarthatvābhāvāt| yathā saumyaikena ityādikamanuṣañjanīyamityapi na sat| pūrvavākyapraviṣṭābhāgasyottaravākyaghaṭakatvena pratisaṃdhāna hyanuṣaṅgaḥ| sa ca prakṛte na saṃbhavati| prakṛte hi mṛttiketyeva satyam ityādeḥ paścāt yathā saumyaikena mṛttiketyanena sarvastadvikāro vijñāto bhavati ityādi hi vivakṣitadṛṣṭāntalābhāya kalpanīyam| na caitatpūrvavākye praviṣṭam| kiṃca yathā vācārambhaṇam ityādinā guṇapradhānabhāvaḥ śrutyā jñāpitastathā mṛtpiṇḍajñāne’pi tadvikārāṇāṃ ghaṭatvādinā jñānāsaṃbhavānmṛttvena rupeṇaiva ghaṭādijñānaṃ mṛtpiṇḍajñānāditi paryālocanāttatsadṛśajñānaṃ tajjñānāghīnamiti lakṣyata ityucyate tarhi saṃskṛtajñānādapabhraṃśajñānāsaṃbhavātsaṃskṛtasya prākṛtakāryakāritvatātparyakam mṛttiketyanena sarvastādvikāro vijñāto bhavati iti vākye saṃbhavatīti tadeva śrutyocyeta, kim vācārambhaṇam ityādinā guṇapradhānabhāvajñāpakeneti śrutisvārasyahāniḥ| api ca kārṣṇāyasāntānāsadṛśadṛṣṭāntānāṃ paścādeva vācārambhaṇaṃ nāmadheyaṃ vikāra itivat saṃskṛta satyam iti sāmānyata evocyate (cyeta) , na tu mṛtpiṇḍādidṛṣṭāntasyaikaikasyānte viśiṣya mṛttikā ityekaikam| śabdāntarāṇāmapi saṃskṛtānāṃ saṃbhavena mṛttiketyevetyāderupapādanamapyasvarasam| āyasamityeva satyam ityasyaiva pūrṇatvasaṃbhave kārṣṇeti kimityuktamiti śaṅkāpyanuttaraiva tvanmate| manmate tu kārṣṇāyasādvācakalaghuśabdāntarasaṃbhave’pi na doṣaḥ| ghaṭādyarthavivakṣāyāṃ ghaṭādiśabdānāmiva kalaśādiśabdānāmapi tatparyāyāṇāṃ prayogāt| ekavijñānena tatsadṛśaṃ tadadhīnaṃ ca sarvaṃmapi jñāyata ityapyasaṃgatam| tathā niyamābhāvāt| nahi gojñāne gavayāde rājajñāne ca prajīderniyataṃ jñānam| aniyatajñāne ca sadṛśadereva kimityupāda(dā)namityatrānuttarameveti dik|
evaṃ matantare prapañcasatyatāmupetya vācārambhaṇaśruterarthapratipādane’svārasyāmevetyataḥ prapañjamithyātvaparatvenaivālasmadbhāṣyopadarśitaṃ vyākhyānaṃ svarasam| tathā hi bhāṣyam -- ekena mṛtpiṣḍena paramārthato mṛdātmanā vijñātena sarvaṃ mṛnmayaṃ ghaṭaśarāvodañcanādikaṃ mṛdātmatvāviśeṣādvijñātaṃ bhavet| yato vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāronāmadheyam| vācaiva kevalamastītyārabhyate vikāro ghaṭaḥ śarāva udañjanaṃ ceti| na vastuvṛttena vikāro nāmakaścidasti| nāmadheyamātraṃ hyetadanṛtamṣa| mṛttiketyeva satyamiti|
nanu nāmadeyamityatra mātrapadārthāntarbhāveṇānṛtatvaparyavasāyi nāmadheyapadavyākhyānaṃ kathaṃ svarasāmīti cet| atrocyate -- vācā vyavahniyamāṇaṃ vikāram vācārambhaṇam iti vākyoditamanūdya nāmadheyatvavidhānaṃ hyatraṃ svarasataḥ pratīyate| tatra ca śrutārthamādāya sāmānādhikaraṇyāsaṃbhavānnāmadheyapade nāmadheyavācyatvalakṣaṇāmupetya sāmānādhikaraṇyopapadāne paunarukttyāpatteḥ mṛttiketyeva satyam itivākyaseṣasvārasāccarthaparisaṃkhyārkhathamupetya mātrapadantarbhāveṇa mithyātvaparyavasāyi tadarthakathanasyaiva svarasatvāt| dṛṣṭaṃ ca nāmapadasyābhāsakadyotakatvam -- yajante nāmayajñaiste dambhenāvidhipūrvakam iti| loke’pi putrābhāse putra iti nāmadheyam, nātra pūtratvamastīti| mṛttiketyeva satyam iti tu guḍhajihvikānyāyena mṛdaḥ satyatvānuvādapūrvakaṃvikārānṛtatāpratipādanāyaiva svarasam| amumarthaṃ dārṣṭāntike darśayati -- evaṃ hi saumya sa ādeśo bhavati iti| evaṃśabdena vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāraḥ, ādeśa eva satya iti pratipādita bhavati| ādeśaścādiśyata ityādeśa iti vyutpattyā uta tamādeśamaprākṣyo yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati ityupakrāntamupadeśaikagamyaṃ brahyaiva| itthameva cāsminprākaraṇe taijasānāṃ padārthānā travṛtkaraṇānirupaṇāvasare spaṣṭameva pratipāditam -- yadagre rohitaṃ rupaṃ cejasastadrūpaṃ yacchuklaṃ tadapāṃ yatkṛṣṇaṃ tadannasyāpāgādagneragnitacva vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ trīṇi rupāṇītyeva satyam iti| atra apāgādegneragnitvam iti spaṣṭameva vikāropamārdo darśitaḥ| ataḥ svarasato vācārambhaṇaśrutervikārānṛtatvaparataivetyavadhāryate| yadvā mṛttiketyeva satyam ityādau hetuḥ vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyam iti ārambhaṇamutpattirvācā vāṅmātraṃ vicārāsahamiti yāvat| tathā cotpattiḥ sato vāsato veti nirupayitumaśakyatvādutpannasāmānyaṃ vicārāsahamiti bhāvaḥ| tāvatāpyanādyavidyāsādhāraṇyena hetvalābhādāha -- vikāro nāmadheyamiti | nāśādivikṛtiyuktaṃ nāmamātraṃ vicārāsahamiti yāvat| arthe nāmābhedāsaṃbhavāt abbhakṣaḥityādāviva mātrārthāntarbhāvena(ṇa) vicārāsahatvalābhaḥ| muktiketyeva satyam ityāsyārthaścandrikoktadūṣaṇaparihārāvasare vakṣyate| evam etedātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tatsatyaṃ sa ātmā tattvamasiśvetaketo’ iti śruterapi vikārānṛtapratipādana eva svārasyam| tathā hi -- aitādātmyamidaṃ sarvam iti hi idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā iti śrutyantarānusārāt sadeva saumyedamagra āsīt ityupakramānusārācca eva ātmāsya iti bahuvrīhiṃ kṛtvā tato bhāvapratyaye kṛte bāvapratyayasya prakārībhūtaprakatyarthaparatvādbāhuvrīhyarthe viśeṣaṇībhūtātmaparāmarśakatvena eṣa ātmā idaṃ sarvam iti pratipādayati| eṣa ātmā idaṃ sarvam ityeva vaktavye sāmānādhikaraṇyamātmāmīyabhāvenaupavārikamitiśaṅkānirākaraṇāya bahuvrīhibhāvapratyayorupādānat| tathā sati bahuvrīhiṇaiva mukhyayā vṛttyātmātmīyabhāvelabdhe punarbhāvamatyayarupayatnāntarakaraṇaṃ vyartha syāditi tarkeṇa tannirāsaḥ| tacca sāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ mṛdghaṭa itiravatpariṇāmābhiprāyeṇa yadrajatamabhātsā śaktiḥ itivadvāropitabādhābhiprāyeṇa veti dvedhā saṃbhavati| atastānnirghāraṇāya tatsatyam iti vākyam| tatra hi tacchabdaḥ prakṛtamātmānaṃ parāmṛśati| tasyaiva tātparyaviṣayatayāsminsaṃdarbhe prādhānyāt| sa ātmā iti vākye tasyaiva parāmarśāt| tadaikārthānusārācca| tataśca tacchabdenāṣanṛtatā bodhayatpūrvopadarśitasāmānādhikaraṇyaṃ bādhāyāmeveti vyavasthāpayati| tataśca tattvamasi iti vākyamapi svasvārasyāpanusāreṇopakramasvārasyānusāreṇa ca yathāśrutameva| na tu -- brahmaṇe tvāmahasa omityātmānaṃ yuñjīta iti śruteḥ tasmai tvaṃ samarpaṇīyo’si ityarthe tasmai tvaṃ tattvam iti yato vā imāni bhūtāni jāyante iti śrute tasmāttvamutpanno’si ityarthe tasmāttvaṃ tattvam iti, sa kāraṇaṃ kāraṇādhipādhipaḥ iti śruteḥ tatsvāmiko’si ityarthe tasya tvaṃ tattvam iti, sanmūlāḥ saumyemāḥ prajāḥ sadāyatanāḥ satpratiṣṭhitāḥ iti śruteḥ tasmistvaṃ pratiṣṭhito’si ityarthe ṭa tasmistvaṃ tattvam iti vā supāsulak iti vibhaktilopādinā kleśena vyākhyeyam| pradarśitānekārthasaṃbhavānayā vyāmohāpatterarthaviśeṣānirṇayāt| evaṃ śarīravācakānāṃ śarīriparyantatvamupetyaśarīriśarīrabhāvena(ṇa) sāmānādhikaraṇyābhiprāyeṇa tattvamasi vākyārthavarṇanamapi na svarasam| na hyatra tvaṃśabdasyeśvaraśarīrabhūtajīvavācakasyeśvare vṛttirabhyupeyate| tvaṃśabdārthasya tacchabdārthe viśeṣaṇatvāpabhāvāt| kiṃtu tacchabdasyaiva prakṛtaparāmarśakasya tvaśabdārthe jīve’bhedasaṃbandhena viśeṣaṇasamarpakatā | anyathā asi iti madhyamapuruṣānupapatteḥ| tvaṃ śabdārthasyetaraviśeṣaṇatva ākāṅkṣābhāvenākhyātasāmānādhikaraṇyāyogāt| yuṣmacchabdasyā khyātasāmānadhikaraṇya eva yuṣpadyupapade samānādhikaraṇe sthāninyapi madhyamaḥ iti sūtreṇa madhyamapuruṣavidhānāt| atastvaṃśabdārthasya viśeṣyatvamupeyam| tatra ca paramātmaparatacchabdasya vṛttiḥ śarīravācakānāṃ śarīriparyantatvābhyupagame’pi na saṃbhavati| īśvarasya jīvaṃ prati śarīratvābhāvāt| ato madhyamapuruṣopapattaye jīvaviśeṣyakameva sāmānādhikaraṇyamupeyam| dṛśyate ca tvamahaṃśabdārthayorviśeṣaṇaviśeṣyabhāvavyatyāse madhyamottamapuruṣayorvyatyāsaḥ -- tvaṃ vāhamasmi bhagavo devate’haṃ vai tvamasīti| evaṃ sadvidyāyāḥ prapañca mithyātvapratipādana eva svārasyaṃ mokṣadharmai śvetaketusuvarcalopākhyānarupopabṛṃhaṇena svaṣṭīkṛtaṃ sūtrakṛtā bhagavatā vyāsena| tathā hi tatropārakhyāyate -- devakanyā suvarcalā janmāntarasiddhasahajabrahmatattvānubhavāttādṛśaṃ patimicchīntī śvetaketu pitṛsamīpato labdhātmatattvabodhamupaśrutya taṃ vṛtavatī| pitrāpi tasmai niveditā| tābhyāṃ dampatībhyāṃ brahmaniṣṭhābhyāṃ vaidikakarmānuṣṭhānayuktaṃ gārhasthyamāśritya sthitamiti| tatra tayoritthaṃ brahmakathārupaḥ saṃvādaḥ prakṛtaḥ
tataḥ kadācidbhartāraṃ śvetaketu suvarcalā|
papraccha ko bhavānatra brūhi me tadvijottama||
tāmāha bhagavānvāgmī tvayā jñāto na saṃśayaḥ|
divajottamo’haṃ samage satyavrataparāyaṇaḥ||
sā tamāha mahātmānaṃ pṛcchāmi hṛdi śāyinam|
tacchrūtvā pratyuvācainā sarvajño munipagaṃvaḥ||
manasaiva samāyuktamātmānaṃ manyase yadi|
tanmithyā, gotrasadbhāve vartate dehabandhanaḥ||
tmamapyahamahaṃ sarvamahamityeva vartate|
nātra tatparamārthaṃ tadyanmā tvaṃ paripṛcchasi||
atra sadvidyāyām tattvamasi ityasakṛtpitropadiṣṭaḥ śvetaketuḥ kathāmātmānaṃ budhyata iti jijñāsamānayā suvarcalayā kastvaṃ dvijottam iti praśnaḥ kṛtaḥ| tataḥ praśnābhiprāyamavagacchatāpi śvetaketunā krameṇa tamarthaṃ sā bodhanīyeti matvā tatrottaramuktam -- dvijottameti saṃbandhayantyā tvayā brāhmaṇo’hamityavagatameva kimatra praṣṭavyamiti| tataḥ suvarcalayā dehopādhikatve nātmāni kalpitaṃ pratyakṣa brāh‌yaṇyaṃ na pṛcchāmi, kiṃtu dvijottamo’ham ityahaṃśabdena dehādviviktaṃ yamātmāna hṛdi śāyinaṃ nirdiśāsi taṃ pṛcchāmītyukte tatrottararamuktam -- ahaṃśabdo manaḥ -- śavalitātmasvarupe vartate| manaḥ śavalitātmasvarupaṃ ca mithyā, brāhmaṇakulotpanne dehopādhika ātmani brāhmaṇyamiva| ata ātmatattvajijñāsubhirnāhaṃśabdārthaḥ praṣṭavyaṃḥ, api cāhaṃbhāvaḥ sarvasādhāraṇaḥ pratyakṣasiddhaḥ | ataḥ paramārthavastujijñāsā cennāhaṃbhāvaḥ paśnārhaḥ| tadanantaraṃ kartṛtvabhoktṛtvādiyukkatamahaṃbhāvaṃ yadyanṛtamavagacchasi tarhi kartṛtvapūrvakaṃ vaidikaṃ kathamācarasīti pṛṣṭe lokasaṃgrahārthaṃ vaidikakarmācaraṇamityuktam| tadanantaraṃ sarvairātmani prayucyamānasyāhaṃśabdasya kathaṃ tadarthatā na syāditi manasi nidhāya śabdārthasaṃbandhapaśne kṛte na saṃyogadiḥ śabdārthayoḥ saṃbandhaḥ,api tu vācyavācakabhāva ityuktaranuktam| tadanantaraṃ dampatyoriyaṃ praśnettaraparamparā -- 
suvarcalā --
sadāhaṃkāraśabdo’yaṃ vyaktapātmani saṃśritaḥ|
na vācastatra vartanta iti mithyā bhaviṣyati||
śvetaketuḥ -- 
ahaṃśabdo hyahaṃbhāve nātmabhāve śubhaprabhe|
na vartante pare’cintye vācaḥ saguṇalakṣaṇāḥ||
suvarcalāḥ -- 
ahaṃbhāvo’tra ko nāma hyatmabhāvastathaiva ca |
tanme brūhi yathānyāyameva cenmunipuṃgava||
śvetaketuḥ -- 
mṛnmayo hi ghaṭābhāsastādṛgbhāva iheṣyate|
ahaṃbhāvaḥ pare’cintye hyatmabhāvo mahānparaḥ|
na vācastatra vartanta iti naiva virudhyate|
atra śabdārthayorvācyavācakabhāva saṃbandho’sti cetkathamahaṃ śabda ātmavācako na syāditi praśne kṛte’haṃśabdo nātmani mukhyayā vṛttyā vartate, kinvahamartha ityuttaramuktam| tataḥ ko nāmāhamarthādanya ātmeti pṛṣṭe mṛdi ghaṭābāsavadātmanyahaṃbhāvo’pyābhāsa ityahamarthātmanorvivekaṃ darśiyitvā kalpitākāro padhānena pravartamānaḥ śabdo na śuddhāghiṣṭhānaparyanta pravartate rajatopadhānena pravartamānaḥ śukto rajataśab iva| athaḥ kalipatopādhinimitto’haṃśabdo na śuddhātmani mukhyāyā vṛttyā pravartata iti na virudhyata ityupasaṃhṛtam| tato’haṃkārādvivikta ātmaiva prapaḍhcādhiṣṭhānaṃ brahyeti siddhavatkṛtya yathākāśasya prapañcāśrayatve’pi tadasaṅgitvameva brahmaṇo’pyasaṅgitve śvetaketunā pratipādite punarime praśnottare -- 
suvarcalāḥ -- 
nirvikāraṃ hyamūrtaṃ ca niraṃśaṃ sarvagaṃ tayā|
dṛśyate ca viyannanityaṃ tādṛgātmā na dṛśyate||
śvetaketuḥ -- 
tvacā spṛśati vai vāyumāphāśasthaṃ punaḥ punaḥ|
tatsthaṃ gandha tathāghrāti jyotiḥ paśyati cakṣuṣā||
tamo raśmigaṇaṃ caiva meghajālaṃ tathaiva ca|
varṣatārāgaṇa caiva nākāśaṃ dṛśyate punaḥ||
ākāśasyāpyathā kāśaṃ sadrūpamiti niścitam|
sadarthe kalpitaṃ sarvaṃ tatsatyaṃ viṣṇureva ca ||
yāni nāmāni gauṇāni hyupacārātparātmani|
na cakṣuṣā na manasā na cānyena puro vibhuḥ||
cintyate sūkṣmayā buddhyā vācā vaktu na śakyate|
etatprapañcamākhilaṃ tasminnaiva pratiṣṭhitam|| iti|
atrākāśadatprapañcādhiṣṭhānaṃ brahma kuto na dṛśyata ithi paśne ākāśasthasya vāyvādereva grahaṇamasti, na tu vivicyākāśasya| yadā laukikākāśa eveyaṃ gatistadā kaiva kathā paramātmani tato’pi sūkṣme| tataḥ sadarthe kalpita sarvam| sa(ta)cca sadrūpaṃ sarvādhiṣṭhānatvādvyāpakam| tatropādhimāśritya pravartamānāḥ śabdāḥ śuddhe sākṣānnāpravartanta ityuttaramuktam| asyāṃ praśnottarapamparāyā manmayo hi ghaṭābhāsa iti dṛṣṭāntoktyā vācā rambhaṇaśrutyarthaḥ, sadrūpamityātmaśabdena prakṛtasya jīvasya sadrūpatvoktyā tattvaṃmasi ithi vākyārtha ḥ, sadarthe kalpitaṃ hyetattatsarvaṃ viṣṇureva ca ityanena etedātmyamidaṃ sarvaṃ tatsatyaṃ sa ātmā iti śrutyarthaśca niṣprapañcapratyagabhinnabrahmāpratipattyanukūlo darśita iti| evaṃ coktopabṛṃhaṇena sadvidyāyā niṣprapañcatprayagabhinnabrahmāparatva eva svārasye siddhe tasya prapañcamithyātvaṃ vinānupapattyā tadantargatavācā rambhaṇamityādiśruteḥ prapañcamithyātvapratipādana eva svārasyamiti spaṣṭameva viduṣām| evam neha nānāsti kicanaṃ ityādivākyasyāpi prapañcamithyātvapratipādana eva svārasyam| atra hyadhyāropanyāyena mahāvākyīyapadārthaśodhakena iha ityanena jagadviśiṣṭabrahmopasthāpanāt| tatra ca nānāna parihṛśyamāne jagatkiṃcana kimapi| yadvā kenāpi prakāreṇa nāstīti dvaitasamānyābhāvarupavidheyasamarpaṇam| asati bādhaka uddeśyatāvacchedakakālādyavacchedyatvabhānasyoddeśavidheyabhāvenānvayasthale vyutpattisiddhatvāt| kālāntarāvacchedena dvaitābhāvabodhasya tarati śokamātmāvit vidvānnāmarupādvimuktaḥ jñātvā deva mucyate sarvapāśaiḥ ityādiśrutibhirjñānanāśyatvānumāpakadṛśyatvalihgādirupamānāntareṇa ca siddhatvena tadvodhane neha nānā itivākyavaiyarthyāpatteḥ| yadā yena saṃbandhena yena rupeṇa ya yatra yatsaṃpratipannaṃ tatkālalāvacchedena tatsabaṃndāvacchinnatadrūpāvacchinnatanniṣṭhābhāvapratiyogitvarupamithyātvabā(bo) dhakatāyā eva tasya svarasatvāt| iha brahmaṇi nānātvaṃ brahmāpratiyogikabhedo nāstīti paramatasiddhatadarthastuḥ na svarasaḥ| nānetstha nānātvaparatayā brahmaṇi jīvaghaṭādipratiyogikabedasattvena tanniṣedhasya kartumaśakyatayā bhedasāmānyani,edhāyogena nānātvamityasya bhedasāmānyasvarasasya brahmāpratiyogikabhedaviśeṣaparatayā ca kliṣṭārthatvāt| tāvatāpi kicanetyasyāsvārasyaṃ vaiyarthyaṃ ceti dik|
yadapi candrikāyāṃ parihṛtaduṣaṇottaram -- ekavijñānena sarvavijñānāyogācca| mṛtpiṇḍadṛṣṭāntānupapatteśca|
kataṃ hi brahmavijñānādvijñāt sakalaṃ bhavet|
na hi śuktisvarupasya vijñānādrajatajñatā||
pratyuta -- 
śakte statrāparijñānādyathaiva rajatajñatā|
brahamatattavāparijñānāttathā sarvajñatocitā||
iti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi mandam| vivartavāde’dhiṣṭhānajñānenāropitasya tatattvato jñātatvaṃ saṃpratipannam| taccādiṣṭhānātmaka svīyatāttvikasvarupakatvam| uktaṃ hi bhāmatyām -- rajjvāṃ jñātāyāṃ bhujaṃgatattvaṃ jñātaṃ bhavati| sā hi tasya tattvamiti| evaṃ ca śukti sākṣātkārānantaraṃ yadrajatabhātsā śuktiriti pratyayādrajate śuktikātmakajñātasvīyatāttvikasvarupakatvātmakaṃ śuktikājñānena tattvato jñātatvamakṣatameva | ekavijñānena sarvavijñānamapi tattvataḥ sarvavijñānameva vivakṣitam| evaṃ cādhiṣṭhānabhūte brahmaṇi jñāte idhaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā sarvaṃ khalvidaṃ brahma iti bādhāyāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyānurodhādbrarupajñātasvīyatāttvikasvarupakatvasya brahmaṇyāropite jagāti sattvānnaikavijñānena sarvivijñānānupapattiḥ, mṛdādivijñānena ghaṭādīnāiṃ vijñānaṃ (ta)svīyatāttvikasvarupakatvaṃ guḍajihvikānyāyenaivetyuktaṃ prāgita noktadṛṣṭantāsaṃgatiḥ|
yattu candrikāyām -- rajjvāṃ jñātāyāṃ bhujaṃgatattvaṃ jñātaṃ bhavati| sā hi tasya tatvam| evaṃ ca brahmajñānātsarva tattvato jñātaṃ bhavatīti bhāmatyuktamanūdya tanna, evaṃ hi brahmajñānena brahmaiva jñātaṃ bhavatītyarthaḥ syāt| taccāyuktam| svasya svahetutvāyogāt| yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavatyamataṃ matamavijñātaṃ vijñātaṃ syāt iti śrutegatāśrutādipadāyogācca| kicāsādhāraṇaṃ svarupaṃ dharmo vā tattvamityucyate| na tu bhramādiṣṭhānam| tathā satyānando brahmaṇaḥ, rajjutvaṃ ca rajjostattvaṃ na syāt| na ca rajjū rajjujñānavivartyasya sarpasya svarupaṃ dharmo vā| na ca paraspararabhinnānekāropyānugatamadiṣṭhānamakaikasyāṣaropitasyāsādhāraṇam| na cāropitamityetāvatā tato’nyasya tattvaṃ bhavati| svīkṛtaṃ ca tvayāpi sarpasya rajjuvyatirokeṇasattvena tato’nyatvābhāve’pi rajjoḥ sarpavyatirekeṇa sattvena tato’nyatvam| kiṃ ca rajjvāmayaṃ sarpa iti dhīrānande brahmadīriva sarpai sarpadhīriva ca pramā syāt| tattatve taddhītvāt| rajjūjñānena sarpabhramanivṛttitvadiṣṭhānatattvajñānasya tadajñānanimittakabhramavirodhitvāt| na ca ghaṭādermṛdādāvāropitattvāttaddṛṣṭānto yukta iti vācyam| mṛtattve jñāte’pi ghaṭādyanivṛtte| yadyapi tārkikamata iva ghaṭādikaṃ mṛdārabdhaṃ dravyāntaraṃ nāsti tathāpi kārakavyāpārasādhyo jalāharaṇādisamartho vyāvahāriko mṛdāditulyo’vasthāviśeṣarupo ghaṭādiśabdārthastvanmate’pyasti| tattvatastu na mṛdapyasti| kiṃca -- 
yadi mṛnmātravijñānādvijñātaṃ syādghaṭādikam|
mṛttvaṃ niścitya na punaḥ saṃśayaḥ syādghaṭādike||
etena yaduktaṃ bhāmatyām -- mṛjjñānena ghaṭādayastatvato jñātābhavanti| mṛdeva hi ghaṭādīnāṃ tattvamiti tannirastam| mṛttvajñāne’pi saṃsthānaviśeṣatvarupaghaṭatvājñānena ghaṭastattvato na jñāta iti vyavahārāt| ghaṭe śarāvatvādyāropānivṛteśceti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi mandam| brahmavijñānena sarvaṃ tattvato vijñātaṃ bhavatītyatra brahmavijñānenetyabhede tṛtīyā| abhedasya ca vijñātamityatra vijñāne’nvayaḥ| tathā ca brahmāvijñānābhinna yattattvato jñānaṃ tadviṣayasvīyatattvajñānābhinnajñānaviṣayakabrahmakaṃ sarvamiti paryavasitārthe na svasya svahetutvāpattirupadūṣaṇāvakāśaḥ| yenāśrutaṃ śrutaṃ bhavati ityādāvapyaśrutaṃ viyadādikaṃ yena tattvataḥ śrutaṃ bhavati yadabhinnaśrutātmakasvīyatāttvikasvarupakaṃ bhavatīti rītyā bādhāyāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyena paryavasitānvayena na śrutādipadāyogo’pīti|
yadapi -- vivartavāde mṛtpiṇḍamṛnmayādidṛṣṭāntānupapattiriti candrikāyāṃ dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapi śrīmadbhāṇyakārahṛdayānavabodhanivandhanam| tathā hi -- vācā rambhaṇaśabdavyākhyānabhāṣyasyāyamabhiprāyaḥ -- yenāśrutaṃ śrutam iti bādhāyāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyam| aśrutaṃ viyadādikaṃ sarvaṃ tattvato yadabhinnaśrutabrahmavyatiriktasvīyatāttvikasvarupaśūnyamityarthaḥ| tathā caikaṃ brahma sarvasyādhiṣṭhānamityarthaḥ pratijñātaḥ| tatra mṛtpiṇḍavyatirekeṇa tattva tasyadvikāro yathā nāstītyādidṛṣṭāntaḥ yathā somyaikena ityādinā pratipādyate| mṛpiṇḍapadaṃ ca mukhyārthakaikapadayogādbhamirupapiṇḍārthakam| piṇḍapadaṃ ca sthaulyaviśeṣabodhakaṃ mṛnmayamātropādānatāyogyatālābhāya| mṛnmayapadaṃ ca bhūmivikāramātrāthakam| tathā ca bhūmirupavākāraḥ sarvo’pi yathā bhūmi vinā tattvato nāstītyarthaḥ| bhūmivikāratvaṃ ca sākṣādeva vṛkṣādāvastīti bhūmervibhaktapiṇḍasya nānāvikārānupādānatve’pi na kṣatiḥ| yadyapi bhūbhirnādiṣṭhānaṃ tathāpyadhiṣṭhānatāvacchedakasuktyādivyātirekeṇa tattvato rupyādi nāstīti vyavahāravadbhūmivikārādisvarupavicāradaśāyāmādhāratāvacchedakabhūmyādivyatirekeṇa tattvatastadvikāro nāstīti vyavahārācchrūtyā tathoktam| evaṃ ca sarvaṃ mṛnmayekamṛtpiṇḍātmakavijñātasvīyatāttvikasvarupakamityapi bādhāyāṃ sāmānādhikaraṇyam| tatra hetuḥ mṛttiketyeva satyam iti| sarvapratyayavedye ca brahmarupe vyavasthite ityukteḥ| mṛttikā bhūmiriti hyagṛmāṇaṃ yattadavacchinnasadrūpaṃ brahma tadeva satyam| mṛttikāsvarupasya satyatvābhāvāt| tadavacchinnasadrapasya dṛṣṭantatvadhīdaśāyāṃ mṛdo vivicyājñānādbhūmitvena jñāyamānatvena sadrapalābhārthamemava mṛttiketyevetyatra iti ityukteḥ| anyathā tadvaiyarthyāpatteḥ| etena śabdaprakareṇa vyākaraṇādau prakaraṇasiddhaṃ śabdaparatvaṃ vihāya na veti vibhāṣā iti sūtrasthanavetiśabdasya svārthaparatvasyevārthaprakaraṇe’rthaparatvasya prakaraṇādeva lābhe śabdaparatāyā eva vaktavyatayā tasya ca prakṛte’nupayogāt mṛttiketyeva ityatretiśabdavaiyarthyameva tvanmata iti paroktadūṣaṇamanavakāśameva| ādyarthako vā prakṛta itiśabdaḥ| iti hetuprakāraṇaprakārādisamāptiṣu ityamarakośāt| tathā ca vikārasvarupasya vicāradaśāyāṃ tatsvarupamivabhūmisvarupasya vicāradaśāyāṃ sāpi mithyaiva| tatkāraṇabūtāpañcīkṛtabhūtānyeva satyāni| teṣāmapi svarupavicāradaśāyāṃ tānyapi mithyā| tatkāraṇaṃ bahyaiva satyamityādyarthalābhaḥ| eva mevottareṣvapi dṛṣṭānteṣu yojanīyam| mṛttiketyeva satyamityatra heturvācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyayamityādi| tacca vyākhyātaṃprāk| asminnevārthe -- 
ko bhavāniti nirdaiśo vācārambo hyanarthakaḥ|
śivikādārusaṃghāto vacanāsthi stha)ni saṃsthitaḥ||
anviṣyatāṃ nṛpaśreṣṭha tadbhedaṃ śibikā tvayā|
evaṃ cchatraśalākānāṃ pṛthagbhāvo vimṛśyatām||
kka yātaṃ chatramityeṣa nyāyastvāyi tathā mayi|
vastu rājeti yalloke yacca rājabhaṭātmakam||
tathānyacca nṛpetyetanna satsaṃkalpanāmayam|
evameva(ka) midaṃ viśva(brahma) na bhedi sakalaṃ jagat||
vāsudevābhidhānasya svarupaṃ paramātmanaḥ||
ityādiviṣṇupurāṇavākyasahastrairupabṛṃhaṇarupairanirvacanīyaparaparyāye prapañcimithyātve nirṇītatātparyakaṃ sadvidyāgatam yadagre rohitaṃ rupaṃ cejasastadrūpaṃ yacchuklaṃ tadapāṃ yatkṛṣṇaṃ tadannasyāpāgādagneragnitvaṃ vācārambhaṇaṃ vikāro nāmadheyaṃ trīṇi rūpāṇītyeva satyam ityādiśrutivākyaṃ pramāṇam| tatra hi trivṛtkṛdagnyādisvirupasyātrivṛtkṛtatejobannasvarupavyatirekeṇa vicārāsahatvamāgnitvamityantena hetukṛtya vācārambhaṇamityādinātri(vṛ)tkṛtamātraṃ mithyeti pratipāditam| parokte’rthe tu na kiṃcitpramāṇamasvārasyaṃ coktamityalamadhikena|
kiṃcāsādhāraṇasvarupaṃ dharmo vā tattvamityādi dūṣaṇamapi mandam| anāropitasyaiva tattvarupatvenoktadūṣaṇānavakāśāt| ata eva tattvajñānānniḥ śreyasādhigamaḥ iti bhāṣye tattvamanāropitasvarupamityudayanācāryairuktam| evaṃ ca vyavahāradaśāyāṃ śakt‌yādāvanāropitatvabhrameṇa rupyāditādātmyopalakṣitatvena ca rupyādeḥ svarupatattvaṃ śuktiriti loke vyavahāraḥ śuktyāderdharmatattvaṃ śuktitvādiriti ca vyavahāraḥ| vivekadaśāyāṃ tu brahmaivānāropitatvena jñāyata iti vastugatyā tadeva sarveṣā svarupatattvam| etadabhiprāyeṇaiva brahma tattvaṃ tapo vedaḥ ityamareṇoktam| tattvaṃ paramātmani vādyabhede svarupe ca iti haimantaśca | prapañcatattvavādinastava tattvavāditvaṃ nāsādhāraṇadharmavāditvam| kiṃtvanāropitaprapañcavāditvameveti tava mate’pyanāropitasvarupasyaiva tattvarupatāyāḥ saṃmatatvenāsādhāraṇasvarupaṃ dharmo vā tattvamityuktiḥ svamatavismaraṇanibandhanaiva | kicaṃ tasya bhāvastattvamiti vyutpannāttattvaśabdānna sādhāraṇadharmo’sādhāraṇadharmāntara vā pratīyate| ghaṭatvamityādiśabdāddhaṭapadajanyabodhaprakārībhūtajātisamaniyatadharmantarāpatīteḥ| ruḍhiśca pāramārthikavastunyeva sarvasaṃmatā| na tvasādhāraṇyamapi tatrāntarbhāvanīyam| evaṃ cānāropitatadīyasvarupameva tadīya svarupatattvamiti vivakṣitam| tadīyatvaṃ ca tanniṣṭhatādātmyapratiyogitvamityuktaṃ prāk| brahmānandayoḥ kālpanikabhedasattvena tādātmyasaṃbhavādānandasya brahyatattvarupatopapattiḥ| rajjutvaṃ ca rajjorna svarupatattvam, kiṃtu dharmatattvameveti na tathā satyānando brahmaṇaḥ , rajjutvaṃ ca rajjostatvaṃ na syāt iti dūṣaṇāvakāśaḥ| sarpabhinnasyāpyuktarītyā kālpanikābhedamādāya tadīyasvarupatattvarupatopapattiśca| rajjavāmayaṃ sarpabuddhiśca( sarpa iti buddhiśca) na pramā| abādhitārthaviṣayakatvābhāvāt| vyavahārakālīnabādhagocarārthaviṣayatvācca na vyāvahārikapramātvamapi| tadīyatattvaviśeṣyakatatprakārakajñānatvādikaṃ tatpramātvamiti tvayā vaktumaśakyam| tathā sati ghaṭe dravyatvamityādheyatāsaṃsargakaghaṭādiprakārakajñānānāṃ tava mate ghaṭādyaṃse pramātvabhāva prasaṅgāt| tadīyāsādhāraṇasvarupādereva tattattvarupatāyāstvayoktatvāt| dravyatvādergaṭādyasādhāraṇasvarupatvābhāvāt| adhiṣṭhānībhūtaṃ yattatvaṃ tajjñānasyaiva bhramanivartakatvāt| mṛttve jñāte’pi ghaṭādyanivṛttiripapannā| upapannā ca mṛdityādijñānādayaṃ ghaṭo na veti saṃśayānivṛttirapi | samānaviṣayakaniścayasyaiva saṃśayavirodhitvāt| mṛdādivijñānena ghaṭādīnāṃ vijñātasvīyatattvakatvaṃ guḍajihvikānyāyādinopapannamiti pratipāditaṃ prāgiti mṛjjñānena ghaṭādayastattvato jñātā bhavanti mṛdeva hi ghaṭādināṃ tattvam iti bhāmapyuktamapi sampageveti etena ityādinoktaṃ dūṣaṇamapi nirastam|
yadapi candrikāyām -- prakṛtyadikaraṇādṛśyatvādhikaraṇābhyāmasya punaruktiscādvaitimate | atrevobhayatrāpi brahmavivartatāyā evoktatvāditi dūṣaṇamuktam| tadatyantaṃ mandam| brahmaṇo nimittatve satyupādānatāyā vyavasthāpanameva prakṛtyādhikaraṇaprameyam| tādṛsopādānatāsvīkāre bhoktṛbhogyavibhāgalopāpattiriti pūrvapakṣe pariṇāmavādābhiprāyeṇa lokavaditi svapakṣabādhakoddhāramuktvā paramārthatastādṛśavibhāga eva nāstīti samādhānāyātra vivartoktāvapi tasyaitadadhikaraṇaprameyatvābhāvāt| adṛśyatvādhikaraṇe bhūtayonivkyasya pradhānaparatāṃ nirasya brahmaparatvavyavasthāpanameva prameyam| tadupapādanāya tatra vivartoktāvapi tasyetadadhikaraṇaprameyatvābhāvāt|
yadapi candrikāyām -- kicaṃ tvanmate pūrvasūtre bhedokteratra cābhedoktervirodhaḥ| na ca pramāṇayoḥ sūtrayorvirodha ekasyānṛta viṣayatvena parihartu yukta iti dūṣaṇamuktam| tadapyuktarītyā tadananyatvamityanena tādātmyaghaṭitamithyātvasyaivokterabhedānukteraprasaktameva | pramāṇayorapi sūtrayoḥ pramāṇamūrdhanyasāmānyaviśeṣaśāstrayoriva bhinnaviṣayatvasya yuktatvāt| pūrvasūtre lokavadityukterloke ca vikārarānṛtatvaprasiddhyabhāvātsatyaṃ bhoktṛbhogyavibhāgamāśrityaiva samādhānamuktamitibhramavāraṇāyaitatsūtrārambha iti abhyupagamya cemaṃ vyāvahārikaṃ bhoktṛbhogyavibhāgam iti bhāṇyābhiprāyasyāsmadbhāṣyavyākhyātṛbhira(bhi) hitatvācca|
yadapi candrikāyām -- kicaṃ ghaṭādermṛdananyatvapratipādaka ārambhaṇaśabdo jagato brahmananyatvarupe tvadīye sādhye na sākṣāddhetuḥ, mama tu heturiti dūpaṇam| tadapi mandam| tavamate’pi kevalārambhaṇaśabdasya hetupapādakatvābhāvena kisvidāsīdadhiṣṭhānamārambhaṇaṃ katamatsvitkathāsīt iti hyākṣepaḥ | yenādhiṣṭhānādinā bhūrmi dyāṃ ca janayañjanayitā viśeṣeṇa babhūva tadadhiṣṭhānaṃ kimāsīt| ārabhyate’nenetyārambhaṇaṃ sādhanaṃ vā kimāsīt| kathaṃ vāsīt| na kiṃcitkathamapīti sādhanādyākṣepaḥ kriyate| ākṣepasya viparyavasānāt| kiṃśabdasya praśnākṣepayoḥ samatvātpraśnārthe kiṃ na syādityata āha ādhiṣṭhānādyanukteriti | praśnaścetparihāro vaktavyaṃḥ| kiṃsviddhimāya bheṣajamiti praśne’grirhimasya bheṣajamitivat| atra parihārāyādhiṣṭhānādyanukternāyaṃ praśnaḥ| kiṃ tvākṣepa evaṃ iti tvadīyatattvaprakāśikādgranthaparyālocanayā svatantrasādhanasya tadananyatvarupatvadabhimatasādhye kevalārambhaṇaśabdasya hetupapādakatvābhāvena tadghaṭitavākyasaṃdarbhasyaiva hetutvasyābhipretatayā manmate’pi tadananyatvalamityatra tayoḥ kāryakāraṇayorananyatvaṃ tādātmyam tasmātkāraṇātkāryasya tattvato’nanyatvam ityarthadvayarupaniruvivakṣitasādhya ārambhaṇaśabdaghaṭitasaṃdarbhasya sadṛṣṭāntasya evaṃ saumya sa ādeśe bhavati iyuttara dārṣṭāntivākyarupasya sākṣādeva hatūpapādakatvāt|
yadapi -- vācārambhaṇaśabdasya samāsatayā samāsatayā vācārambaṇa śabdādibhyaḥ ityeva sūtraṇīyam | viśiṣṭapadagrahaṇaucityāt| padaikadeśagrahaṇaṃ tvanaucitamiti candrikokta dūṣaṇam| tadapi na sādhu| vācaiva kevalamastītyārabhyate ghaṭa śarāva udañcanaṃ ca iti bhāṣye vyastasyaiva vācetyasyopādānāt| na caiva sati kṛdyoge ṣaṣṭhīprasaktyā vāceti tṛtīyānupapattiriti vācyam| vācetyatra karaṇatṛtīyopagamāt| vāceti tṛtīyā ṣaṣṭhyartha iti cchendogyabhāṣyavivaraṇe bhinnapadatāyā uktatvācca| vāgārambhaṇamiti vāgālambanamityatat iti cchāndogyabhāṣyaṃ tu phalitārthakathanaparam| saṃdarbhaviśeṣātmakavākyaviśeṣasphuraṇāya samāsaukadeśopādāne’pi vādhakābhāvāt| ārambhaṇaśabdamātrasya matadvaye hetupapādakatvābhāvasyoktatvāt|5
yadapi candrikāyām -- tvanmate sautrādiśabdārthasya idaṃ sarvaṃ yadayamātmā ityāderapi śabdarupatvātsūtre vācārambhaṇādiśabdebhyaḥ ityeva vinyāsaḥ syāditi dūṣaṇam| tadapi mandam| ādiśabdādyuktibhiśceti tvadīyabhāṣye’pi yuktīnāmiva yathā ghaṭakarakādyuktibhiśceti tvadīyabhāṣye’pi yuktīnāmivayathā ghaṭakarakādyākāśānāiṃ mahākāśānanyatvaṃ yathā ca mṛgatṛṣṇikodakādīnāmūṣarādibhyo’nanyatvam| dṛṣṭanaṣṭasvarupatvātsvarupeṇānu pākhyatvāt| evamasya bhoktṛbhogyādiprapañcasya brahmavyatirekeṇābhāvaḥ ityasmadīyabhāṣyeṇa dṛśyatvaparicchinnatvādirupayuktīnamapyādiśabdārthatāperadarthanāyopanyastatvena śabdamātrasyaivādiśabdārthatvokterbhrāntimūlakatvāt| dṛṣṭanaṣṭasvarupatvāt ityanena śrutisiddhe prapañcamithyātve’numānamapi darśitam| vipratipannaṃ viyadādikaṃ mithyā, dṛśyatvānannāśapratiyogitvācca ityanumānadvayamatra vivakṣitamityasmadīyaistadbhāṣyavyākhyānācca|| 14||
bhāve copalabdheḥ|| 15||
atra -- sūtrātprāka kāraṇasya iti upalabdherityasya pūrvam kāryasya iti cādhyāhartavyam| bhāva ityasya bhāva evetyarthaḥ| caśabdena kāraṇopalabdhāvevopalabhyamānatvaṃ saṃmuccīyate| evaṃ ca kāraṇasattva eva sattvasya kāraṇopalabdhāvevopa labhyamānatvasamuccitasya kāraṇānanyatvarupasādhyahetutālābhaḥ| etādṛśārthadvayalābhāyaiva bhāve ca sattvāt ityanuktvā bhāve copalabdheḥ, ityaktam| tatra ca pābhayāmupalabhyamānāyāmevopalabyaghaṭītarupaviśeṣādeḥ prabhānyatvādvyabhicāravāraṇāya kaṇṭhoktasya vahnisattva eva sato dhūmaviśeṣasya vahnibhinnatvādvyabhicāravāraṇāya ca samuccitaviśeṣaṇasyopādānam| kāraṇasya bhāvaḥ sattā copalambhaśca iti ṭīkāyām iti dvayaṃ hetuviśeṣaṇakoṭau vivakṣitam iti śeṣaḥ| tathā ca ṭīkāgranthasya phalitārthakathanaparatvāt| bhāvaśabdopalabdhiśabdayoravṛttirvibhaktivipariṇāmḥ kāraṇasya kāryasya ceti padadvayādhyāhāraścetyanekadoṣāpātācca| mama tu uphalabdheḥ ityuttaram prasaṅgāt iti hetumātraṃ kalpyam iti candrikoktadūṣaṇānavakāśaḥ| kāraṇasya svatantrasādhanasya iti pūraṇamapekṣya laghu| upalabdherityasyopalabdhiprasaṅgādityarthakalpanā paraṃ parasyātiricyate| svatantrasādhane pramāṇairanupalabhyamānatāyāḥ svata eva siddhatvāttadabhidhānanārthakyādupalabdherityasyopalabdhiprasaṅgadityarthakatāyā āvaśyakatvāt| tathā hi parakīyavyākhyānam -- yadi bhagavatsṛṣṭau tadatiriktaṃ svatantrasādhanamasti tarhi vedādipramāṇaurupalabhyate| na copalabhyate | ato nāstītyartha iti|
yattu -- kāraṇasattva eva sattvarupahetutvasyānṛtatvarupasādhyavirodhiteti| tanna| hetuviśeṣaṇapraviṣṭasattāyā vidyamānatārupatvenānirvācyatvarupānṛtatvādivirodhitvāt| atra sūtre tādātmyarupānanyatvasyaiva sādhyatayā vivakṣitatvācca| uktaṃ cādvaitānandaśrīcaraṇaiḥ -- anena sūtreṇopādānopādeyabhāvaniyāmakatayā tādātmyakhyasvarupasaṃbandhaviśeṣa upapādyata iti|
yadapi -- śabdaikasamadhigamyanirviṣacinmātrarupasya śuddhasya sarvajñatvādiviśiṣṭasya saguṇasya vānupalambha eva| prapañcopalambhena sautro heturasiddha iti| tadapi na | tadupalabdhītyanena tattadīyopahitarupānyataraviṣayakopalabdereva vivakṣaṇīyatvāt| bhāvāccopalabdheḥ iti pāṭe bhāvastādātmyam| layablope pañcamī| bhāvaṃ viṣayīkṛtyopalabdheścetyarthaḥ| caśabdena pūrvoktānumānādisamuccayaḥ| pratyakṣopalabdhibhāvācca ityādibhāṣyadikaṃ ca phalitārthakathaparam| bhavati hi pratyakṣopalabdhi | kāryakāraṇayorananyatve ityagramabhāṣye ananyatve iti saptamīviṣayatvārthikā| tacca bhāṣyaṃ bhāvādiryasya vivaraṇarupamiti bodhyam| tenāsminpakṣe bhāvaśabdo vyartha iti śaṅkā nirastā| yattvasminpakṣe kāryakāraṇayorabhedasyaiva siddhyā kāryasya nānṛtatvasiddhiriti tatprāgeva nirastaprāyam|| 15||
sattvāccāvarasya || 16||
pūrvasūtre pratyakṣānumānābhyāṃ paṭādīnāṃ tantvādyabhinnatvaprasādhanena taddṛṣṭāntenopādeyatvahetunā viyadādeḥ prapañcasya brahmānanyatvam| sattvāccāvarasya kāraṇātmanaiva kāraṇe sattvamiti svīyasūkṣmarūpātmanā paramakāraṇabrahmatādātmyamityarthaḥ| kāryāvasthāpannaviyadādisūkṣmarupasya sattādātmyaśravaṇāditi niṣkarṣaṃḥ| kāryāvasthāpannaviyadāditādātmyaviśiṣṭasya sato’grakālasattvānupapattyedaṃśabdopasthāpyatāvacchedakakāryāvasthāyā upalakṣaṇatayaiva vākyārthānipraveśasyāvaśyakatvāt| na ca kāryāvasthāpannasyaiva viyadāderupalakṣaṇatvasaṃbhavātkāryāvasyāmātrasyopalakṣaṇatvoktirayukteti vācyam| viśiṣṭārthaparatvasyautsargikatayā viśeṣaṇāse tattyāgamātreṇāpavādakabādhopaśāntyupayuktāviśeṣyāṃśe’pi vinā nimittaṃ tattyāgasyānucitatvāditi bhāvaḥ| uktaśrutyarthasya vakṣyamāṇāyāṃ jagadupādānatāyāmupayogitā dyotayitumāha -- yacca yadātmaneti| eva ca śrutāvidaṃśabdopasaṃdānasya prayojanamupadarśita bhavati| nanvevamapi kāryāsthāpannasya jagataḥ sattādātmyasiddhamityāśaṅkyāha -- tasyātprāgutpatteriti| tathā ca kāryāvasthāpannaṃ viyadādi sattādātmyavat, svasamānakālīnasattādātmyavatsvīsayūkṣmarupakatvāt| yatsvasamānakālīnasattādātmyavatsūkṣmarupakaṃ tattādātmyavat| yathā ghaṭasamānakālīnamṛtsāmānyābhinnasvīyasūkṣmarupātmako ghaṭo mṛtsāmānyatādāmyavāniti sāmānyamukhavyāptyupaṣṭabhyānumānena kāryamātre paramakāraṇasattādātmyasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ|
prakārāntareṇa sūtraṃ vyākhyātumupakramate -- yathā ceti| triṣu kāleṣu sattvaṃ na vyabhicaratīti kasyāmapi sadaviṣayapratītiṃ na viṣaya ityarthaḥ| evamagre’pi | tathā ca viyadādikaṃ sattādātmyavat, sadaviṣayakapratītyaviṣayatvāt| yo yadaviṣayakapratītyāviṣayaḥ| sa tattādātmyavān, yathā saṃpratipanna ityanumānena jagadbrahmaṇostādātmyasiddhiriti bhāvaḥ| na ca bhāve copalabdheḥ ityanenāsya gatārthatā śaṅkyā| tatra tantupaṭādīnāṃ tādātmyaṃ prasādhya taddṛṣṭāntena brahmaṇo’pi jagattādātmyamupādānatvena prasādhitam| atra ca sākṣādeveti viśeṣāt| na copādānatvasya tādātmyaghaṭitatayā sādhyāniṣiṣṭatā| tatprāgabhāvavattvarupopādānatāyā eva tatra hetutvena vivakṣaṇāt| na caivamapi tulyanyā tena gatārthatā| paṭādau tantvādyaviṣayakapatītyaviṣayatvasyaiva jagati brahmāviṣayakapatītyaviṣayavāsiddheḥ| atra ca ghaṭaḥ sanpaṭaḥ sannityādyanugatapratīvanugatasyaikasyaiva sato viṣayatve lāghavamāśritya tasya samarthanīyatvāt| tādṛśakāghavaṃ ca nāprāmāṇikam| sadeva ityādiśrutyanugṛhītatvāt| pāramārthikatvena svasajātīyadvitīyatve hi brahmaṇa ekatvāvadhāraṇānupapatteḥ sphuṭatvāt| samabhivyāhṛtaviśeṣyatāvacchedakarupeṇa sajātīyāntaravyavacchedakatve saṃbhavati yojanāntarakleśasyānucitatvāt| bhavati ca pāramārthikatvamatiprasiddhaṃ satpadārthatāvacchedakamiti| sato bhāvaḥ sattvamiti vikkṣitaikatvasādhakavibhavaktyantena samāsāśrayaṇāduktārthasūcakatvaṃ sūtrasyopapadyate| etādṛśatarkasphoraṇānurodhena ca khalvidaṃ brahma ityādivākyamanudāhṛtyoktavākyodāharaṇam| aitena -- avarakālīnasya viyadādeḥ sattva tvadabhipretānṛtatvaviruddhamityavarasya sattvādityasaṃgatam| utpatteḥ prāgityadhyāhāragauravaṃ ca dvitīyasūtrasya tantupaṭādiviṣayaḥ| tṛtīyasya tu brahmaviyadādīti(ti) bhinnaviṣayatvena caśabdāsvārasyaṃ ca | tattejo’sṛjata iti vākyaśeṣeṇa sadeva ityādivākyasya kāraṇaparatvātkāryakāraṇabhedasya cādyāpyasiddhestena kāryasattvāsiddhiśceti parāstam| sattādātmyakālasaṃbandhādilakṣaṇasattvasyāsadvilakṣaṇatvādiniyatāyā manirvācyatāyāmanuguṇatvena tadvirodhitvāyogāt| atyantāsattvaṃ tu nātra prasādhyate| kāryasya sattādātmyaśravaṇādityukte viyadādeḥ kāryāvasthāpannasyoktavākyairuktatātādātmyaṃ prāguktarītyā bodhayitumaśakyamiti kathamevamuktamiti śaṅkāvyudāsāya pravṛtte bhāṣya utpatteḥ prāgityuktatve’pi sotravākyārthe’sadarthānupraveśavirahānnādhyāhāragauravam| dvitīyasūtretyādikaṃ cāpātata iti suvyaktameva|| 16||
asadvyapadeśānneti cenna dharmāntareṇa vākyaśeṣāt|| 17||
nanu kkacidasattavamapīti| ayaṃ bhāvaḥ -- asadevedam ityatra na samasta padam| asadāsīt ityuktau vyāhatyāpatteḥ| asāditi padadvayam| tathā ca idamātmakaṃ sannāsīt ityāpakkacicchrūyate | yadvā -- idaṃ jagadagre’sadavartamānamāsīdityarthaḥ| tathā ca śrutidvayaprāmāṇyanirvāhāya sadevaiti vākye idaṃmagre iti samastaṃ padam| idamiti sadviśeṣaṇaṃ vā| ātmā vā idam iti vākye’pi ayaṃ cāsāvekaśca ithi karmadhārayo vācyaḥ| evaṃ ca nānena vākyena jagadbrahmatādātmyāsiddhiriti |
nanvidamasagatam| tadasadeva sanmano’kuru iti vākyaśeṣāduktavākyasya kāraṇaparatvena kāryāsattvāprāpakatvāt| na ca kāryakāraṇayorananyatvāṅgīkāraeṇa kāryāsattvāśaṅketi yuktam| kāryasattvasyāpyananyatvasiddhyarthakatvenānanyatvāṅgīkāreṇāsattvaśaṅkānudayāditi cet| na | vākyaśeṣeṇa nirṇayasya siddhāntayuktitayā tadvirodhena pūrvapakṣānutthityabhidhānasya tvadviṣayakamahāmohaprasādaikaphalatvāt| tadasatsṛṣṭeḥ prākkāle’vartamānaṃ manaḥ sadbrahmakuruta iti yojanāmāśritya pūrvapakṣapravṛttisabhavācca|| 17||
yadyapyatra śabdāntarasya sadevetyādereva bhāṣya udāhṛtatvāt sattvāccāvarasya ityanenāsya paunaruktyāmivābhāti tathāpyavyahitapūrvasūtre pūrvapakṣiṇā tasyānyathā vyākhyātatvāttadupekṣāyāṃ tādṛśavākyārthe’pi kiṃcidbādhakaṃ nāstīti bhramaḥ syāttadvāraṇāya punaruktiḥ| tathā coktarītyāsadvetyādivākyatātparye varṇite noktaṃ śrutivyākhyānamucitam| tathā satīdamādipadavairthyāpatteparnirarthakalakṣaṇādisvīkārāpatteśceti pūrvoktaśrutyā tatsiddhiranapavādeti phaliṣyati| anyathā yuktiśabdena pūrvāparasarvayuktīnāmārambhaṇaśabdādītyatrādiśabdena vā sarvayuktigrahaṇasaṃbhavādetatsūtrasārthakyaṃ tadāpi durupapādaṃ syādeveti|| 17||
paṭavacca|| 19||
atra ca kārya kāraṇādbhidyate tasminniśrīyamānapi saṃdigdhatvāttato’dhikaparimāṇatvānmaśakādiva śaśakaḥ ityanumāne vyabhicārapadarśanāyādyam| tatraivala vilakṣaṇakāritvahetorvyābhicārapradarthanāya dvitīyamiti dṛṣṭāntadvayopadarśanaphalaṃ saṃpradāyavidbhiruktam| dṛṣṭānte sarvasāmyānapekṣaṇācca saṃveṣṭitapaṭaprāṇadṛṣṭāntau na vivartavādināmanuguṇau , jagato brahmaṇīva prasāraṇādeḥ paṭādāvanadhyastatvātsaṃveṣṭitaprasāritapaṭādāvica niravayave brahmaṇi saṃkocavikāsayorasaṃbhavācceti dūṣaṇamanavakāśam||
mīmāsānyāyapañcasyarāghavācāryasūriṇām|
śiṣyasya śāṃkare pādabhūṣaṇe paradūṣaṇe||
kṛtirvitanutāṃ modamasminnayavivecane||
śrīmacchaṃkararupaśaṃkaramahācāryairvicāryāhitaṃ
yacchārīrakabhāṣyamāryavinutaṃ doṣojjhitaṃ tatra yaiḥ|
doṣā ye pratipāditāḥ parihṛtā niḥ śeṣataste mayā
teṣāmeva ca dūṣitāḥ pratipadaṃ duryojanāyuktibhiḥ||
śārīrakasthaprathamadvitīyādhyāyādyapādadvayabhūṣaṇāvahaḥ|
grantho’yamanvarthakanāmadheyaḥ samāpitaḥ śaṃkarapādabhūṣaṇaḥ||
yeṣā śaṃkarasaṃjñapūrvajavarairojaḥ prasiddheḥ pūrā
labdhā lokamanojñapantasacivābhikhyā bhṛśaṃ śreyasī|
nīrātīrasamīpabhoganagaraṃ yadvāsabhūḥ saṃtataṃ
śrīkṛṣṇārcanatatparā raghuvarotsāhe’pi dattāśayāḥ||
nānāsāhebanāsnā ye prasiddhāstu tadāśrayāt|
siddhiṃ gataśca gantho’yamaṅikataśca śilākṣareḥ||
tatprāṅvivākaśca bhiḍeupāhvo madgranthasiddho paramāśrayo yaḥ|
gopāsalunaḥ saca viṣṇunāmā jīyācciraṃ śaivajanapravīṇaḥ||
ityārambhaṇādhikaraṇaṃ samāptam||
samāpto’yaṃ śāṃkarapādabhūṣaṇābhidho granthaḥ||
